# Conduit V0.2-C Tenants and Users Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Turn the single-tenant V0.1 platform into a usable multi-tenant one: a platform admin who manages tenants and can enter any tenant, tenant admins who manage their tenant's products, devices and users, read-only users, forced password changes, and disabled tenants that are cut off at login, at the MQTT edge and in the pipeline.

**Architecture:** Flyway `V3` makes `app_user.tenant_id` nullable (platform admins have no tenant) and adds role/status/code constraints. The JWT gains a nullable `tid`, an `imp` claim (the impersonating platform admin's user id) and an `mcp` claim (must change password); the access token's role is the *effective* role, so an impersonating platform admin carries `TENANT_ADMIN`. `SecurityConfig` enforces the spec §3.3 matrix by path and method, `PasswordChangeGateFilter` returns 403 `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED`, and `ImpersonationLogFilter` writes one INFO line per impersonated write. A `SessionService` issues tokens and re-reads users and tenants on refresh. New packages: `user` (tenant user management) and `platform` (cross-tenant overview, tenant lifecycle, enter). Device identity now carries the tenant status, so MQTT auth denies and the pipeline dead-letters `TENANT_DISABLED`. The frontend gets typed API modules, a richer auth store (impersonation, forced password change), `usePermission`, role-aware routing and menus, the impersonation bar, the forced-password dialog, the platform home and tenant management pages, the user management page, personal settings, and `canWrite` gating on every existing write button.

**Tech Stack:** Java 21, Spring Boot 3.5.6, Spring Security 6, MyBatis-Plus 3.5.12, Flyway, jjwt, PostgreSQL 16, Redis 7.4, Testcontainers; Vue 3.5.42 + TypeScript + Element Plus 2.11.1 + `@element-plus/icons-vue` 2.3.1 + Pinia 2.3.1 + vue-router 4.5.0 + Axios 1.20.0 + Vitest + @vue/test-utils.

**Governing spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2c-tenants-users-design.md` (behavior); repo-root `CLAUDE.md` (conventions, comment rules, maintenance rule).

## Global Constraints

**Operator-provided placeholders** (set by the operator in their shell; never write their real values into any committed file):

| Placeholder | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `$JAVA21_HOME` | Path of a JDK 21 installation |
| `<docker-host>` | Address of the (remote) Docker host that runs Testcontainers and the infrastructure Compose stack |
| `$ADMIN_PASSWORD` | Password of the default tenant's `admin` (`CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD`) — Task 9 acceptance only |
| `$SYSADMIN_PASSWORD` | Password of the platform admin `sysadmin` (`CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD`) — Task 9 acceptance only |
| `$CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN` | The EMQX callback token (`CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN`) — Task 9 acceptance only |

**Shell preamble**: run once per shell before any Maven or Docker command in this plan. All commands run from the repository (or worktree) root unless a step says otherwise.

```bash
export JAVA_HOME="$JAVA21_HOME" PATH="$JAVA21_HOME/bin:$PATH"
export DOCKER_HOST="tcp://<docker-host>:2375" TESTCONTAINERS_HOST_OVERRIDE="<docker-host>"
```

**Command patterns** (the tasks use them verbatim):

- Unit test classes: `mvn -B -q -pl conduit-server -am test -Dtest=<Class>[,<Class>] -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Integration test classes: `mvn -B -pl conduit-server -am verify -Dtest=NONE -Dit.test=<Class>[,<Class>] -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false -Dfailsafe.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Full backend build (Task 9 only): `mvn -B verify`
- Frontend specs: `cd conduit-ui && npx vitest run <paths under conduit-ui> ; cd ..`
- Frontend type check: `cd conduit-ui && npm run typecheck ; cd ..`
- Full frontend (Task 9 only): `cd conduit-ui && npm test && npm run build ; cd ..`

**Execution model (speed rule):**

- **Two parallel tracks in separate git worktrees.** Backend Tasks 1–5 run in order in one worktree (branch `v0.2c-backend`); frontend Tasks 6–8 run in order in another (branch `v0.2c-frontend`). The tracks touch disjoint directories (`conduit-server/` vs `conduit-ui/`), so they merge without conflicts. The frontend codes against the API contract of spec §4; Task 6 Step 1 pins every request and response type, and the backend DTOs in Tasks 2–4 produce exactly that JSON.
- **Task 9 runs on `main` after both branches are merged.** It is the only task that runs the full `mvn -B verify`, the full `npm test && npm run build`, and the acceptance checks against the locally running stack.
- **Tests per task:** each task runs only the test classes and spec files it names.
- **Model split:** implementation subagents use a cheaper model (Sonnet); reviews use the main model. The code in this plan is complete: implementers copy it, they do not design. If something in a step does not compile or a named test fails for a reason the step does not explain, stop and report instead of improvising.

**Project rules:**

- **Code comments (owner rule, applies to every code block in this plan):** (1) every class, interface, record and enum, every TS module and every Vue component gets a Chinese header comment saying what it is for: Javadoc on the Java type, a top-of-file comment in TS, and a comment at the top of `<script setup>` in Vue; (2) every key method or function gets a Chinese Javadoc/JSDoc stating what it does, its important parameters and return value, and any non-obvious behavior (exceptions, edge cases, side effects). "Key" means public/service/controller methods, pure policy functions and anything with branching logic. Trivial getters and one-line helpers are exempt. Test classes and spec files get a header comment too. Comments say "what and why", never restate the code, and are updated together with the code (CLAUDE.md §2.1). Do not edit `CLAUDE.md` except in Task 9, which its maintenance rule requires.
- **Git:** Conventional Commits. This repo commits with the author name only and an empty email: never run `git config user.email` and never pass an email. Commits and PRs carry no AI attribution lines (no `Co-Authored-By`, no "Generated with").
- **No secrets in tracked files:** no IP addresses, host names, local Windows paths, user names of real people, real passwords or tokens. Docs use `<docker-host>`. Local config (`deploy/.env`, `application-local.yml`, `simulator-local.properties`) stays untracked. The test passwords in `application-test.yml` and in test classes are test values only.
- **No deploy steps.** Nothing in this plan runs `deploy/build-local.sh` or touches the Docker host's application stack (CLAUDE.md §3). Acceptance is tests plus API checks against the stack the owner runs from the IDE (`Conduit (local all)`).
- **Versions (exact):** Java 21, Spring Boot 3.5.6; Element Plus `2.11.1`, Vue `3.5.42`, vue-router `4.5.0`, Pinia `2.3.1`, Axios `1.20.0`. This plan adds **no** Maven or npm dependency (`OutputCaptureExtension` and `ArgumentCaptor` ship with `spring-boot-starter-test`; Flyway is already a dependency).
- **Tests:** `*Test` = unit (surefire), `*IT` = integration (failsafe, Testcontainers). Server integration tests extend `IntegrationTestBase` and share one database and one Redis, so tests that assert exact counts create their own tenants and unique names (`unique(...)`). Frontend specs use Vitest + `@vue/test-utils`, mock `@/api/*` modules, and use `createTestRouter`/`createTestPinia`/`settle` from `src/test/viewTestUtils.ts`.
- **Errors:** errors are `ProblemDetail`; throw `ApiException` (400 parameters, 401 not signed in / account unusable, 403 permission, 404 not found, 409 conflict). Do not add class-level `@Validated` to controllers. "Redis unavailable" means `org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException`.
- **Logging (CLAUDE.md §2.2):** SLF4J parameterized; never log passwords, password hashes, JWTs, refresh cookies, the internal token or `Authorization`. Management actions (tenant create/disable/enable/delete, user create/delete/role change/password reset, enter/exit tenant, impersonated writes) log one INFO line; login results and access-path detail are DEBUG.
- **UI conventions (CLAUDE.md §2.2):** all copy is Chinese; paged tables default to 10 rows with sizes 10/20/50/100 (`DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE`, `PAGE_SIZES`) through `useTable`; pages start with `PageHeader`; list filters use `FilterBar`; roles and statuses are `el-tag`; online state uses `OnlineDot`; the empty state is `el-empty` with a guide button; first load shows a skeleton; styles use only CSS variables (`--el-*` or `--cd-*`), never literal colors, and must be readable in dark mode; every new page is registered in `ROUTE_META`. Demo and test data use meaningful Chinese names (「验收租户（华东）」, 「华东1号楼 电表01」) and tests clean up what they create where the shared test database would otherwise be polluted beyond what other ITs already do.
- **Spec values copied verbatim:**
  - Roles: `PLATFORM_ADMIN` (平台管理员, belongs to no tenant), `TENANT_ADMIN` (租户管理员), `TENANT_VIEWER` (只读用户).
  - Username: 「3～32 位，字母开头，只能含字母、数字、`_`、`-`、`.`；全平台唯一，重复 409。登录不需要选择租户。」 → regex `^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{2,31}$`.
  - Password: 「至少 8 位，同时含字母和数字；BCrypt。」 → 8–64 characters (64 is this plan's cap, see Task 1 decisions), at least one ASCII letter and one digit.
  - Tenant code: `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$` (2～32 位), globally unique, immutable; default tenant code `default`, which cannot be disabled, enabled or deleted (409).
  - User status `ACTIVE` / `DISABLED`; tenant status `ACTIVE` / `DISABLED`.
  - Env `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_USERNAME` (default `sysadmin`) and `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD`; empty password → no platform admin, one WARN, startup continues.
  - Error code `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED` (ProblemDetail property `code`, status 403). Allowed while the flag is set: `PUT /api/v1/me/password`, `GET /api/v1/auth/me`, logout (and the public login/refresh).
  - JWT: access 15 min, refresh 7 days in an HttpOnly cookie; claims user id (`sub`), `tid` (absent for platform admins), `role` (effective), `typ`, plus `imp` when impersonating.
  - Enter-tenant confirm text: 「将以租户管理员权限操作租户 XX 的数据，所有修改都会被记录」. Impersonation bar: 「正在以平台管理员身份操作租户：XX」 with 「退出」.
  - Every paged list defaults to 10 per page; `topTenants` = the 5 tenants with the most devices.

**Facts about the existing code this plan relies on** (verified against the repository at `875d6fd`):

| Fact | Where |
|---|---|
| `app_user.tenant_id` is `not null references tenant (id)`; PostgreSQL names that FK `app_user_tenant_id_fkey` | `V1__init.sql` |
| `tenant` and `app_user` are not tenant-filtered; every other table is, and an unbound `TenantContext` makes filtered SQL throw | `MybatisPlusConfig.TENANT_FREE_TABLES`, `TenantContext.require()` |
| `JwtAuthFilter` trusts any valid access token, grants `ROLE_<role>` and binds its tenant; it does not look the user up | `auth/JwtAuthFilter` |
| `SecurityConfig` today only distinguishes public vs authenticated | `auth/SecurityConfig` |
| `AdminBootstrap` creates tenant `default` (name `Default`) and user `admin` with role `ADMIN` | `bootstrap/AdminBootstrap` |
| `OverviewApiIT` and `JwtServiceTest` build `AuthPrincipal(…, "ADMIN")` directly; `AuthIT` and `AdminBootstrapIT` assert role `ADMIN` | tests |
| `DeviceIdentity(deviceKey, tenantId, status, secretHash, secretSalt, productKey)` is built by `DeviceMapper.findIdentity` (records map by constructor argument name; the Boot parent compiles with `-parameters`) and cached as JSON in `conduit:auth:{deviceKey}` for 5 minutes | `device/DeviceIdentity*`, `DeviceMapper` |
| Tests construct `DeviceIdentity` in `DeviceApiIT` (3×), `DeviceIdentityLookupIT` (2×), `PipelineProcessorTest` (1×), `TenantIsolationIT` (1×) | tests |
| The simulator logs in as `admin` and creates products/devices through the REST API; `admin` stays `TENANT_ADMIN` with `must_change_password = false`, so it keeps working unchanged. The e2e module drives the simulator | `ConduitApiClient`, `NoDataLossE2EIT` |
| The frontend `http.ts` skips the token for every `/api/v1/auth/` URL; `exit-tenant` and `me` need it, so the rule narrows to login/refresh/logout | `api/http.ts` |
| View specs for products and devices mount without Pinia; `usePermission` needs it, so they switch to `createTestPinia()` | `views/*.spec.ts` |

## Task 1: Backend schema V3, entities, roles, account policies and the platform admin bootstrap

Adds Flyway `V3`, the new entity fields, role constants, the username/password/display-name/tenant-code rules, and creates the platform admin from `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_*` at startup.

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__tenants_users.sql`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/Roles.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AppUser.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/Tenant.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/UsernamePolicy.java`, `PasswordPolicy.java`, `DisplayNamePolicy.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TenantCodePolicy.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/bootstrap/BootstrapProperties.java`, `AdminBootstrap.java` (full replacements)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/resources/application.yml`, `conduit-server/src/test/resources/application-test.yml`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/support/IntegrationTestBase.java` (`TEST_PASSWORD`, `createUser`)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AccountPoliciesTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/bootstrap/AdminBootstrapTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/SchemaV3IT.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/bootstrap/AdminBootstrapIT.java` (full replacement)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AuthIT.java` (one assertion)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ApiException.badRequest(String)`, `AppUserMapper`, `TenantMapper` (MyBatis-Plus `BaseMapper`), `PasswordEncoder` (BCrypt), `IntegrationTestBase` (`jdbc`, `unique`, `createTenant`).
- Produces (later tasks rely on these exact names):
  - `Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN`, `Roles.TENANT_ADMIN`, `Roles.TENANT_VIEWER` (`String`), `Roles.isTenantRole(String) → boolean`.
  - `AppUser` fields `id, tenantId (Long, null for platform admins), username, displayName, passwordHash, role, status, mustChangePassword (Boolean), createdAt, updatedAt`; constants `AppUser.ACTIVE = "ACTIVE"`, `AppUser.DISABLED = "DISABLED"`.
  - `Tenant` fields `id, code, name, status, createdAt, updatedAt`; constants `Tenant.ACTIVE`, `Tenant.DISABLED`, `Tenant.DEFAULT_CODE = "default"`.
  - `UsernamePolicy.requireValid(String) → String`, `PasswordPolicy.requireValid(String) → String`, `DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(String) → String` (null for blank), `TenantCodePolicy.requireValid(String) → String`; all throw `ApiException` 400.
  - `BootstrapProperties(adminUsername, adminPassword, platformAdminUsername, platformAdminPassword)`; `AdminBootstrap.ensurePlatformAdmin()` (package-private).
  - Test config: platform admin `sysadmin` / `sysadmin-pass-123`.
  - `IntegrationTestBase.TEST_PASSWORD = "Test-pass-123"`, `IntegrationTestBase.createUser(long tenantId, String role, boolean mustChangePassword) → String username`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- The password cap is **64** characters (BCrypt only uses the first 72 bytes; 64 keeps typical input inside it). Letters are ASCII letters, digits are `0–9`.
- Display names are trimmed; blank becomes `null` (the UI then shows the username); more than 64 characters → 400 (column is `varchar(64)`).
- The tenant-code rule is a database constraint as well as an API rule. The only existing code, `default`, satisfies it.
- The FK `app_user → tenant` becomes `on delete cascade`; Task 4 still deletes the users explicitly in the same transaction (both options of spec §2.2).
- The bootstrap does not apply the password policy to the environment passwords (an operator-chosen value must not stop startup); the platform admin is created with `must_change_password = false`.
- If the configured platform-admin username already belongs to a tenant user, the bootstrap logs a WARN and creates nothing instead of failing startup.
- Until Task 2 lands, logging in as `sysadmin` fails (the old `AuthPrincipal` cannot hold a null tenant). No Task 1 test logs in as `sysadmin`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing policy unit test**

Create `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AccountPoliciesTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantCodePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import org.assertj.core.api.ThrowableAssert.ThrowingCallable;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

/** 账号规则单元测试：用户名、密码、显示名称与租户编码的合法与非法取值（非法一律 400），以及租户角色判断。 */
class AccountPoliciesTest {

    private static void assertBadRequest(ThrowingCallable call) {
        assertThatThrownBy(call).isInstanceOfSatisfying(ApiException.class,
                e -> assertThat(e.status()).isEqualTo(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST));
    }

    @Test
    void usernames() {
        for (String ok : List.of("abc", "Admin_01", "zhang.san", "a-b", "a".repeat(32))) {
            assertThat(UsernamePolicy.requireValid(ok)).isEqualTo(ok);
        }
        for (String bad : Arrays.asList(null, "", "ab", "1abc", "_abc", "abc def", "张三丰", "a".repeat(33))) {
            assertBadRequest(() -> UsernamePolicy.requireValid(bad));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void passwords() {
        for (String ok : List.of("abcdefg1", "Passw0rd!", "1234567a", "a".repeat(63) + "1")) {
            assertThat(PasswordPolicy.requireValid(ok)).isEqualTo(ok);
        }
        for (String bad : Arrays.asList(null, "", "abc1234", "abcdefgh", "12345678", "a".repeat(64) + "1")) {
            assertBadRequest(() -> PasswordPolicy.requireValid(bad));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void displayNames() {
        assertThat(DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(null)).isNull();
        assertThat(DisplayNamePolicy.normalize("   ")).isNull();
        assertThat(DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(" 华东运维 张工 ")).isEqualTo("华东运维 张工");
        assertThat(DisplayNamePolicy.normalize("名".repeat(64))).hasSize(64);
        assertBadRequest(() -> DisplayNamePolicy.normalize("名".repeat(65)));
    }

    @Test
    void tenantCodes() {
        for (String ok : List.of("default", "ab", "east-1", "a" + "b".repeat(31))) {
            assertThat(TenantCodePolicy.requireValid(ok)).isEqualTo(ok);
        }
        for (String bad : Arrays.asList(null, "", "a", "1abc", "Abc", "a_b", "-ab", "a".repeat(33), "华东")) {
            assertBadRequest(() -> TenantCodePolicy.requireValid(bad));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void tenantRoles() {
        assertThat(Roles.isTenantRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)).isTrue();
        assertThat(Roles.isTenantRole(Roles.TENANT_VIEWER)).isTrue();
        assertThat(Roles.isTenantRole(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN)).isFalse();
        assertThat(Roles.isTenantRole(null)).isFalse();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the roles and the policies**

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/Roles.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 角色常量（app_user.role 与 JWT 的 role 声明）。平台管理员不属于任何租户；租户管理员可读写本租户并管理用户；只读用户只能查看。
 * 平台管理员进入租户后，令牌中的有效角色是 TENANT_ADMIN。
 */
public final class Roles {

    public static final String PLATFORM_ADMIN = "PLATFORM_ADMIN";
    public static final String TENANT_ADMIN = "TENANT_ADMIN";
    public static final String TENANT_VIEWER = "TENANT_VIEWER";

    /** 租户内用户可以使用的角色。 */
    public static final Set<String> TENANT_ROLES = Set.of(TENANT_ADMIN, TENANT_VIEWER);

    private Roles() {
    }

    /** 是否为租户内角色（TENANT_ADMIN / TENANT_VIEWER）；null 返回 false。 */
    public static boolean isTenantRole(String role) {
        return role != null && TENANT_ROLES.contains(role);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;

import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** 用户名规则：3～32 位，字母开头，只能含字母、数字、_、-、.。全平台唯一由数据库唯一键与服务层检查保证。 */
public final class UsernamePolicy {

    static final Pattern PATTERN = Pattern.compile("^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{2,31}$");

    private UsernamePolicy() {
    }

    /**
     * 校验用户名。
     *
     * @return 原样返回合法的用户名
     * @throws ApiException 400：为 null 或不符合规则
     */
    public static String requireValid(String username) {
        if (username == null || !PATTERN.matcher(username).matches()) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("用户名须为 3～32 位，字母开头，只能包含字母、数字、_、-、.");
        }
        return username;
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/PasswordPolicy.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;

/**
 * 密码规则：8～64 位，同时包含（ASCII）字母和数字。上限 64 让常见输入落在 BCrypt 只取前 72 字节的范围内。
 * 只用于通过接口设置的新密码；启动时由环境变量给出的初始密码不受此限制。
 */
public final class PasswordPolicy {

    static final int MIN_LENGTH = 8;
    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 64;

    private PasswordPolicy() {
    }

    /**
     * 校验新密码。
     *
     * @return 原样返回合法的密码
     * @throws ApiException 400：为 null、长度不在 8～64、缺少字母或缺少数字
     */
    public static String requireValid(String password) {
        if (password == null || password.length() < MIN_LENGTH || password.length() > MAX_LENGTH
                || password.chars().noneMatch(c -> (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z'))
                || password.chars().noneMatch(c -> c >= '0' && c <= '9')) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字");
        }
        return password;
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/DisplayNamePolicy.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;

/** 显示名称规则：去掉首尾空白，空白视为未设置（界面显示用户名），最多 64 个字符。 */
public final class DisplayNamePolicy {

    static final int MAX_LENGTH = 64;

    private DisplayNamePolicy() {
    }

    /**
     * 规范化显示名称。
     *
     * @return 去掉首尾空白后的名称；null 或空白返回 null
     * @throws ApiException 400：超过 64 个字符
     */
    public static String normalize(String displayName) {
        if (displayName == null || displayName.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        String trimmed = displayName.trim();
        if (trimmed.length() > MAX_LENGTH) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("显示名称最多 64 个字符");
        }
        return trimmed;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.tenant;

import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;

import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** 租户编码规则：^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$（2～32 位，小写字母开头），全局唯一，创建后不可修改。数据库也有同样的约束。 */
public final class TenantCodePolicy {

    static final Pattern PATTERN = Pattern.compile("^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$");

    private TenantCodePolicy() {
    }

    /**
     * 校验租户编码。
     *
     * @return 原样返回合法的编码
     * @throws ApiException 400：为 null 或不符合规则
     */
    public static String requireValid(String code) {
        if (code == null || !PATTERN.matcher(code).matches()) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("租户编码须为 2～32 位，小写字母开头，只能包含小写字母、数字和 -");
        }
        return code;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.tenant;

import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.flywaydb.core.Flyway;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;

import javax.sql.DataSource;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

/**
 * V3 迁移集成测试：旧的 ADMIN 角色迁移为 TENANT_ADMIN（在独立 schema 中从 V2 迁移上来，测试后删除该 schema）；
 * 平台管理员没有租户、租户用户必须有租户；角色、状态、租户编码约束；新列默认值；删除租户时其用户随之删除。
 */
class SchemaV3IT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    @Autowired
    DataSource dataSource;

    @Test
    void migratesTheLegacyAdminRoleToTenantAdmin() {
        String schema = "mig_v3_" + unique("s");
        try {
            Flyway.configure().dataSource(dataSource).schemas(schema).target("2").load().migrate();
            jdbc.update("insert into " + schema + ".tenant (code, name) values ('legacy', '旧租户')");
            jdbc.update("insert into " + schema + ".app_user (tenant_id, username, password_hash, role) "
                    + "select id, 'legacy-admin', 'h', 'ADMIN' from " + schema + ".tenant where code = 'legacy'");

            Flyway.configure().dataSource(dataSource).schemas(schema).load().migrate();

            Map<String, Object> admin = jdbc.queryForMap("select role, must_change_password, display_name from "
                    + schema + ".app_user where username = 'legacy-admin'");
            assertThat(admin.get("role")).isEqualTo("TENANT_ADMIN");
            assertThat(admin.get("must_change_password")).isEqualTo(false);
            assertThat(admin.get("display_name")).isNull();
        } finally {
            jdbc.execute("drop schema if exists " + schema + " cascade");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void platformAdminsHaveNoTenantAndTenantUsersHaveOne() {
        long tenant = createTenant("sv3-");

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into app_user (tenant_id, username, password_hash, role) "
                + "values (?, ?, 'h', 'PLATFORM_ADMIN')", tenant, unique("u")))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into app_user (username, password_hash, role) "
                + "values (?, 'h', 'TENANT_ADMIN')", unique("u")))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsUnknownRolesAndStatuses() {
        long tenant = createTenant("sv3-");

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into app_user (tenant_id, username, password_hash, role) "
                + "values (?, ?, 'h', 'ADMIN')", tenant, unique("u")))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into app_user (tenant_id, username, password_hash, role, status) "
                + "values (?, ?, 'h', 'TENANT_VIEWER', 'LOCKED')", tenant, unique("u")))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("update tenant set status = 'PAUSED' where id = ?", tenant))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidTenantCodes() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into tenant (code, name) values ('Bad_Code', '非法编码')"))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbc.update("insert into tenant (code, name) values ('x', '太短')"))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void newColumnsHaveDefaults() {
        long tenant = createTenant("sv3-");
        String username = unique("u");
        jdbc.update("insert into app_user (tenant_id, username, password_hash, role) values (?, ?, 'h', 'TENANT_VIEWER')",
                tenant, username);

        Map<String, Object> user = jdbc.queryForMap(
                "select display_name, must_change_password, updated_at from app_user where username = ?", username);
        assertThat(user.get("display_name")).isNull();
        assertThat(user.get("must_change_password")).isEqualTo(false);
        assertThat(user.get("updated_at")).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test
    void deletingATenantDeletesItsUsers() {
        long tenant = createTenant("sv3-");
        String username = createUser(tenant, "TENANT_ADMIN", true);

        jdbc.update("delete from tenant where id = ?", tenant);

        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from app_user where username = ?", Integer.class, username))
                .isZero();
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.bootstrap;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/** AdminBootstrap.ensurePlatformAdmin 单元测试：没有平台管理员时按配置创建；密码为空只警告不创建；已有平台管理员时不改动。 */
@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
class AdminBootstrapTest {

    private final TenantMapper tenants = mock(TenantMapper.class);
    private final AppUserMapper users = mock(AppUserMapper.class);
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder = mock(PasswordEncoder.class);

    private AdminBootstrap bootstrap(String platformPassword) {
        return new AdminBootstrap(new BootstrapProperties("admin", "admin-pass-123", "sysadmin", platformPassword),
                tenants, users, encoder);
    }

    @Test
    void createsThePlatformAdminWhenNoneExists() {
        when(users.exists(any())).thenReturn(false);
        when(encoder.encode("Platform-pass-1")).thenReturn("hashed");

        bootstrap("Platform-pass-1").ensurePlatformAdmin();

        ArgumentCaptor<AppUser> saved = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AppUser.class);
        verify(users).insert(saved.capture());
        AppUser admin = saved.getValue();
        assertThat(admin.getTenantId()).isNull();
        assertThat(admin.getUsername()).isEqualTo("sysadmin");
        assertThat(admin.getRole()).isEqualTo(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        assertThat(admin.getPasswordHash()).isEqualTo("hashed");
        assertThat(admin.getStatus()).isEqualTo(AppUser.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(admin.getMustChangePassword()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void onlyWarnsWhenThePasswordIsEmpty(CapturedOutput output) {
        when(users.exists(any())).thenReturn(false);

        bootstrap("  ").ensurePlatformAdmin();

        verify(users, never()).insert(any(AppUser.class));
        assertThat(output).contains("CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD");
    }

    @Test
    void keepsAnExistingPlatformAdmin() {
        when(users.exists(any())).thenReturn(true);

        bootstrap("Platform-pass-1").ensurePlatformAdmin();

        verify(users, never()).insert(any(AppUser.class));
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.bootstrap;

import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/** 启动初始化集成测试：默认租户与租户管理员 admin、按配置创建的平台管理员 sysadmin（无租户），以及重复执行的幂等性。 */
class AdminBootstrapIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    @Autowired
    AdminBootstrap bootstrap;

    @Autowired
    PasswordEncoder encoder;

    @Test
    void createsDefaultTenantAndAdminOnStartup() {
        Long tenantId = jdbc.queryForObject("select id from tenant where code = 'default'", Long.class);
        var admin = jdbc.queryForMap("select * from app_user where username = 'admin'");

        assertThat(admin.get("tenant_id")).isEqualTo(tenantId);
        assertThat(admin.get("role")).isEqualTo("TENANT_ADMIN");
        assertThat(admin.get("must_change_password")).isEqualTo(false);
        assertThat(encoder.matches("admin-pass-123", (String) admin.get("password_hash"))).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void createsThePlatformAdminFromSettings() {
        var sysadmin = jdbc.queryForMap("select * from app_user where username = 'sysadmin'");

        assertThat(sysadmin.get("tenant_id")).isNull();
        assertThat(sysadmin.get("role")).isEqualTo("PLATFORM_ADMIN");
        assertThat(sysadmin.get("status")).isEqualTo("ACTIVE");
        assertThat(sysadmin.get("must_change_password")).isEqualTo(false);
        assertThat(encoder.matches("sysadmin-pass-123", (String) sysadmin.get("password_hash"))).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void isIdempotent() throws Exception {
        bootstrap.run(null);
        bootstrap.run(null);

        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from tenant where code = 'default'", Integer.class)).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from app_user where username = 'admin'", Integer.class)).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from app_user where username = 'sysadmin'", Integer.class)).isEqualTo(1);
    }
}
```

```sql
-- V0.2-C 多租户与用户管理：平台管理员不属于任何租户；旧 ADMIN 角色迁移为 TENANT_ADMIN；
-- 角色、状态、租户编码加约束；删除租户时其用户随之删除。
alter table app_user alter column tenant_id drop not null;
update app_user set role = 'TENANT_ADMIN' where role = 'ADMIN';

alter table app_user
    add column display_name         varchar(64),
    add column must_change_password boolean     not null default false,
    add column updated_at           timestamptz not null default now();

alter table app_user
    add constraint ck_app_user_role check (role in ('PLATFORM_ADMIN', 'TENANT_ADMIN', 'TENANT_VIEWER')),
    add constraint ck_app_user_status check (status in ('ACTIVE', 'DISABLED')),
    add constraint ck_app_user_tenant check ((role = 'PLATFORM_ADMIN') = (tenant_id is null));

alter table app_user drop constraint app_user_tenant_id_fkey;
alter table app_user
    add constraint fk_app_user_tenant foreign key (tenant_id) references tenant (id) on delete cascade;
create index idx_app_user_tenant on app_user (tenant_id);

alter table tenant add column updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
alter table tenant
    add constraint ck_tenant_status check (status in ('ACTIVE', 'DISABLED')),
    add constraint ck_tenant_code check (code ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$');
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.IdType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableId;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableName;
import lombok.Data;

import java.time.OffsetDateTime;

/**
 * 平台用户（表 app_user）。租户用户属于一个租户；平台管理员不属于任何租户（tenantId 为 null）。密码只存 BCrypt 哈希。
 * 该表不参与租户行过滤：登录时尚无租户上下文，需要全局按用户名查找；按租户的查询必须显式带 tenant_id 条件。
 */
@Data
@TableName("app_user")
public class AppUser {
    public static final String ACTIVE = "ACTIVE";
    public static final String DISABLED = "DISABLED";

    @TableId(type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long id;
    /** 所属租户；平台管理员为 null。 */
    private Long tenantId;
    private String username;
    /** 显示名称；为空时界面显示用户名。 */
    private String displayName;
    private String passwordHash;
    /** 角色，取值见 {@link Roles}。 */
    private String role;
    /** ACTIVE 或 DISABLED。 */
    private String status;
    /** 为 true 时登录后只能修改密码（新建的用户、被重置密码的用户）。 */
    private Boolean mustChangePassword;
    private OffsetDateTime createdAt;
    private OffsetDateTime updatedAt;
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.tenant;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.IdType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableId;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableName;
import lombok.Data;

/**
 * 租户（表 tenant），多租户隔离的最顶层单位：产品、设备、租户用户都归属某个租户。
 * 该表本身不做租户行过滤。编码规则见 {@link TenantCodePolicy}，创建后不可修改；默认租户 default 不能停用或删除。

@TableName("tenant")
public class Tenant {

    public static final String DISABLED = "DISABLED";

    private String code;

    /** ACTIVE 或 DISABLED（停用后其用户不能登录、设备不能接入）。 */
    private String status;
    private OffsetDateTime createdAt;
    private OffsetDateTime updatedAt;
}
```

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/bootstrap/AdminBootstrap.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.bootstrap;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationRunner;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * 启动初始化（幂等）：保证默认租户 default 与其租户管理员存在；没有任何平台管理员时按配置创建一个。
 * 已存在的账号不做任何改动（不会重置密码）。
 */
@Component
public class AdminBootstrap implements ApplicationRunner {

    public static final String DEFAULT_TENANT_CODE = Tenant.DEFAULT_CODE;

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AdminBootstrap.class);

    private final BootstrapProperties props;
    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;

    public AdminBootstrap(BootstrapProperties props, TenantMapper tenants, AppUserMapper users,
                          PasswordEncoder encoder) {
        this.props = props;
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.users = users;
        this.encoder = encoder;
    }

    /**
     * 依次保证默认租户、其租户管理员与平台管理员存在。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 默认租户管理员的用户名或密码未配置，进程启动失败
     */
    @Override
    public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
        if (props.adminUsername() == null || props.adminUsername().isBlank()
                || props.adminPassword() == null || props.adminPassword().isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "conduit.bootstrap.admin-username and conduit.bootstrap.admin-password must be set");
        }
        Tenant tenant = ensureDefaultTenant();
        ensureTenantAdmin(tenant);
        ensurePlatformAdmin();
    }

    /** 缺失时创建默认租户（编码 default，名称 Default）。 */
    private Tenant ensureDefaultTenant() {
        Tenant tenant = tenants.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<Tenant>().eq(Tenant::getCode, DEFAULT_TENANT_CODE));
        if (tenant == null) {
            tenant = new Tenant();
            tenant.setCode(DEFAULT_TENANT_CODE);
            tenant.setName("Default");
            tenant.setStatus(Tenant.ACTIVE);
            tenants.insert(tenant);
            log.info("Created default tenant id={}", tenant.getId());
        }
        return tenant;
    }

    /** 缺失时在默认租户下创建租户管理员（不要求首次登录改密码）。 */
    private void ensureTenantAdmin(Tenant tenant) {
        boolean exists = users.exists(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getUsername, props.adminUsername()));
        if (!exists) {
            AppUser admin = new AppUser();
            admin.setTenantId(tenant.getId());
            admin.setUsername(props.adminUsername());
            admin.setPasswordHash(encoder.encode(props.adminPassword()));
            admin.setRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN);
            admin.setStatus(AppUser.ACTIVE);
            admin.setMustChangePassword(false);
            users.insert(admin);
            log.info("Created tenant admin '{}' in the default tenant", props.adminUsername());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 没有任何平台管理员时，按 platformAdminUsername / platformAdminPassword 创建一个（不属于任何租户）。
     * 密码为空时只打一条 WARN、不创建，不阻止启动；用户名已被租户用户占用时同样只警告。
     */
    void ensurePlatformAdmin() {
        if (users.exists(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getRole, Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN))) {
            return;
        }
        String username = props.platformAdminUsername();
        String password = props.platformAdminPassword();
        if (password == null || password.isBlank()) {
            log.warn("No platform admin exists and CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD is empty; "
                    + "platform admin '{}' was not created", username);
            return;
        }
        if (users.exists(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getUsername, username))) {
            log.warn("Username '{}' is already taken by a tenant user; platform admin was not created", username);
            return;
        }
        AppUser admin = new AppUser();
        admin.setUsername(username);
        admin.setPasswordHash(encoder.encode(password));
        admin.setRole(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        admin.setStatus(AppUser.ACTIVE);
        admin.setMustChangePassword(false);
        users.insert(admin);
        log.info("Created platform admin '{}'", username);
    }
}
```

## Task 2: Backend tokens, sessions, the permission matrix, the forced password change and personal endpoints

JWTs carry a nullable tenant, the impersonator and the must-change-password flag; login and refresh re-check users and tenants; `SecurityConfig` enforces spec §3.3; `PasswordChangeGateFilter` enforces spec §3.4; `GET /auth/me`, `PUT /me`, `PUT /me/password` and `POST /auth/exit-tenant` follow spec §3.5 and §4.3.

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AuthPrincipal.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/JwtService.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/JwtAuthFilter.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/PasswordChangeGateFilter.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SecurityConfig.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AuthDtos.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SessionService.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AuthController.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/MeService.java`, `MeController.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/support/IntegrationTestBase.java` (login helpers)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/JwtServiceTest.java` (full replacement)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/PermissionMatrixIT.java`, `SessionIT.java`, `MeApiIT.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/overview/OverviewApiIT.java` (role in `tokenFor`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1): `Roles.*`, `AppUser.ACTIVE/DISABLED`, `AppUser.getMustChangePassword()/getDisplayName()`, `Tenant.ACTIVE/DISABLED`, `PasswordPolicy.requireValid`, `DisplayNamePolicy.normalize`, `IntegrationTestBase.createUser/TEST_PASSWORD`.
- Produces:
  - `AuthPrincipal(long userId, Long tenantId, String username, String role, Long impersonatorId, boolean mustChangePassword)`, convenience constructor `AuthPrincipal(long userId, Long tenantId, String username, String role)`, `boolean impersonating()`.
  - JWT claims: `sub` user id, `tid` (omitted when null), `usr`, `role`, `typ`, `imp` (only when impersonating), `mcp` (only when true).
  - `SessionService` (`@Component`): `REFRESH_COOKIE = "conduit_refresh"`; `AppUser requireActive(AppUser)` (401); `AuthPrincipal principalOf(AppUser)`; `AuthPrincipal impersonation(AppUser admin, Tenant tenant)`; `ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> issue(AuthPrincipal)`; `ResponseCookie clearedRefreshCookie()`; `UserView userView(AuthPrincipal)`.
  - `AuthDtos.UserView(String username, String displayName, String role, String tenantCode, String tenantName, boolean mustChangePassword, String impersonatedBy)`; `TokenResponse(String accessToken, long expiresIn, UserView user)`; `UpdateMeRequest(String displayName)`; `ChangePasswordRequest(String oldPassword, String newPassword)`.
  - Endpoints: `POST /api/v1/auth/exit-tenant` → 200 `TokenResponse` (409 when not impersonating); `PUT /api/v1/me` → 200 `UserView`; `PUT /api/v1/me/password` → 200 `TokenResponse` (400 wrong old / weak / unchanged, 409 when impersonating).
  - `PasswordChangeGateFilter.CODE = "PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED"`; `SecurityConfig.writeProblem(HttpServletResponse, ObjectMapper, HttpStatus, String detail, String code)` (package-private static).
  - `IntegrationTestBase`: `PLATFORM_ADMIN = "sysadmin"`, `PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD = "sysadmin-pass-123"`, `MvcResult loginResult(String, String)`, `String login(String, String)`, `String loginAsPlatformAdmin()`, `long userId(String)`, `String tenantCode(long)`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- The access token carries the **effective** role, so the matrix is plain role checks: `/platform/**` needs `ROLE_PLATFORM_ADMIN` (an impersonation token has `TENANT_ADMIN`, so it is refused, as spec §3.3 requires); `/users/**` needs `ROLE_TENANT_ADMIN`; tenant `GET` needs `TENANT_ADMIN` or `TENANT_VIEWER`; tenant writes need `TENANT_ADMIN`; `/auth/**` and `/me/**` need any login. A platform admin outside a tenant has no tenant role, so every tenant endpoint returns 403 (including `/overview` and `/codecs`).
- In an impersonation token, `sub` and `usr` are the platform admin's own id and username, `tid` is the target tenant, `role` is `TENANT_ADMIN`, and `imp` repeats the platform admin's id. `/auth/me` then reports `impersonatedBy` = that username and the target tenant's code and name.
- The must-change-password flag travels as the `mcp` claim, so the gate needs no database read per request. `PUT /me/password` returns a fresh `TokenResponse` (and a new refresh cookie), so the gate lifts immediately. A password reset by an admin reaches already-issued tokens at the next refresh, like every other account change (spec §3.2).
- `GET /auth/me`'s `mustChangePassword` reads the database (it may turn true before the token does); impersonation always reports `false`.
- Login: unknown user or wrong password → 401 「用户名或密码错误」; only after the password matches do disabled users and tenants get their own 401 messages 「账号已禁用」 / 「所属租户已停用」 (no account-existence leak).
- Refresh of an impersonation token re-checks the platform admin (must still exist, be `PLATFORM_ADMIN` and `ACTIVE`, else 401) and the target tenant; a deleted or disabled tenant returns the platform admin's own session (spec §3.2) and logs one INFO line.
- `PUT /me/password` rejects a new password equal to the old one (400 「新密码不能与原密码相同」), so a forced change really changes it. `PUT /me` under impersonation updates the platform admin's own display name (only the password change is forbidden there).
- `/auth/exit-tenant` and `/auth/me` stay under `/api/v1/auth/**` (authenticated); the gate allows only `GET /auth/me`, `PUT /me/password`, login, refresh and logout.

```java
    static final AuthPrincipal ALICE = new AuthPrincipal(7, 3L, "alice", Roles.TENANT_ADMIN);

        AuthPrincipal root = new AuthPrincipal(1, null, "sysadmin", Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN);

        AuthPrincipal parsed = jwt.parse(jwt.issue(root, ACCESS), ACCESS);

        assertThat(parsed).isEqualTo(root);

        assertThat(parsed.impersonating()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void carriesTheImpersonatorAndThePasswordChangeFlag() {
        JwtService jwt = at(T0);
        AuthPrincipal entered = new AuthPrincipal(1, 3L, "sysadmin", Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, 1L, false);
        AuthPrincipal fresh = new AuthPrincipal(9, 3L, "east-viewer", Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, null, true);

        assertThat(jwt.parse(jwt.issue(entered, REFRESH), REFRESH)).isEqualTo(entered);
        assertThat(jwt.parse(jwt.issue(entered, ACCESS), ACCESS).impersonating()).isTrue();
        assertThat(jwt.parse(jwt.issue(fresh, ACCESS), ACCESS).mustChangePassword()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test

        String refresh = jwt.issue(ALICE, REFRESH);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jwt.parse(refresh, ACCESS)).isInstanceOf(JwtException.class);
    }

    @Test

        String token = at(T0).issue(ALICE, ACCESS);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> at(T0.plus(Duration.ofMinutes(16))).parse(token, ACCESS))
                .isInstanceOf(JwtException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void refreshTokenOutlivesAccessToken() {
        String token = at(T0).issue(ALICE, REFRESH);
        assertThat(at(T0.plus(Duration.ofDays(6))).parse(token, REFRESH)).isEqualTo(ALICE);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsTamperedToken() {
        JwtService jwt = at(T0);
        String token = jwt.issue(ALICE, ACCESS);
        String tampered = token.substring(0, token.length() - 2) + (token.endsWith("A") ? "BB" : "AA");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jwt.parse(tampered, ACCESS)).isInstanceOf(JwtException.class);
    }

        var weak = new JwtProperties("too-short", Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofDays(7), false);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new JwtService(weak, Clock.systemUTC()))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

/**
 * 已认证的调用者身份，由 JWT 解析得到并放入 Spring Security 上下文；tenantId 决定后续数据访问的租户范围。
 *
 * @param userId             用户 id；代入令牌中是平台管理员自己的 id
 * @param tenantId           租户 id；未代入的平台管理员为 null，代入时为目标租户
 * @param username           用户名；代入令牌中是平台管理员的用户名
 * @param role               有效角色（见 {@link Roles}）；代入时为 TENANT_ADMIN
 * @param impersonatorId     代入的平台管理员用户 id；未代入为 null
 * @param mustChangePassword 为 true 时除修改密码等少数接口外一律 403（见 {@link PasswordChangeGateFilter}）
 */
public record AuthPrincipal(long userId, Long tenantId, String username, String role, Long impersonatorId,
                            boolean mustChangePassword) {

    /** 普通身份（未代入、无需改密码）。 */
    public AuthPrincipal(long userId, Long tenantId, String username, String role) {
        this(userId, tenantId, username, role, null, false);
    }

    /** 是否为平台管理员代入租户的身份。 */
    public boolean impersonating() {
        return impersonatorId != null;
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/JwtService.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtBuilder;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtException;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import io.jsonwebtoken.security.Keys;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Date;

/**
 * JWT 的签发与校验（HS 系列对称签名）。声明：sub 用户 id、tid 租户 id（平台管理员没有）、usr 用户名、role 有效角色、
 * typ 令牌类型、imp 代入的平台管理员用户 id（仅代入时）、mcp 需要先修改密码（仅为 true 时）。
 * 由 {@link SessionService} 签发、{@link JwtAuthFilter} 与刷新接口校验。
 */
@Component
public class JwtService {

    /** 令牌类型：ACCESS 用于访问接口，REFRESH 仅用于换发；校验时必须匹配，防止互相冒用。 */
    public enum TokenType { ACCESS, REFRESH }

    private final JwtProperties props;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final SecretKey key;

    public JwtService(JwtProperties props, Clock clock) {
        if (props.jwtSecret() == null || props.jwtSecret().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("conduit.auth.jwt-secret must be at least 32 bytes");
        }
        this.props = props;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(props.jwtSecret().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }

    /** 签发指定类型的令牌，有效期取配置中对应类型的 TTL；为空的可选声明（tid、imp）与为 false 的 mcp 不写入。 */
    public String issue(AuthPrincipal principal, TokenType type) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        Duration ttl = type == TokenType.ACCESS ? props.accessTtl() : props.refreshTtl();
        JwtBuilder builder = Jwts.builder()
                .subject(Long.toString(principal.userId()))
                .claim("usr", principal.username())
                .claim("role", principal.role())
                .claim("typ", type.name());
        if (principal.tenantId() != null) {
            builder.claim("tid", principal.tenantId());
        }
        if (principal.impersonatorId() != null) {
            builder.claim("imp", principal.impersonatorId());
        }
        if (principal.mustChangePassword()) {
            builder.claim("mcp", true);
        }
        return builder.issuedAt(Date.from(now)).expiration(Date.from(now.plus(ttl))).signWith(key).compact();
    }

    /**
     * 校验签名与有效期并还原身份。
     *
     * @param expected 期望的令牌类型
     * @throws JwtException 签名错误、已过期、格式非法或类型不符（调用方应一并处理 IllegalArgumentException）
     */
    public AuthPrincipal parse(String token, TokenType expected) {
        Claims claims = Jwts.parser()
                .verifyWith(key)
                .clock(() -> Date.from(clock.instant()))
                .build()
                .parseSignedClaims(token)
                .getPayload();
        if (!expected.name().equals(claims.get("typ", String.class))) {
            throw new JwtException("Unexpected token type");
        }
        return new AuthPrincipal(
                Long.parseLong(claims.getSubject()),
                optionalLong(claims.get("tid")),
                claims.get("usr", String.class),
                claims.get("role", String.class),
                optionalLong(claims.get("imp")),
                Boolean.TRUE.equals(claims.get("mcp", Boolean.class)));
    }

    /** 数值声明转 Long；声明不存在时为 null。 */
    private static Long optionalLong(Object value) {
        return value instanceof Number n ? n.longValue() : null;
    }
}
```

```java
    /** 用户名密码登录，返回完整结果（状态码、响应体与 refresh Cookie 由调用方断言）。 */
    protected MvcResult loginResult(String username, String password) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/login")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{\"username\":\"%s\",\"password\":\"%s\"}".formatted(username, password)))
                .andReturn();
    }

    /** 登录并返回 access token；登录不成功时断言失败。 */
    protected String login(String username, String password) throws Exception {
        MvcResult result = loginResult(username, password);
        assertThat(result.getResponse().getStatus()).as("login %s", username).isEqualTo(200);
        return JsonPath.read(result.getResponse().getContentAsString(), "$.accessToken");
    }

    /** 以测试配置的平台管理员登录。 */
    protected String loginAsPlatformAdmin() throws Exception {
        return login(PLATFORM_ADMIN, PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD);
    }

    /** 按用户名查用户 id。 */
    protected long userId(String username) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject("select id from app_user where username = ?", Long.class, username);
    }

    /** 按租户 id 查租户编码。 */
    protected String tenantCode(long tenantId) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject("select code from tenant where id = ?", String.class, tenantId);
    }
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;

import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockHttpServletRequestBuilder;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.request;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 权限矩阵集成测试（spec §3.3）：平台管理员、租户管理员、只读用户与代入的平台管理员分别访问平台接口、租户读、租户写、
 * 用户管理与个人接口。「允许」以「不是 401/403」判断（平台与用户管理接口在后续任务实现前返回 404，实现后同样成立）；
 * 租户写用空请求体探测，允许时因参数校验返回 400，不会真的写入数据。
 */
class PermissionMatrixIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    @Autowired
    JwtService jwt;

    /** 一次探测的结果：各类接口的 HTTP 状态码。 */
    record Probe(int platform, int tenantRead, int tenantWrite, int users, int me) {
    }

    private int status(String token, HttpMethod method, String path) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletRequestBuilder builder = request(method, path).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token));
        if (method == HttpMethod.POST || method == HttpMethod.PUT) {
            builder.contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{}");
        }
        return mvc.perform(builder).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
    }

    private Probe probe(String token) throws Exception {
        return new Probe(
                status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/platform/overview"),
                status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/products"),
                status(token, HttpMethod.POST, "/api/v1/products"),
                status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/users"),
                status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/auth/me"));
    }

    @Test
    void platformAdminReachesOnlyPlatformAndPersonalEndpoints() throws Exception {
        String token = loginAsPlatformAdmin();
        Probe p = probe(token);

        assertThat(p.platform()).isNotIn(401, 403);
        assertThat(p.tenantRead()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.tenantWrite()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.users()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.me()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/overview")).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/codecs")).isEqualTo(403);
    }

    @Test
    void tenantAdminReadsWritesAndManagesUsersButNotThePlatform() throws Exception {
        String admin = createUser(createTenant("pm-"), Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);
        Probe p = probe(login(admin, TEST_PASSWORD));

        assertThat(p.platform()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.tenantRead()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(p.tenantWrite()).isEqualTo(400);
        assertThat(p.users()).isNotIn(401, 403);
        assertThat(p.me()).isEqualTo(200);
    }

    @Test
    void viewerOnlyReads() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(createTenant("pm-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        String token = login(viewer, TEST_PASSWORD);
        Probe p = probe(token);

        assertThat(p.platform()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.tenantRead()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(p.tenantWrite()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.users()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.me()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(status(token, HttpMethod.PUT, "/api/v1/devices/dk-none")).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(status(token, HttpMethod.DELETE, "/api/v1/products/pk-none")).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(status(token, HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/codecs")).isEqualTo(200);
    }

    @Test
    void impersonatingPlatformAdminActsAsTenantAdminButLosesThePlatform() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("pm-");
        long adminId = userId(PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        String token = jwt.issue(new AuthPrincipal(adminId, tenant, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, adminId, false),
                JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);
        Probe p = probe(token);

        assertThat(p.platform()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(p.tenantRead()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(p.tenantWrite()).isEqualTo(400);
        assertThat(p.users()).isNotIn(401, 403);
        assertThat(p.me()).isEqualTo(200);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/auth/me").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.impersonatedBy").value(PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tenantCode").value(tenantCode(tenant)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN));
    }

    @Test
    void anonymousCallsAreUnauthorized() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/overview")).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users")).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products")).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import jakarta.servlet.http.Cookie;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.header;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 会话集成测试：登录返回完整用户视图；用户禁用、租户停用后登录与续期被拒；角色变化在续期后生效；
 * 平台管理员登录没有租户；代入令牌续期在租户可用时保持代入、停用或删除后退回平台管理员；退出租户。
 */
@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
class SessionIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    @Autowired
    JwtService jwt;

    private Cookie refreshCookie(String username) throws Exception {
        return loginResult(username, TEST_PASSWORD).getResponse().getCookie(SessionService.REFRESH_COOKIE);
    }

    private Cookie impersonationRefresh(long tenantId) {
        long adminId = userId(PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        String token = jwt.issue(new AuthPrincipal(adminId, tenantId, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, adminId, false),
                JwtService.TokenType.REFRESH);
        return new Cookie(SessionService.REFRESH_COOKIE, token);
    }

    @Test
    void loginReturnsTheFullUserView() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");
        String viewer = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        jdbc.update("update app_user set display_name = '华东运维 张工' where username = ?", viewer);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/login").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{\"username\":\"%s\",\"password\":\"%s\"}".formatted(viewer, TEST_PASSWORD)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.username").value(viewer))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.displayName").value("华东运维 张工"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.TENANT_VIEWER))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantCode").value(tenantCode(tenant)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.mustChangePassword").value(false))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.impersonatedBy").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void platformAdminLogsInWithoutATenant() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/login").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{\"username\":\"%s\",\"password\":\"%s\"}".formatted(PLATFORM_ADMIN, PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantCode").isEmpty())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantName").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void disabledUsersCannotLogInOrRefresh() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(createTenant("ss-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        Cookie refresh = refreshCookie(viewer);

        jdbc.update("update app_user set status = 'DISABLED' where username = ?", viewer);

        assertThat(loginResult(viewer, TEST_PASSWORD).getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh)).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }

    @Test
    void disabledTenantsBlockLoginAndRefresh() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");
        String admin = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);
        Cookie refresh = refreshCookie(admin);

        jdbc.update("update tenant set status = 'DISABLED' where id = ?", tenant);

        assertThat(loginResult(admin, TEST_PASSWORD).getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).contains("所属租户已停用");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh)).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }

    @Test
    void wrongPasswordDoesNotRevealADisabledAccount() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(createTenant("ss-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        jdbc.update("update app_user set status = 'DISABLED' where username = ?", viewer);

        assertThat(loginResult(viewer, "Wrong-pass-1").getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).contains("用户名或密码错误");
    }

    @Test
    void roleChangesTakeEffectOnRefresh() throws Exception {
        String user = createUser(createTenant("ss-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        Cookie refresh = refreshCookie(user);

        jdbc.update("update app_user set role = 'TENANT_ADMIN' where username = ?", user);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN));
    }

    @Test
    void impersonationRefreshKeepsAnActiveTenant() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(impersonationRefresh(tenant)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.impersonatedBy").value(PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantCode").value(tenantCode(tenant)));
    }

    @Test
    void impersonationRefreshFallsBackWhenTheTenantIsDisabled() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");
        Cookie refresh = impersonationRefresh(tenant);
        jdbc.update("update tenant set status = 'DISABLED' where id = ?", tenant);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.impersonatedBy").isEmpty())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantCode").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void impersonationRefreshFallsBackWhenTheTenantIsDeleted() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");
        Cookie refresh = impersonationRefresh(tenant);
        jdbc.update("delete from tenant where id = ?", tenant);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN));
    }

    @Test
    void exitTenantReturnsThePlatformAdminSession(CapturedOutput output) throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("ss-");
        long adminId = userId(PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        String access = jwt.issue(new AuthPrincipal(adminId, tenant, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, adminId, false),
                JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(access)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE, org.hamcrest.Matchers.containsString("conduit_refresh=")))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.impersonatedBy").isEmpty());
        assertThat(output).contains("exited tenant " + tenantCode(tenant));
    }

    @Test
    void exitTenantWithoutImpersonationIsAConflict() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsPlatformAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 个人接口集成测试：修改显示名称；需改密码时只放行 /auth/me、修改密码与登出，其余 403 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED；
 * 修改密码成功后换发令牌并解除限制；原密码错误、新密码不合规或与原密码相同 400；代入期间修改密码 409。
 */
class MeApiIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private static final String NEW_PASSWORD = "Changed-pass-456";

    @Autowired
    JwtService jwt;

    private ResultActions changePassword(String token, String oldPassword, String newPassword) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/me/password").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"oldPassword\":\"%s\",\"newPassword\":\"%s\"}".formatted(oldPassword, newPassword)));
    }

    @Test
    void updatesAndClearsTheDisplayName() throws Exception {
        String token = login(createUser(createTenant("me-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false), TEST_PASSWORD);

        mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/me").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"displayName\":\"  华东运维 张工  \"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.displayName").value("华东运维 张工"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/auth/me").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.displayName").value("华东运维 张工"));
        mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/me").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"displayName\":\"   \"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.displayName").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void mustChangePasswordGatesEverythingButTheAllowedEndpoints() throws Exception {
        String username = createUser(createTenant("me-"), Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, true);
        String token = login(username, TEST_PASSWORD);

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/auth/me").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mustChangePassword").value(true));
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/logout").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        String body = changePassword(token, TEST_PASSWORD, NEW_PASSWORD)
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.mustChangePassword").value(false))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String fresh = JsonPath.read(body, "$.accessToken");

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(fresh)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select must_change_password from app_user where username = ?",
                Boolean.class, username)).isFalse();
        login(username, NEW_PASSWORD);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongWeakAndUnchangedPasswords() throws Exception {
        String token = login(createUser(createTenant("me-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false), TEST_PASSWORD);

        changePassword(token, "Wrong-pass-1", NEW_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.detail").value("原密码错误"));
        changePassword(token, TEST_PASSWORD, "short1").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        changePassword(token, TEST_PASSWORD, TEST_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.detail").value("新密码不能与原密码相同"));
    }

    @Test
    void impersonationCannotChangeThePassword() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("me-");
        long adminId = userId(PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        String token = jwt.issue(new AuthPrincipal(adminId, tenant, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, adminId, false),
                JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);

        changePassword(token, PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD, NEW_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/JwtAuthFilter.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantContext;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtException;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.authority.SimpleGrantedAuthority;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 解析 Bearer access token，设置认证信息（权限为 ROLE_<有效角色>）并绑定租户；未代入的平台管理员没有租户，不绑定。
 * token 无效时保持未认证，由入口点返回 401。
 */
public class JwtAuthFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    private final JwtService jwt;

    public JwtAuthFilter(JwtService jwt) {
        this.jwt = jwt;
    }

    /**
     * 有合法 Bearer access token 时写入认证信息，令牌带租户时绑定 TenantContext；令牌无效不报错，仅保持未认证。
     * 无论请求如何结束都会在 finally 中清除 TenantContext，避免线程复用时租户串用。
     */
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, FilterChain chain)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        String header = request.getHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);
        if (header != null && header.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            try {
                AuthPrincipal principal = jwt.parse(header.substring(7), JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);
                var authentication = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(principal, null,
                        List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_" + principal.role())));
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(authentication);
                if (principal.tenantId() != null) {
                    TenantContext.set(principal.tenantId());
                }
            } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                // 保持未认证
            }
        }
        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            TenantContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```
