Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/PasswordChangeGateFilter.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import java.io.IOException;

/**
 * 强制修改密码的闸门：令牌带 mcp（需要先修改密码）时，除 GET /api/v1/auth/me、PUT /api/v1/me/password、登录、刷新、登出
 * 以及非 /api/ 路径外，一律返回 403，ProblemDetail 属性 code = PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED。位于 JwtAuthFilter 之后、权限判断之前。
 */
public class PasswordChangeGateFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    /** ProblemDetail 的 code 属性值，前端据此弹出强制改密码对话框。 */
    public static final String CODE = "PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED";

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public PasswordChangeGateFilter(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    /** 已认证且需改密码、请求又不在放行清单内时直接写出 403；其余情况放行。 */
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, FilterChain chain)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        Authentication authentication = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        if (authentication != null && authentication.getPrincipal() instanceof AuthPrincipal principal
                && principal.mustChangePassword() && !allowed(request)) {
            SecurityConfig.writeProblem(response, objectMapper, HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "请先修改密码", CODE);
            return;
        }
        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    /** 需改密码时仍放行的请求。 */
    static boolean allowed(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String path = request.getRequestURI();
        String method = request.getMethod();
        return !path.startsWith("/api/")
                || ("GET".equals(method) && "/api/v1/auth/me".equals(path))
                || ("PUT".equals(method) && "/api/v1/me/password".equals(path))
                || "/api/v1/auth/login".equals(path)
                || "/api/v1/auth/refresh".equals(path)
                || "/api/v1/auth/logout".equals(path);
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SecurityConfig.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ProblemDetail;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.http.SessionCreationPolicy;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.security.web.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter;

import java.io.IOException;

/**
 * Spring Security 配置：无状态 JWT 认证与权限矩阵（spec §3.3，后端统一按路径与方法判断，前端只做隐藏）：
 * <ul>
 *   <li>登录、刷新、登出、/internal/**（EMQX 回调，自行校验）、健康检查公开；</li>
 *   <li>/api/v1/auth/**、/api/v1/me/**：所有登录用户；</li>
 *   <li>/api/v1/platform/**：平台管理员（代入令牌的有效角色是 TENANT_ADMIN，因此被拒）；</li>
 *   <li>/api/v1/users/**：租户管理员（含代入的平台管理员）；</li>
 *   <li>其余 /api/v1/** 的 GET：租户管理员、只读用户；其他方法：租户管理员。</li>
 * </ul>
 * 未认证 401、无权限 403，均为 problem+json。过滤器顺序：JwtAuthFilter → PasswordChangeGateFilter → 权限判断。
 */
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
public class SecurityConfig {

    /** 构建过滤链（规则见类注释）。 */
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http, JwtService jwt, ObjectMapper objectMapper)
            throws Exception {
        http
                // 无会话 + refresh Cookie 为 SameSite=Strict 且只发往 /api/v1/auth，关闭 CSRF
                .csrf(c -> c.disable())
                .httpBasic(b -> b.disable())
                .formLogin(f -> f.disable())
                .sessionManagement(s -> s.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(a -> a
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/auth/login", "/api/v1/auth/refresh", "/api/v1/auth/logout").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/internal/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/actuator/health", "/actuator/health/**", "/error").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/auth/**", "/api/v1/me", "/api/v1/me/**").authenticated()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/platform/**").hasRole(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN)
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/users", "/api/v1/users/**").hasRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/**").hasAnyRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER)
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/**").hasRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)
                        .anyRequest().authenticated())
                .exceptionHandling(e -> e
                        .authenticationEntryPoint((req, res, ex) ->
                                writeProblem(res, objectMapper, HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "未认证或登录已过期", null))
                        .accessDeniedHandler((req, res, ex) ->
                                writeProblem(res, objectMapper, HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "无权限", null)))
                .addFilterBefore(new JwtAuthFilter(jwt), UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class)
                .addFilterAfter(new PasswordChangeGateFilter(objectMapper), JwtAuthFilter.class);
        return http.build();
    }

    /**
     * 直接向响应写入 RFC 7807 problem+json（过滤器层异常不经过 MVC 异常处理器）。
     *
     * @param code 非空时写入 ProblemDetail 的 code 属性（如 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED）
     */
    static void writeProblem(HttpServletResponse response, ObjectMapper objectMapper,
                             HttpStatus status, String detail, String code) throws IOException {
        ProblemDetail problem = ProblemDetail.forStatusAndDetail(status, detail);
        if (code != null) {
            problem.setProperty("code", code);
        }
        response.setStatus(status.value());
        response.setContentType("application/problem+json");
        objectMapper.writeValue(response.getOutputStream(), problem);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;

/** 认证与个人接口（{@link AuthController}、{@link MeController}）的请求/响应 DTO 集合。 */
public final class AuthDtos {

    private AuthDtos() {
    }

    /** 登录请求，用户名与密码均不可为空。 */
    public record LoginRequest(@NotBlank String username, @NotBlank String password) {
    }

    /**
     * 返回给 UI 的当前用户信息（不含任何敏感字段）。
     *
     * @param displayName        显示名称；未设置时为 null（界面显示用户名）
     * @param role               有效角色；代入时为 TENANT_ADMIN
     * @param tenantCode         当前租户编码；未代入的平台管理员为 null
     * @param tenantName         当前租户名称；未代入的平台管理员为 null
     * @param mustChangePassword 是否需要先修改密码（代入时恒为 false）
     * @param impersonatedBy     代入的平台管理员用户名；未代入为 null
     */
    public record UserView(String username, String displayName, String role, String tenantCode, String tenantName,
                           boolean mustChangePassword, String impersonatedBy) {
    }

    /**
     * 登录、刷新、进入/退出租户、修改密码的响应。
     *
     * @param accessToken access token
     * @param expiresIn   access token 有效期，单位秒
     * @param user        当前用户
     */
    public record TokenResponse(String accessToken, long expiresIn, UserView user) {
    }

    /** 修改自己的显示名称；空白表示清除。 */
    public record UpdateMeRequest(String displayName) {
    }

    /** 修改自己的密码。 */
    public record ChangePasswordRequest(@NotBlank String oldPassword, @NotBlank String newPassword) {
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SessionService.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.TokenResponse;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UserView;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseCookie;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * 会话的校验与签发：判断用户（及其租户）能否登录或续期，构造普通身份与代入身份，签发 access + refresh 令牌
 * （refresh 写入 HttpOnly Cookie），组装给 UI 的当前用户视图。登录、续期、进入/退出租户、修改密码都经由这里签发令牌。
 */
@Component
public class SessionService {

    /** refresh 令牌的 Cookie 名称（HttpOnly、SameSite=Strict、只发往 /api/v1/auth）。 */
    public static final String REFRESH_COOKIE = "conduit_refresh";

    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final JwtService jwt;
    private final JwtProperties props;

    public SessionService(AppUserMapper users, TenantMapper tenants, JwtService jwt, JwtProperties props) {
        this.users = users;
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.jwt = jwt;
        this.props = props;
    }

    /**
     * 确认用户可以登录或续期：用户存在且启用；租户用户的租户存在且启用（平台管理员没有租户，不检查）。
     *
     * @return 原样返回用户
     * @throws ApiException 401：用户不存在或已禁用，所属租户不存在或已停用
     */
    public AppUser requireActive(AppUser user) {
        if (user == null) {
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("用户不可用");
        }
        if (!AppUser.ACTIVE.equals(user.getStatus())) {
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("账号已禁用");
        }
        if (user.getTenantId() != null) {
            Tenant tenant = tenants.selectById(user.getTenantId());
            if (tenant == null) {
                throw ApiException.unauthorized("所属租户不存在");
            }
            if (!Tenant.ACTIVE.equals(tenant.getStatus())) {
                throw ApiException.unauthorized("所属租户已停用");
            }
        }
        return user;
    }

    /** 用户自己的身份：角色、租户与「需改密码」标记都取数据库当前值。 */
    public AuthPrincipal principalOf(AppUser user) {
        return new AuthPrincipal(user.getId(), user.getTenantId(), user.getUsername(), user.getRole(), null,
                Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getMustChangePassword()));
    }

    /** 平台管理员代入目标租户的身份：有效角色 TENANT_ADMIN，imp 为平台管理员自己的 id，不带改密码标记。 */
    public AuthPrincipal impersonation(AppUser admin, Tenant tenant) {
        return new AuthPrincipal(admin.getId(), tenant.getId(), admin.getUsername(), Roles.TENANT_ADMIN,
                admin.getId(), false);
    }

    /** 签发令牌：refresh 写入 Cookie，access、有效期（秒）与当前用户视图放在响应体。 */
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> issue(AuthPrincipal principal) {
        String access = jwt.issue(principal, JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);
        String refresh = jwt.issue(principal, JwtService.TokenType.REFRESH);
        return ResponseEntity.ok()
                .header(HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE, refreshCookie(refresh, props.refreshTtl()).toString())
                .body(new TokenResponse(access, props.accessTtl().toSeconds(), userView(principal)));
    }

    /** 清除 refresh Cookie（Max-Age=0），登出时使用。 */
    public ResponseCookie clearedRefreshCookie() {
        return refreshCookie("", Duration.ZERO);
    }

    /**
     * 组装当前用户视图：显示名称与「需改密码」取数据库当前值；租户编码与名称按令牌中的租户查（平台管理员为 null）；
     * 代入时 impersonatedBy 为平台管理员用户名，mustChangePassword 恒为 false。
     */
    public UserView userView(AuthPrincipal principal) {
        AppUser user = users.selectById(principal.userId());
        Tenant tenant = principal.tenantId() == null ? null : tenants.selectById(principal.tenantId());
        boolean mustChange = !principal.impersonating() && user != null
                && Boolean.TRUE.equals(user.getMustChangePassword());
        return new UserView(principal.username(), user == null ? null : user.getDisplayName(), principal.role(),
                tenant == null ? null : tenant.getCode(), tenant == null ? null : tenant.getName(), mustChange,
                principal.impersonating() ? principal.username() : null);
    }

    /** refresh Cookie：HttpOnly、SameSite=Strict、仅发往 /api/v1/auth；Secure 由配置决定（生产应开启）。 */
    private ResponseCookie refreshCookie(String value, Duration maxAge) {
        return ResponseCookie.from(REFRESH_COOKIE, value)
                .httpOnly(true)
                .secure(props.cookieSecure())
                .sameSite("Strict")
                .path("/api/v1/auth")
                .maxAge(maxAge)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/AuthController.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.LoginRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.TokenResponse;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UserView;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtException;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.CookieValue;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

/**
 * 认证 REST 接口（UI 使用）：登录签发短期 access token（响应体）与长期 refresh token（HttpOnly Cookie），刷新、登出、
 * 查询当前用户，以及平台管理员退出代入的租户。登录、刷新、登出公开，其余需要登录。
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/auth")
public class AuthController {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AuthController.class);

    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final JwtService jwt;
    private final SessionService sessions;

    public AuthController(AppUserMapper users, TenantMapper tenants, PasswordEncoder encoder, JwtService jwt,
                          SessionService sessions) {
        this.users = users;
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.jwt = jwt;
        this.sessions = sessions;
    }

    /**
     * 用户名密码登录。用户不存在或密码错误返回同一条 401「用户名或密码错误」（不泄露账号是否存在）；
     * 密码正确后才检查账号禁用、租户停用（各自的 401）。需改密码的用户照常登录，令牌带 mcp。
     */
    @PostMapping("/login")
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> login(@Valid @RequestBody LoginRequest request) {
        AppUser user = users.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getUsername, request.username()));
        if (user == null || !encoder.matches(request.password(), user.getPasswordHash())) {
            log.debug("Login rejected for {}: bad credentials", request.username());
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("用户名或密码错误");
        }
        sessions.requireActive(user);
        log.debug("Login succeeded for {} (role={})", user.getUsername(), user.getRole());
        return sessions.issue(sessions.principalOf(user));
    }

    /**
     * 用 refresh Cookie 换发新的令牌（滚动续期），以数据库当前状态为准：用户禁用、租户停用 401，角色变化在此生效。
     * 代入令牌：平台管理员仍可用时，目标租户可用则继续代入，已停用或已删除则退回平台管理员自己的令牌（INFO 日志）。
     */
    @PostMapping("/refresh")
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> refresh(
            @CookieValue(name = SessionService.REFRESH_COOKIE, required = false) String refreshToken) {
        if (refreshToken == null || refreshToken.isBlank()) {
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("缺少刷新凭证");
        }
        AuthPrincipal principal;
        try {
            principal = jwt.parse(refreshToken, JwtService.TokenType.REFRESH);
        } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("刷新凭证无效");
        }
        if (principal.impersonating()) {
            return refreshImpersonation(principal);
        }
        AppUser user = sessions.requireActive(users.selectById(principal.userId()));
        return sessions.issue(sessions.principalOf(user));
    }

    /** 代入令牌的续期（规则见 {@link #refresh}）。 */
    private ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> refreshImpersonation(AuthPrincipal principal) {
        AppUser admin = users.selectById(principal.impersonatorId());
        if (admin == null || !Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN.equals(admin.getRole())) {
            throw ApiException.unauthorized("用户不可用");
        }
        sessions.requireActive(admin);
        Tenant tenant = principal.tenantId() == null ? null : tenants.selectById(principal.tenantId());
        if (tenant == null || !Tenant.ACTIVE.equals(tenant.getStatus())) {
            log.info("Platform admin {} left tenant id={} on refresh: tenant {}", admin.getUsername(),
                    principal.tenantId(), tenant == null ? "deleted" : "disabled");
            return sessions.issue(sessions.principalOf(admin));
        }
        return sessions.issue(sessions.impersonation(admin, tenant));
    }

    /** 登出：仅通过 Max-Age=0 清除 refresh Cookie；令牌无服务端状态，已签发的 access token 到期前仍有效。 */
    @PostMapping("/logout")
    public ResponseEntity<Void> logout() {
        return ResponseEntity.noContent()
                .header(HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE, sessions.clearedRefreshCookie().toString())
                .build();
    }

    /** 当前登录用户（含显示名称、租户、是否需改密码、代入者）。 */
    @GetMapping("/me")
    public UserView me(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal principal) {
        return sessions.userView(principal);
    }

    /**
     * 平台管理员退出代入的租户：换回自己的令牌，记一行 INFO。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 409：当前令牌不是代入令牌；401：平台管理员已被删除或禁用
     */
    @PostMapping("/exit-tenant")
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> exitTenant(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal principal) {
        if (!principal.impersonating()) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("当前没有进入任何租户");
        }
        AppUser admin = sessions.requireActive(users.selectById(principal.impersonatorId()));
        Tenant tenant = tenants.selectById(principal.tenantId());
        log.info("Platform admin {} exited tenant {}", admin.getUsername(),
                tenant == null ? String.valueOf(principal.tenantId()) : tenant.getCode());
        return sessions.issue(sessions.principalOf(admin));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Add the personal endpoints**

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/MeService.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.LambdaUpdateWrapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.ChangePasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UpdateMeRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UserView;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;

/** 个人设置：修改自己的显示名称与密码。按令牌中的用户 id 操作（代入时即平台管理员本人）。 */
@Service
public class MeService {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MeService.class);

    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final SessionService sessions;
    private final Clock clock;

    public MeService(AppUserMapper users, PasswordEncoder encoder, SessionService sessions, Clock clock) {
        this.users = users;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.sessions = sessions;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /**
     * 修改自己的显示名称（去首尾空白，空白即清除）。
     *
     * @return 更新后的当前用户视图
     * @throws ApiException 400：超过 64 个字符
     */
    public UserView updateProfile(AuthPrincipal principal, UpdateMeRequest request) {
        String displayName = DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(request.displayName());
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, principal.userId())
                .set(AppUser::getDisplayName, displayName)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        return sessions.userView(principal);
    }

    /**
     * 修改自己的密码并清除「需改密码」标记。
     *
     * @return 新的会话身份（调用方据此签发不带 mcp 的令牌）
     * @throws ApiException 409：代入期间；401：用户已不可用；400：原密码错误、新密码不合规或与原密码相同
     */
    public AuthPrincipal changePassword(AuthPrincipal principal, ChangePasswordRequest request) {
        if (principal.impersonating()) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("代入租户期间不能修改密码，请先退出租户");
        }
        AppUser user = sessions.requireActive(users.selectById(principal.userId()));
        if (!encoder.matches(request.oldPassword(), user.getPasswordHash())) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("原密码错误");
        }
        PasswordPolicy.requireValid(request.newPassword());
        if (encoder.matches(request.newPassword(), user.getPasswordHash())) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("新密码不能与原密码相同");
        }
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, user.getId())
                .set(AppUser::getPasswordHash, encoder.encode(request.newPassword()))
                .set(AppUser::getMustChangePassword, false)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        log.info("User {} changed the password", user.getUsername());
        user.setMustChangePassword(false);
        return sessions.principalOf(user);
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/MeController.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.ChangePasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.TokenResponse;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UpdateMeRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.UserView;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

/** 个人接口（/api/v1/me，所有登录用户）：修改显示名称、修改密码；逻辑在 {@link MeService}。 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/me")
public class MeController {

    private final MeService service;
    private final SessionService sessions;

    public MeController(MeService service, SessionService sessions) {
        this.service = service;
        this.sessions = sessions;
    }

    /** 修改自己的显示名称，返回更新后的当前用户。 */
    @PutMapping
    public UserView update(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal principal,
                           @RequestBody UpdateMeRequest request) {
        return service.updateProfile(principal, request);
    }

    /** 修改自己的密码；成功后换发令牌（不再带需改密码标记）并写入新的 refresh Cookie。 */
    @PutMapping("/password")
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> changePassword(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal principal,
                                                        @Valid @RequestBody ChangePasswordRequest request) {
        return sessions.issue(service.changePassword(principal, request));
    }
}
```

## Task 3: Backend tenant user management `/api/v1/users`

Tenant admins (and impersonating platform admins) list, create, edit, disable/enable, reset and delete the users of their own tenant, with the self and last-admin protections of spec §4.2.

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TenantMapper.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserDtos.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserService.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserController.java`
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserApiIT.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 1–2): `AppUser`, `AppUserMapper`, `Roles`, `UsernamePolicy`, `PasswordPolicy`, `DisplayNamePolicy`, `AuthPrincipal.impersonating()/userId()`, `TenantContext.require()`, `PageView<T>(items, total, page, size)`, `IntegrationTestBase.createUser/login/loginResult/userId/PLATFORM_ADMIN`, `JwtService.issue`.
- Produces:
  - `TenantMapper.lockById(long id) → Long` (row lock, inside a transaction).
  - `UserDtos.UserItem(String username, String displayName, String role, String status, boolean mustChangePassword, OffsetDateTime createdAt)` with `static UserItem of(AppUser)` — Task 4 reuses it for `GET /platform/tenants/{code}/users`.
  - `UserDtos.CreateUserRequest(username, displayName, role, password)`, `UpdateUserRequest(displayName, role)`, `PasswordRequest(password)` — Task 4 reuses `PasswordRequest`.
  - Endpoints (all under `/api/v1/users`): `GET ?keyword&role&status&page&size` → `PageView<UserItem>`; `POST` → 201 `UserItem`; `PUT /{username}` → `UserItem`; `POST /{username}/disable|enable` → `UserItem`; `POST /{username}/reset-password` → 204; `DELETE /{username}` → 204.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `app_user` is not tenant-filtered, so every query in `UserService` adds `tenant_id = TenantContext.require()` explicitly; a user of another tenant is 404.
- Lists sort by role, then username (the same order as the platform's per-tenant list), 10 per page by default; `keyword` matches the username or the display name; `role` and `status` filters accept any case and must be valid values (400 otherwise).
- "Self" means `!actor.impersonating() && actor.userId() == target.id`. Self cannot be deleted, disabled or have its role changed; editing one's own display name with an unchanged role is fine; resetting one's own password through this endpoint is allowed (it sets the must-change flag).
- The last-admin rule is checked under a row lock on the tenant (`select … for update`), so two concurrent demotions cannot both pass. It applies when the target is an **active** `TENANT_ADMIN` and the operation leaves it not an active `TENANT_ADMIN` (demote, disable, delete).
- Enable/disable are idempotent (repeating them returns 200 with the current state).
- Changes reach the target's existing tokens at its next refresh (spec §3.2); `UserService` does not revoke tokens.

Create `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserApiIT.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.user;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthPrincipal;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.JwtService;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.SessionService;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import jakarta.servlet.http.Cookie;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 租户用户管理集成测试：新建（需改密码）、分页与筛选、输入规则与重名 409、编辑显示名称与角色、
 * 「自己」不能删除/禁用/改角色、最后一个启用的租户管理员不能被降级/禁用/删除（代入的平台管理员同样受限）、
 * 禁用后续期失败、重置密码、删除，以及其他租户的用户不可见（404）。每个测试使用自己新建的租户。
 */
class UserApiIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private static final String VIEWER_PASSWORD = "Viewer-pass-1";

    @Autowired
    JwtService jwt;

    private long tenant;
    private String admin;
    private String token;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        tenant = createTenant("ua-");
        admin = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);
        token = login(admin, TEST_PASSWORD);
    }

    private ResultActions createViaApi(String bearerToken, String username, String role, String password)
            throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/users").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(bearerToken))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"username\":\"%s\",\"displayName\":\"华东运维 张工\",\"role\":\"%s\",\"password\":\"%s\"}"
                        .formatted(username, role, password)));
    }

    private ResultActions action(String bearerToken, String username, String verb) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/users/{u}/" + verb, username).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(bearerToken)));
    }

    private ResultActions update(String bearerToken, String username, String displayName, String role) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/users/{u}", username).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(bearerToken))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"displayName\":\"%s\",\"role\":\"%s\"}".formatted(displayName, role)));
    }

    private String impersonationToken() {
        long adminId = userId(PLATFORM_ADMIN);
        return jwt.issue(new AuthPrincipal(adminId, tenant, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, adminId, false),
                JwtService.TokenType.ACCESS);
    }

    @Test
    void createsListsAndFilters() throws Exception {
        String viewer = unique("v");
        createViaApi(token, viewer, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, VIEWER_PASSWORD)
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.username").value(viewer))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.displayName").value("华东运维 张工"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.role").value(Roles.TENANT_VIEWER))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("ACTIVE"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mustChangePassword").value(true));

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(2))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.size").value(10))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[1].username").value(viewer));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").param("role", "tenant_viewer").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].username").value(viewer));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").param("keyword", "张工").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").param("status", "DISABLED").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(0));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").param("role", "PLATFORM_ADMIN").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidInputAndDuplicates() throws Exception {
        createViaApi(token, "1abc", Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        createViaApi(token, unique("v"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, "short1").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        createViaApi(token, unique("v"), Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        createViaApi(token, admin, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isConflict());
        createViaApi(token, PLATFORM_ADMIN, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }

    @Test
    void newUsersMustChangeTheirPassword() throws Exception {
        String viewer = unique("v");
        createViaApi(token, viewer, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isCreated());

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(login(viewer, VIEWER_PASSWORD))))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED"));
    }

    @Test
    void updatesTheDisplayNameAndTheRole() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        update(token, viewer, "华东运维 李工", Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.displayName").value("华东运维 李工"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN));
    }

    @Test
    void protectsTheActingAdmin() throws Exception {
        createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);

        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/users/{u}", admin).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
        action(token, admin, "disable").andExpect(status().isConflict());
        update(token, admin, "", Roles.TENANT_VIEWER).andExpect(status().isConflict());
        update(token, admin, "华东管理员", Roles.TENANT_ADMIN).andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    @Test
    void keepsTheLastActiveTenantAdmin() throws Exception {
        String platform = impersonationToken();

        action(platform, admin, "disable").andExpect(status().isConflict());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/users/{u}", admin).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(platform)))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
        update(platform, admin, "", Roles.TENANT_VIEWER).andExpect(status().isConflict());

        createViaApi(platform, unique("a"), Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, VIEWER_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        action(platform, admin, "disable").andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("DISABLED"));
        action(platform, admin, "enable").andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("ACTIVE"));
    }

    @Test
    void disablingAUserStopsItsNextRefresh() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);
        Cookie refresh = loginResult(viewer, TEST_PASSWORD).getResponse().getCookie(SessionService.REFRESH_COOKIE);

        action(token, viewer, "disable").andExpect(status().isOk());

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/refresh").cookie(refresh)).andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }

    @Test
    void resetsThePasswordAndRequiresAChange() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/users/{u}/reset-password", viewer).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"weak\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/users/{u}/reset-password", viewer).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"Reset-pass-9\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select must_change_password from app_user where username = ?",
                Boolean.class, viewer)).isTrue();
        login(viewer, "Reset-pass-9");
    }

    @Test
    void deletesUsers() throws Exception {
        String viewer = createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/users/{u}", viewer).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/users/{u}", viewer).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from app_user where username = ?", Integer.class, viewer))
                .isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void usersOfOtherTenantsAreInvisible() throws Exception {
        String foreign = createUser(createTenant("ub-"), Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        update(token, foreign, "", Roles.TENANT_ADMIN).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        action(token, foreign, "disable").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/users/{u}", foreign).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/users").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the tenant row lock**

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TenantMapper.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.tenant;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.InterceptorIgnore;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.mapper.BaseMapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;

/** {@link Tenant} 的 MyBatis-Plus Mapper（PostgreSQL 的 tenant 表，不做租户行过滤）。 */
@Mapper
public interface TenantMapper extends BaseMapper<Tenant> {

    /**
     * 在事务中锁定租户行（select … for update），串行化同一租户内的用户管理变更，
     * 保证「至少保留一个启用的租户管理员」不会被并发操作绕过。租户不存在时返回 null。
     */
    @InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")
    @Select("select id from tenant where id = #{id} for update")
    Long lockById(@Param("id") long id);
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.user;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;

import java.time.OffsetDateTime;

/** 用户管理接口的请求与响应对象集合（租户用户管理与平台查看租户用户共用 UserItem）。 */
public final class UserDtos {

    private UserDtos() {
    }

    /**
     * 用户列表项。
     *
     * @param displayName        显示名称；未设置为 null
     * @param role               TENANT_ADMIN 或 TENANT_VIEWER
     * @param status             ACTIVE 或 DISABLED
     * @param mustChangePassword 下次登录是否需要先修改密码
     */
    public record UserItem(String username, String displayName, String role, String status,
                           boolean mustChangePassword, OffsetDateTime createdAt) {

        /** 由实体组装列表项（不含任何密码信息）。 */
        public static UserItem of(AppUser u) {
            return new UserItem(u.getUsername(), u.getDisplayName(), u.getRole(), u.getStatus(),
                    Boolean.TRUE.equals(u.getMustChangePassword()), u.getCreatedAt());
        }
    }

    /** 新建用户；role 只能是 TENANT_ADMIN / TENANT_VIEWER，password 为初始密码（用户首次登录须修改）。 */
    public record CreateUserRequest(@NotBlank String username, String displayName, @NotBlank String role,
                                    @NotBlank String password) {
    }

    /** 修改用户的显示名称（空白即清除）与角色。 */
    public record UpdateUserRequest(String displayName, @NotBlank String role) {
    }

    /** 重置密码：新的初始密码（用户下次登录须修改）。 */
    public record PasswordRequest(@NotBlank String password) {
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserService.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.user;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.LambdaUpdateWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthPrincipal;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.DisplayNamePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.PasswordPolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.UsernamePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantContext;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.CreateUserRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.PasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UpdateUserRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UserItem;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import dev.conduit.server.web.PageView;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * 租户用户管理（当前租户，由令牌绑定的 TenantContext 决定）：列表、新建、编辑、启用/禁用、重置密码、删除。
 * app_user 不做租户行过滤，因此所有查询显式带 tenant_id。保护规则（409）：不能删除、禁用自己或修改自己的角色
 * （代入的平台管理员不算本租户用户，不受此限）；本租户最后一个启用的租户管理员不能被降级、禁用或删除
 * （在租户行锁内检查，防止并发绕过）。
 */
@Service
public class UserService {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(UserService.class);

    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final Clock clock;

    public UserService(AppUserMapper users, TenantMapper tenants, PasswordEncoder encoder, Clock clock) {
        this.users = users;
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /**
     * 分页列出当前租户的用户，按角色、用户名排序。
     *
     * @param keyword 匹配用户名或显示名称（可空）
     * @param role    TENANT_ADMIN / TENANT_VIEWER（不区分大小写，可空）
     * @param status  ACTIVE / DISABLED（不区分大小写，可空）
     * @throws ApiException 400：role 或 status 不合法
     */
    public PageView<UserItem> list(String keyword, String role, String status, int page, int size) {
        long tenantId = TenantContext.require();
        LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser> query = new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenantId);
        if (keyword != null && !keyword.isBlank()) {
            String kw = keyword.trim();
            query.and(q -> q.like(AppUser::getUsername, kw).or().like(AppUser::getDisplayName, kw));
        }
        if (role != null && !role.isBlank()) {
            query.eq(AppUser::getRole, requireTenantRole(role));
        }
        if (status != null && !status.isBlank()) {
            query.eq(AppUser::getStatus, requireStatus(status));
        }
        query.orderByAsc(AppUser::getRole).orderByAsc(AppUser::getUsername);
        Page<AppUser> result = users.selectPage(new Page<>(page, size), query);
        return new PageView<>(result.getRecords().stream().map(UserItem::of).toList(), result.getTotal(), page, size);
    }

    /**
     * 在当前租户新建用户，要求首次登录修改密码。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 400：用户名、角色、密码或显示名称不合规；409：用户名已存在（全平台唯一）
     */
    @Transactional
    public UserItem create(CreateUserRequest request) {
        long tenantId = TenantContext.require();
        String username = UsernamePolicy.requireValid(request.username());
        String role = requireTenantRole(request.role());
        PasswordPolicy.requireValid(request.password());
        String displayName = DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(request.displayName());
        if (users.exists(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getUsername, username))) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("用户名已存在: " + username);
        }
        AppUser user = new AppUser();
        user.setTenantId(tenantId);
        user.setUsername(username);
        user.setDisplayName(displayName);
        user.setPasswordHash(encoder.encode(request.password()));
        user.setRole(role);
        user.setStatus(AppUser.ACTIVE);
        user.setMustChangePassword(true);
        users.insert(user);
        log.info("Created user {} (role={}) in tenant id={}", username, role, tenantId);
        return UserItem.of(users.selectById(user.getId()));
    }

    /**
     * 修改显示名称与角色。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：用户不在当前租户；409：修改自己的角色，或降级最后一个启用的租户管理员
     */
    @Transactional
    public UserItem update(AuthPrincipal actor, String username, UpdateUserRequest request) {
        long tenantId = lockTenant();
        AppUser target = require(tenantId, username);
        String role = requireTenantRole(request.role());
        String displayName = DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(request.displayName());
        boolean roleChanged = !role.equals(target.getRole());
        if (roleChanged) {
            if (isSelf(actor, target)) {
                throw ApiException.conflict("不能修改自己的角色");
            }
            guardLastAdmin(tenantId, target, role, target.getStatus());
        }
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, target.getId())
                .set(AppUser::getDisplayName, displayName)
                .set(AppUser::getRole, role)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        if (roleChanged) {
            log.info("Changed role of user {} from {} to {} by {}", username, target.getRole(), role, actor.username());
        }
        return UserItem.of(users.selectById(target.getId()));
    }

    /**
     * 启用或禁用用户（重复操作幂等）。禁用在对方下次续期时生效。
     *
     * @param status ACTIVE 或 DISABLED
     * @throws ApiException 404：用户不在当前租户；409：禁用自己，或禁用最后一个启用的租户管理员
     */
    @Transactional
    public UserItem setStatus(AuthPrincipal actor, String username, String status) {
        long tenantId = lockTenant();
        AppUser target = require(tenantId, username);
        if (AppUser.DISABLED.equals(status) && AppUser.ACTIVE.equals(target.getStatus())) {
            if (isSelf(actor, target)) {
                throw ApiException.conflict("不能禁用自己");
            }
            guardLastAdmin(tenantId, target, target.getRole(), AppUser.DISABLED);
        }
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, target.getId())
                .set(AppUser::getStatus, status)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        log.info("Set status of user {} to {} by {}", username, status, actor.username());
        return UserItem.of(users.selectById(target.getId()));
    }

    /**
     * 重置密码并要求对方下次登录修改。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：用户不在当前租户；400：密码不合规
     */
    @Transactional
    public void resetPassword(AuthPrincipal actor, String username, PasswordRequest request) {
        long tenantId = TenantContext.require();
        AppUser target = require(tenantId, username);
        PasswordPolicy.requireValid(request.password());
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, target.getId())
                .set(AppUser::getPasswordHash, encoder.encode(request.password()))
                .set(AppUser::getMustChangePassword, true)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        log.info("Reset password of user {} by {}", username, actor.username());
    }

    /**
     * 删除用户。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：用户不在当前租户；409：删除自己，或删除最后一个启用的租户管理员
     */
    @Transactional
    public void delete(AuthPrincipal actor, String username) {
        long tenantId = lockTenant();
        AppUser target = require(tenantId, username);
        if (isSelf(actor, target)) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("不能删除自己");
        }
        guardLastAdmin(tenantId, target, null, null);
        users.deleteById(target.getId());
        log.info("Deleted user {} from tenant id={} by {}", username, tenantId, actor.username());
    }

    /** 锁定当前租户行并返回租户 id（必须在事务中调用）。 */
    private long lockTenant() {
        long tenantId = TenantContext.require();
        tenants.lockById(tenantId);
        return tenantId;
    }

    /** 取当前租户中的用户，不存在（含属于其他租户、平台管理员）抛 404。 */
    private AppUser require(long tenantId, String username) {
        AppUser user = users.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenantId)
                .eq(AppUser::getUsername, username));
        if (user == null) {
            throw ApiException.notFound("用户不存在: " + username);
        }
        return user;
    }

    /**
     * 「最后一个启用的租户管理员」保护：目标当前是启用的租户管理员、而操作后不再是（newRole/newStatus 为 null 表示删除）时，
     * 若本租户启用的租户管理员只剩它一个则抛 409。
     */
    private void guardLastAdmin(long tenantId, AppUser target, String newRole, String newStatus) {
        boolean activeAdminNow = Roles.TENANT_ADMIN.equals(target.getRole()) && AppUser.ACTIVE.equals(target.getStatus());
        boolean activeAdminAfter = Roles.TENANT_ADMIN.equals(newRole) && AppUser.ACTIVE.equals(newStatus);
        if (!activeAdminNow || activeAdminAfter) {
            return;
        }
        long activeAdmins = users.selectCount(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenantId)
                .eq(AppUser::getRole, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)
                .eq(AppUser::getStatus, AppUser.ACTIVE));
        if (activeAdmins <= 1) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("租户至少需要保留一个启用的租户管理员");
        }
    }

    /** 操作者是否就是目标用户本人（代入的平台管理员不算本租户用户）。 */
    static boolean isSelf(AuthPrincipal actor, AppUser target) {
        return !actor.impersonating() && actor.userId() == target.getId();
    }

    /** 校验租户角色（不区分大小写），返回大写值；非法抛 400。 */
    static String requireTenantRole(String role) {
        String upper = role == null ? null : role.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!Roles.isTenantRole(upper)) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("角色只能是 TENANT_ADMIN 或 TENANT_VIEWER");
        }
        return upper;
    }

    /** 校验用户状态（不区分大小写），返回大写值；非法抛 400。 */
    static String requireStatus(String status) {
        String upper = status.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!AppUser.ACTIVE.equals(upper) && !AppUser.DISABLED.equals(upper)) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("状态只能是 ACTIVE 或 DISABLED");
        }
        return upper;
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/user/UserController.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.user;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthPrincipal;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.CreateUserRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.PasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UpdateUserRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UserItem;
import dev.conduit.server.web.PageView;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.DeleteMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ResponseStatus;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

/**
 * 租户用户管理 REST 接口（/api/v1/users，租户管理员与代入的平台管理员，权限在 SecurityConfig）；逻辑在 {@link UserService}。
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/users")
public class UserController {

    private final UserService service;

    public UserController(UserService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }

    /** 分页列出当前租户的用户（默认每页 10 条）；可按关键字、角色、状态筛选。 */
    @GetMapping
    public PageView<UserItem> list(@RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) String role,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") @Min(1) int page,
                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") @Min(1) @Max(100) int size) {
        return service.list(keyword, role, status, page, size);
    }

    @PostMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public UserItem create(@Valid @RequestBody CreateUserRequest request) {
        return service.create(request);
    }

    @PutMapping("/{username}")
    public UserItem update(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor, @PathVariable String username,
                           @Valid @RequestBody UpdateUserRequest request) {
        return service.update(actor, username, request);
    }

    @PostMapping("/{username}/disable")
    public UserItem disable(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor, @PathVariable String username) {
        return service.setStatus(actor, username, AppUser.DISABLED);
    }

    @PostMapping("/{username}/enable")
    public UserItem enable(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor, @PathVariable String username) {
        return service.setStatus(actor, username, AppUser.ACTIVE);
    }

    @PostMapping("/{username}/reset-password")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void resetPassword(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor, @PathVariable String username,
                              @Valid @RequestBody PasswordRequest request) {
        service.resetPassword(actor, username, request);
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{username}")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void delete(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor, @PathVariable String username) {
        service.delete(actor, username);
    }
}
```

## Task 4: Backend platform APIs: overview, tenant lifecycle, tenant users, enter, impersonation logging

Adds `/api/v1/platform/**` (spec §4.1), cross-tenant statistics, cache eviction when a tenant is disabled or enabled, the INFO line per impersonated write (spec §3.5), and the two-tenant isolation acceptance test (spec §6).

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceIdentityCache.java` (add `evictAll`)
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformDtos.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformStatsMapper.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantStats.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformOverviewService.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantAdminService.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformController.java`
- Create: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/ImpersonationLogFilter.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SecurityConfig.java` (register the filter)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantStatsTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformTenantApiIT.java`, `PlatformOverviewApiIT.java`, `ImpersonationIT.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TwoTenantIsolationIT.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 1–3): `Tenant` (+ constants), `TenantMapper`, `TenantCodePolicy`, `AppUser`, `AppUserMapper`, `Roles`, `UsernamePolicy`, `PasswordPolicy`, `DisplayNamePolicy`, `SessionService.requireActive/impersonation/issue`, `AuthPrincipal`, `UserDtos.UserItem.of`, `UserDtos.PasswordRequest`, `ProductMapper`, `DeviceMapper`, `PresenceStore.getAll(List<String>) → Map<String, Presence>`, `Presence.online()`, `TenantContext.callAs(long, Supplier<T>)`, `PageView`, `IntegrationTestBase` helpers.
- Produces:
  - `DeviceIdentityCache.evictAll(Collection<String> deviceKeys)`.
  - `PlatformDtos.PlatformOverview(int tenantCount, int activeTenantCount, int disabledTenantCount, int userCount, int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount, List<TenantStat> topTenants)`; `TenantStat(String code, String name, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount)`; `TenantView(String code, String name, String status, int adminCount, int userCount, int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount, OffsetDateTime createdAt)`; `CreateTenantRequest(String code, String name, AdminAccount admin)`; `AdminAccount(String username, String displayName, String password)`; `UpdateTenantRequest(String name)`.
  - `PlatformStatsMapper.TenantCount(long tenantId, long n)`, `TenantDevice(long tenantId, String deviceKey)`; `productCounts()`, `devices()`, `deviceKeysOf(long tenantId)`.
  - `TenantStats.snapshot() → Snapshot`; `Snapshot.of(long tenantId) → TenantCounts(int userCount, int adminCount, int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount)`, `userCount()`, `productCount()`, `deviceCount()`, `onlineCount()` (`Integer`, null when Redis is down).
  - `TenantAdminService`: `list`, `create`, `rename`, `setStatus`, `delete`, `users`, `resetAdminPassword`, `enter(AuthPrincipal, String) → AuthPrincipal`, `require(String) → Tenant`.
  - Endpoints: `GET /platform/overview`; `GET /platform/tenants?keyword&status&page&size` → `PageView<TenantView>`; `POST /platform/tenants` → 201 `TenantView`; `PUT /platform/tenants/{code}` → `TenantView`; `POST /platform/tenants/{code}/disable|enable` → `TenantView`; `DELETE /platform/tenants/{code}` → 204; `GET /platform/tenants/{code}/users` → `UserItem[]`; `POST /platform/tenants/{code}/users/{username}/reset-password` → 204; `POST /platform/tenants/{code}/enter` → 200 `TokenResponse`.
  - Log lines (tests grep them): `Platform admin {username} entered tenant {code}`; `Impersonated write by platform admin {username} in tenant {code}: {METHOD} {path} -> {status}`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- Cross-tenant reads are explicit: `PlatformStatsMapper` methods carry `@InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")`; per-tenant checks bind the tenant with `TenantContext.callAs` (the "has products or devices" check on delete). `tenant` and `app_user` are not tenant-filtered anyway.
- `TenantStats` reads all tenant users, per-tenant product counts and all device keys once, then one Redis pipeline for presence. That is O(devices) per call — fine for this personal platform; recorded in the backlog (Task 9). The tenant list computes one snapshot per page request.
- `userCount` on the platform overview counts tenant users only (platform admins excluded); `adminCount` counts all `TENANT_ADMIN` users of the tenant (active or not).
- `topTenants`: all tenants sorted by device count (descending), ties by code, first 5 — tenants without devices can appear when there are fewer than 5 with devices.
- Tenant list: newest first (`id desc`), `keyword` matches name or code, `status` must be `ACTIVE`/`DISABLED` (any case; 400 otherwise). Tenant name: trimmed, 1–64 characters.
- Create checks the code (409 「租户编码已存在」), inserts the tenant, **then** checks the admin username (409 「用户名已存在」) and inserts the admin, all in one transaction — the username failure therefore exercises the rollback that spec §6 asks to test (no tenant row left behind).
- Disable/enable return the updated `TenantView`, are idempotent, and both evict `conduit:auth:{deviceKey}` for every device of the tenant (best effort: a Redis failure is logged at WARN; the 5-minute TTL is the fallback). Enable also evicts, so a cached "tenant disabled" identity from Task 5 does not outlive re-enabling.
- Delete removes the tenant's users explicitly before the tenant row (the FK cascade from Task 1 is the backstop).
- `enter`: 404 unknown tenant, 409 disabled tenant; the caller must still be an active platform admin (401 otherwise). The response sets the impersonation refresh cookie, so a page reload stays inside the tenant.
- `ImpersonationLogFilter` logs after the request completes (so the line carries the final status, including 400/403/409), skips `/api/v1/auth/**` (enter/exit have their own lines) and never logs bodies or query strings.

Create `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantStatsTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformStatsMapper.TenantCount;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformStatsMapper.TenantDevice;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.TenantStats.Snapshot;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.TenantStats.TenantCounts;
import dev.conduit.server.presence.Presence;
import dev.conduit.server.presence.PresenceStore;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.redis.RedisConnectionFailureException;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyList;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/** TenantStats 单元测试：按租户统计用户、管理员、产品、设备与在线数；Redis 不可用时在线数为 null；没有设备时不读 Redis。 */
class TenantStatsTest {

    private final AppUserMapper users = mock(AppUserMapper.class);
    private final PlatformStatsMapper statsMapper = mock(PlatformStatsMapper.class);
    private final PresenceStore presence = mock(PresenceStore.class);
    private final TenantStats stats = new TenantStats(users, statsMapper, presence);

    private static AppUser user(long tenantId, String role) {
        AppUser u = new AppUser();
        u.setTenantId(tenantId);
        u.setRole(role);
        return u;
    }

    private void givenThreeTenants() {
        when(users.selectList(any())).thenReturn(List.of(
                user(1, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN), user(1, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER), user(2, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN)));
        when(statsMapper.productCounts()).thenReturn(List.of(new TenantCount(1, 2), new TenantCount(3, 1)));
        when(statsMapper.devices()).thenReturn(List.of(
                new TenantDevice(1, "a"), new TenantDevice(1, "b"), new TenantDevice(3, "c")));
    }

    @Test
    void countsPerTenant() {
        givenThreeTenants();
        when(presence.getAll(List.of("a", "b", "c"))).thenReturn(Map.of(
                "a", new Presence(true, 1, ""), "c", new Presence(false, 1, "")));

        Snapshot s = stats.snapshot();

        assertThat(s.of(1)).isEqualTo(new TenantCounts(2, 1, 2, 2, 1));
        assertThat(s.of(2)).isEqualTo(new TenantCounts(1, 1, 0, 0, 0));
        assertThat(s.of(3)).isEqualTo(new TenantCounts(0, 0, 1, 1, 0));
        assertThat(s.of(99)).isEqualTo(new TenantCounts(0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
        assertThat(s.userCount()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(s.productCount()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(s.deviceCount()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(s.onlineCount()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void redisUnavailableLeavesOnlineCountsNull() {
        givenThreeTenants();
        when(presence.getAll(anyList())).thenThrow(new RedisConnectionFailureException("down"));

        Snapshot s = stats.snapshot();

        assertThat(s.onlineCount()).isNull();
        assertThat(s.of(1)).isEqualTo(new TenantCounts(2, 1, 2, 2, null));
        assertThat(s.of(99).onlineCount()).isNull();
        assertThat(s.deviceCount()).isEqualTo(3);
    }

    @Test
    void noDevicesDoesNotTouchRedis() {
        when(users.selectList(any())).thenReturn(List.of());
        when(statsMapper.productCounts()).thenReturn(List.of());
        when(statsMapper.devices()).thenReturn(List.of());

        Snapshot s = stats.snapshot();

        assertThat(s.onlineCount()).isZero();
        verify(presence, never()).getAll(anyList());
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformStatsMapper.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.InterceptorIgnore;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Mapper;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Select;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 平台级跨租户统计查询。每个方法都显式绕过租户行过滤（@InterceptorIgnore），只供平台管理员接口
 * （平台概况、租户列表、停用时清缓存）在内部使用，禁止用于租户内接口。
 */
@Mapper
public interface PlatformStatsMapper {

    /** 某租户的计数（一行）。 */
    record TenantCount(long tenantId, long n) {
    }

    /** 某租户的一台设备。 */
    record TenantDevice(long tenantId, String deviceKey) {
    }

    /** 各租户的产品数（没有产品的租户不出现）。 */
    @InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")
    @Select("select tenant_id, count(*) as n from product group by tenant_id")
    List<TenantCount> productCounts();

    /** 全平台设备的租户与 deviceKey。 */
    @InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")
    @Select("select tenant_id, device_key from device")
    List<TenantDevice> devices();

    /** 指定租户全部设备的 deviceKey（停用/启用租户时清除身份缓存用）。 */
    @InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")
    @Select("select device_key from device where tenant_id = #{tenantId}")
    List<String> deviceKeysOf(@Param("tenantId") long tenantId);
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantStats.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformStatsMapper.TenantCount;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformStatsMapper.TenantDevice;
import dev.conduit.server.presence.Presence;
import dev.conduit.server.presence.PresenceStore;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 按租户统计用户数、租户管理员数、产品数、设备数与在线设备数（跨租户，供平台概况与租户列表使用）。
 * 用户、产品、设备来自 PostgreSQL，在线状态来自 Redis（一次管道批量读取）；Redis 不可用时在线数为 null，其余照常。
 * 本期读出全平台设备再统计，开销为 O(设备数)，规模变大时改增量计数（见 backlog）。
 */
@Component
public class TenantStats {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TenantStats.class);

    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final PlatformStatsMapper statsMapper;
    private final PresenceStore presence;

    public TenantStats(AppUserMapper users, PlatformStatsMapper statsMapper, PresenceStore presence) {
        this.users = users;
        this.statsMapper = statsMapper;
        this.presence = presence;
    }

    /**
     * 一个租户的计数。
     *
     * @param userCount   租户用户数（不含平台管理员）
     * @param adminCount  租户管理员数（含已禁用的）
     * @param onlineCount 在线设备数；Redis 不可用时为 null
     */
    public record TenantCounts(int userCount, int adminCount, int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount) {
    }

    /**
     * 一次统计的结果。
     *
     * @param byTenant    有数据的租户的计数，键为租户 id
     * @param onlineCount 全平台在线设备数；Redis 不可用时为 null
     */
    public record Snapshot(Map<Long, TenantCounts> byTenant, Integer onlineCount) {

        /** 指定租户的计数；没有任何数据的租户为全 0（Redis 不可用时在线数为 null）。 */
        public TenantCounts of(long tenantId) {
            return byTenant.getOrDefault(tenantId, new TenantCounts(0, 0, 0, 0, onlineCount == null ? null : 0));
        }

        /** 全平台租户用户数。 */
        public int userCount() {
            return byTenant.values().stream().mapToInt(TenantCounts::userCount).sum();
        }

        /** 全平台产品数。 */
        public int productCount() {
            return byTenant.values().stream().mapToInt(TenantCounts::productCount).sum();
        }

        /** 全平台设备数。 */
        public int deviceCount() {
            return byTenant.values().stream().mapToInt(TenantCounts::deviceCount).sum();
        }
    }

    /** 统计全平台（规则见类注释）。 */
    public Snapshot snapshot() {
        Map<Long, int[]> acc = new HashMap<>();
        List<AppUser> tenantUsers = users.selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>()
                .select(AppUser::getTenantId, AppUser::getRole)
                .isNotNull(AppUser::getTenantId));
        for (AppUser u : tenantUsers) {
            int[] a = slot(acc, u.getTenantId());
            a[0]++;
            if (Roles.TENANT_ADMIN.equals(u.getRole())) {
                a[1]++;
            }
        }
        for (TenantCount c : statsMapper.productCounts()) {
            slot(acc, c.tenantId())[2] += (int) c.n();
        }
        List<TenantDevice> devices = statsMapper.devices();
        for (TenantDevice d : devices) {
            slot(acc, d.tenantId())[3]++;
        }
        Set<String> online = onlineKeys(devices);
        if (online != null) {
            for (TenantDevice d : devices) {
                if (online.contains(d.deviceKey())) {
                    slot(acc, d.tenantId())[4]++;
                }
            }
        }
        Map<Long, TenantCounts> byTenant = new HashMap<>();
        acc.forEach((tenantId, a) -> byTenant.put(tenantId,
                new TenantCounts(a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3], online == null ? null : a[4])));
        return new Snapshot(Map.copyOf(byTenant), online == null ? null : online.size());
    }

    /** 取某租户的累加槽位：[用户, 管理员, 产品, 设备, 在线]。 */
    private static int[] slot(Map<Long, int[]> acc, long tenantId) {
        return acc.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, id -> new int[5]);
    }

    /** 在线设备集合；没有设备时不访问 Redis 直接返回空集合；Redis 不可用时记录告警并返回 null。 */
    private Set<String> onlineKeys(List<TenantDevice> devices) {
        if (devices.isEmpty()) {
            return Set.of();
        }
        try {
            Map<String, Presence> states = presence.getAll(devices.stream().map(TenantDevice::deviceKey).toList());
            Set<String> online = new HashSet<>();
            states.forEach((deviceKey, p) -> {
                if (p.online()) {
                    online.add(deviceKey);
                }
            });
            return online;
        } catch (DataAccessException e) {
            log.warn("Presence store unavailable, platform online counts omitted", e);
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```
