```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.device.DeviceIdentityCache;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 平台租户管理集成测试：新建租户与首个管理员（事务性，管理员需改密码）、输入规则与重复 409、改名、按状态与关键字筛选、
 * 停用（登录被拒、清除设备身份缓存）与启用、默认租户不能停用/启用/删除、删除（有数据 409、用户随之删除）、
 * 查看租户用户与只重置租户管理员的密码、未知租户 404。
 */
class PlatformTenantApiIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private static final String INITIAL_PASSWORD = "Init-pass-2026";

    private String sys;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        sys = loginAsPlatformAdmin();
    }

    private ResultActions createViaApi(String code, String name, String adminUsername, String password) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content(("{\"code\":\"%s\",\"name\":\"%s\",\"admin\":{\"username\":\"%s\","
                        + "\"displayName\":\"华东管理员\",\"password\":\"%s\"}}")
                        .formatted(code, name, adminUsername, password)));
    }

    private ResultActions call(String path, Object... vars) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post(path, vars).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)));
    }

    private int tenantRows(String code) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from tenant where code = ?", Integer.class, code);
    }

    @Test
    void createsATenantWithItsFirstAdmin() throws Exception {
        String code = unique("t-");
        String admin = unique("a");

        createViaApi(code, "验收租户（华东）", admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD)
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value(code))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.name").value("验收租户（华东）"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("ACTIVE"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.adminCount").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.userCount").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.productCount").value(0))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.deviceCount").value(0))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.onlineCount").value(0));

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants").param("keyword", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.size").value(10))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].code").value(code));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/users", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].username").value(admin))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].displayName").value("华东管理员"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].mustChangePassword").value(true));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(login(admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD))))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED"));
    }

    @Test
    void creationIsAtomic() throws Exception {
        String code = unique("t-");

        createViaApi(code, "管理员重名的租户", PLATFORM_ADMIN, INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isConflict());

        assertThat(tenantRows(code)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidInputAndDuplicateCodes() throws Exception {
        createViaApi("Bad_Code", "非法编码", unique("a"), INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        createViaApi(unique("t-"), "弱密码", unique("a"), "weak").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        createViaApi(unique("t-"), "非法用户名", "1abc", INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());

        String code = unique("t-");
        createViaApi(code, "验收租户（华东）", unique("a"), INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        createViaApi(code, "重复编码", unique("a"), INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }

    @Test
    void renamesATenant() throws Exception {
        String code = tenantCode(createTenant("rn-"));

        mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\" 验收租户（华南） \"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.name").value("验收租户（华南）"));
    }

    @Test
    void filtersByStatusAndKeyword() throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("fs-");
        String code = tenantCode(id);
        jdbc.update("update tenant set status = 'DISABLED' where id = ?", id);

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants").param("keyword", code).param("status", "disabled")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].status").value("DISABLED"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants").param("keyword", code).param("status", "ACTIVE")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(0));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants").param("status", "bogus").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void disablingBlocksLoginAndEnablingRestoresIt() throws Exception {
        String code = unique("t-");
        String admin = unique("a");
        createViaApi(code, "验收租户（华东）", admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isCreated());

        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/disable", code)
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("DISABLED"));
        assertThat(loginResult(admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD).getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);

        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enable", code)
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("ACTIVE"));
        assertThat(loginResult(admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD).getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
    }

    @Test
    void disablingEvictsTheDeviceIdentityCache() throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("dc-");
        TestDevice device = createDevice(id, "ENABLED");
        String key = DeviceIdentityCache.key(device.deviceKey());
        redis.opsForValue().set(key, "{}");

        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/disable", tenantCode(id)).andExpect(status().isOk());

        assertThat(redis.hasKey(key)).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void theDefaultTenantCannotBeDisabledEnabledOrDeleted() throws Exception {
        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/disable", "default").andExpect(status().isConflict());
        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enable", "default").andExpect(status().isConflict());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}", "default").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }

    @Test
    void deletingNeedsAnEmptyTenantAndRemovesItsUsers() throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("dl-");
        String code = tenantCode(id);
        String user = createUser(id, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);
        TestDevice device = createDevice(id, "ENABLED");

        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());

        jdbc.update("delete from device where device_key = ?", device.deviceKey());
        jdbc.update("delete from product where product_key = ?", device.productKey());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        assertThat(tenantRows(code)).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from app_user where username = ?", Integer.class, user)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void listsTenantUsersAndResetsOnlyTenantAdmins() throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("rp-");
        String code = tenantCode(id);
        String admin = createUser(id, Roles.TENANT_ADMIN, false);
        String viewer = createUser(id, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/users", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.length()").value(2))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].username").value(admin))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[1].username").value(viewer));

        String reset = "/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/users/{u}/reset-password";
        mvc.perform(post(reset, code, viewer).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"Reset-pass-9\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
        mvc.perform(post(reset, code, admin).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"weak\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(post(reset, code, "nobody").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"Reset-pass-9\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(post(reset, code, admin).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"password\":\"Reset-pass-9\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select must_change_password from app_user where username = ?",
                Boolean.class, admin)).isTrue();
        login(admin, "Reset-pass-9");
    }

    @Test
    void unknownTenantsAreNotFound() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/users", "no-such-tenant").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/disable", "no-such-tenant").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        call("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enter", "no-such-tenant").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/** 平台概况集成测试：各计数与数据库一致（共享库，按当时的库内数量比较）；设备最多的租户最多 5 个、按设备数降序；租户用户 403。 */
class PlatformOverviewApiIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private int count(String sql) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject(sql, Integer.class);
    }

    @Test
    void countsMatchTheDatabase() throws Exception {
        long tenant = createTenant("po-");
        createDevice(tenant, "ENABLED");
        createUser(tenant, Roles.TENANT_VIEWER, false);

        String body = mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/overview").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsPlatformAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.tenantCount")).isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from tenant"));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.activeTenantCount"))
                .isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from tenant where status = 'ACTIVE'"));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.disabledTenantCount"))
                .isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from tenant where status = 'DISABLED'"));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.userCount"))
                .isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from app_user where tenant_id is not null"));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.productCount")).isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from product"));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.deviceCount")).isEqualTo(count("select count(*) from device"));
        assertThat((Object) JsonPath.read(body, "$.onlineCount")).isNotNull();

        List<Integer> top = JsonPath.read(body, "$.topTenants[*].deviceCount");
        assertThat(top).hasSizeBetween(1, 5);
        List<Integer> sorted = new ArrayList<>(top);
        sorted.sort(Comparator.reverseOrder());
        assertThat(top).isEqualTo(sorted);
    }

    @Test
    void tenantUsersAreForbidden() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/overview").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden());
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.hamcrest.Matchers;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.header;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 进入租户集成测试：平台管理员进入后以租户管理员身份读写目标租户、看不到其他租户，写请求与进入各记一行 INFO；
 * 退出后回到平台身份；停用的租户不能进入；代入令牌不能访问平台接口；租户用户不能进入。
 */
@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
class ImpersonationIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private String enter(String code) throws Exception {
        String body = mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enter", code)
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsPlatformAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(HttpHeaders.SET_COOKIE, Matchers.containsString("conduit_refresh=")))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.impersonatedBy").value(PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.tenantCode").value(code))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return JsonPath.read(body, "$.accessToken");
    }

    @Test
    void entersWritesOnlyTheTargetTenantAndLogsWrites(CapturedOutput output) throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("im-");
        String code = tenantCode(id);
        TestDevice foreign = createDevice(defaultTenantId(), "ENABLED");
        String token = enter(code);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\"验收电表（代入）\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated());

        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("select count(*) from product where tenant_id = ?", Integer.class, id)).isEqualTo(1);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products/{pk}", foreign.productKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        assertThat(output)
                .contains("Platform admin " + PLATFORM_ADMIN + " entered tenant " + code)
                .contains("Impersonated write by platform admin " + PLATFORM_ADMIN + " in tenant " + code
                        + ": POST /api/v1/products -> 201");

        jdbc.update("delete from product where tenant_id = ?", id);
    }

    @Test
    void exitReturnsToThePlatform() throws Exception {
        String token = enter(tenantCode(createTenant("im-")));

        String body = mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user.role").value(Roles.PLATFORM_ADMIN))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/overview")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(JsonPath.read(body, "$.accessToken"))))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    @Test
    void disabledTenantsCannotBeEntered() throws Exception {
        long id = createTenant("im-");
        jdbc.update("update tenant set status = 'DISABLED' where id = ?", id);

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enter", tenantCode(id))
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsPlatformAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }

    @Test
    void impersonationTokensCannotUseThePlatformApi() throws Exception {
        String token = enter(tenantCode(createTenant("im-")));

        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/platform/tenants").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden());
    }

    @Test
    void tenantUsersCannotEnter() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enter", tenantCode(createTenant("im-")))
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden());
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TwoTenantIsolationIT.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.tenant;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import dev.conduit.server.support.IntegrationTestBase;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

/**
 * 两租户隔离验收测试（路线图 V0.2 验收）：经平台接口新建两个租户，各自管理员改密码后建一个产品与一台设备；
 * 双方的产品、设备、最新值、历史与首页统计互不可见。测试结束经接口删除设备、产品与租户。
 */
class TwoTenantIsolationIT extends IntegrationTestBase {

    private static final String INITIAL_PASSWORD = "Init-pass-2026";
    private static final String ACTIVE_PASSWORD = "Active-pass-2026";

    /** 一个准备好的租户：编码、管理员令牌、产品与设备。 */
    record Workspace(String code, String token, String productKey, String deviceKey) {
    }

    private String body(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions actions) throws Exception {
        return actions.andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private Workspace workspace(String tenantName, String productName, String deviceName) throws Exception {
        String sys = loginAsPlatformAdmin();
        String code = unique("iso-");
        String admin = unique("a");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(("{\"code\":\"%s\",\"name\":\"%s\",\"admin\":{\"username\":\"%s\",\"password\":\"%s\"}}")
                                .formatted(code, tenantName, admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated());
        String first = login(admin, INITIAL_PASSWORD);
        String token = JsonPath.read(body(mvc.perform(put("/api/v1/me/password")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(first))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{\"oldPassword\":\"%s\",\"newPassword\":\"%s\"}".formatted(INITIAL_PASSWORD, ACTIVE_PASSWORD)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())), "$.accessToken");
        String productKey = JsonPath.read(body(mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/products")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\"%s\"}".formatted(productName)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())), "$.productKey");
        String deviceKey = JsonPath.read(body(mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/devices")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{\"productKey\":\"%s\",\"name\":\"%s\"}".formatted(productKey, deviceName)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())), "$.device.deviceKey");
        return new Workspace(code, token, productKey, deviceKey);
    }

    private void assertSeesOnlyItsOwn(Workspace me, Workspace other) throws Exception {
        String auth = bearer(me.token());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].productKey").value(me.productKey()));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/products/{pk}", other.productKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/devices").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.total").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].deviceKey").value(me.deviceKey()));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/devices/{dk}", other.deviceKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/devices/{dk}/latest", other.deviceKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/devices/{dk}/history", other.deviceKey()).param("properties", "Ua")
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/overview").header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, auth))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.productCount").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.deviceCount").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.products[0].productKey").value(me.productKey()));
    }

    private void tearDown(Workspace w) throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/devices/{dk}", w.deviceKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(w.token())))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/products/{pk}", w.productKey()).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(w.token())))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}", w.code())
                        .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(loginAsPlatformAdmin())))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());
    }

    @Test
    void twoTenantsSeeOnlyTheirOwnData() throws Exception {
        Workspace east = workspace("验收租户（华东）", "华东电表", "华东1号楼 电表01");
        Workspace south = workspace("验收租户（华南）", "华南水表", "华南1号楼 水表01");

        assertSeesOnlyItsOwn(east, south);
        assertSeesOnlyItsOwn(south, east);

        tearDown(east);
        tearDown(south);
    }
}
```

```java

    /**
     * 批量删除缓存项（租户停用/启用时清除该租户全部设备的身份，使鉴权立即按新状态判断）；空集合不访问 Redis。
     * Redis 异常（DataAccessException）原样抛出，由调用方决定是否忽略。
     */
    public void evictAll(Collection<String> deviceKeys) {
        if (deviceKeys.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        redis.delete(deviceKeys.stream().map(DeviceIdentityCache::key).toList());
    }
```

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;

import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/** 平台接口（/api/v1/platform/**）的请求与响应对象集合。在线数在 Redis 不可用时为 null。 */
public final class PlatformDtos {

    private PlatformDtos() {
    }

    /** 设备最多的租户中的一项。 */
    public record TenantStat(String code, String name, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount) {
    }

    /**
     * 平台概况。
     *
     * @param userCount  租户用户总数（不含平台管理员）
     * @param topTenants 设备数最多的 5 个租户（设备数降序，相同时按编码）
     */
    public record PlatformOverview(int tenantCount, int activeTenantCount, int disabledTenantCount, int userCount,
                                   int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount,
                                   List<TenantStat> topTenants) {
    }

    /**
     * 租户列表项与详情。
     *
     * @param status     ACTIVE 或 DISABLED
     * @param adminCount 租户管理员数（含已禁用）
     */
    public record TenantView(String code, String name, String status, int adminCount, int userCount,
                             int productCount, int deviceCount, Integer onlineCount, OffsetDateTime createdAt) {
    }

    /** 新租户的首个管理员（首次登录须修改密码）。 */
    public record AdminAccount(@NotBlank String username, String displayName, @NotBlank String password) {
    }

    /** 新建租户：编码、名称与首个管理员，同一事务创建。 */
    public record CreateTenantRequest(@NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String name,
                                      @Valid @NotNull AdminAccount admin) {
    }

    /** 修改租户名称（编码不可修改）。 */
    public record UpdateTenantRequest(@NotBlank String name) {
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformOverviewService.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.PlatformOverview;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.TenantStat;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.TenantStats.TenantCounts;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

/** 平台概况：租户数（启用/停用）、租户用户数、产品数、设备数、在线数与设备最多的 5 个租户（跨租户只读统计）。 */
@Service
public class PlatformOverviewService {

    static final int TOP_TENANTS = 5;

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(PlatformOverviewService.class);

    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final TenantStats stats;

    public PlatformOverviewService(TenantMapper tenants, TenantStats stats) {
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.stats = stats;
    }

    /**
     * 组装平台概况。
     *
     * @return 概况；Redis 不可用时 onlineCount 与 topTenants[].onlineCount 为 null
     */
    public PlatformOverview overview() {
        long start = System.nanoTime();
        List<Tenant> all = tenants.selectList(null);
        int active = (int) all.stream().filter(t -> Tenant.ACTIVE.equals(t.getStatus())).count();
        TenantStats.Snapshot snapshot = stats.snapshot();
        List<TenantStat> top = all.stream()
                .map(t -> {
                    TenantCounts c = snapshot.of(t.getId());
                    return new TenantStat(t.getCode(), t.getName(), c.deviceCount(), c.onlineCount());
                })
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(TenantStat::deviceCount).reversed().thenComparing(TenantStat::code))
                .limit(TOP_TENANTS)
                .toList();
        PlatformOverview view = new PlatformOverview(all.size(), active, all.size() - active, snapshot.userCount(),
                snapshot.productCount(), snapshot.deviceCount(), snapshot.onlineCount(), top);
        log.debug("Platform overview: tenants={} users={} devices={} online={} elapsedMs={}", all.size(),
                view.userCount(), view.deviceCount(), view.onlineCount(), (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000);
        return view;
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/TenantAdminService.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.LambdaUpdateWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUser;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AppUserMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthPrincipal;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.DisplayNamePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.PasswordPolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.Roles;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.SessionService;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.UsernamePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.device.DeviceIdentityCache;
import dev.conduit.server.device.DeviceMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.AdminAccount;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.CreateTenantRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.TenantView;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.UpdateTenantRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.product.ProductMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantCodePolicy;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantContext;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.PasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UserItem;
import dev.conduit.server.web.ApiException;
import dev.conduit.server.web.PageView;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * 平台管理员的租户管理：列表、新建（含首个管理员）、改名、停用/启用、删除、查看租户用户、重置租户管理员密码、进入租户。
 * 平台接口没有租户上下文：tenant 与 app_user 不做租户过滤，按租户检查产品/设备时用 TenantContext.callAs 显式绑定。
 * 默认租户 default 不能停用、启用或删除（409）。
 */
@Service
public class TenantAdminService {

    static final int NAME_MAX = 64;

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TenantAdminService.class);

    private final TenantMapper tenants;
    private final AppUserMapper users;
    private final ProductMapper products;
    private final DeviceMapper devices;
    private final PlatformStatsMapper statsMapper;
    private final TenantStats stats;
    private final DeviceIdentityCache identityCache;
    private final SessionService sessions;
    private final PasswordEncoder encoder;
    private final Clock clock;

    public TenantAdminService(TenantMapper tenants, AppUserMapper users, ProductMapper products, DeviceMapper devices,
                              PlatformStatsMapper statsMapper, TenantStats stats, DeviceIdentityCache identityCache,
                              SessionService sessions, PasswordEncoder encoder, Clock clock) {
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.users = users;
        this.products = products;
        this.devices = devices;
        this.statsMapper = statsMapper;
        this.stats = stats;
        this.identityCache = identityCache;
        this.sessions = sessions;
        this.encoder = encoder;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /**
     * 分页列出租户（新建的在前），带用户、管理员、产品、设备与在线计数。
     *
     * @param keyword 匹配名称或编码（可空）
     * @param status  ACTIVE / DISABLED（不区分大小写，可空）
     * @throws ApiException 400：status 不合法
     */
    public PageView<TenantView> list(String keyword, String status, int page, int size) {
        LambdaQueryWrapper<Tenant> query = new LambdaQueryWrapper<Tenant>().orderByDesc(Tenant::getId);
        if (keyword != null && !keyword.isBlank()) {
            String kw = keyword.trim();
            query.and(q -> q.like(Tenant::getName, kw).or().like(Tenant::getCode, kw));
        }
        if (status != null && !status.isBlank()) {
            query.eq(Tenant::getStatus, requireStatus(status));
        }
        Page<Tenant> result = tenants.selectPage(new Page<>(page, size), query);
        TenantStats.Snapshot snapshot = stats.snapshot();
        return new PageView<>(result.getRecords().stream().map(t -> view(t, snapshot)).toList(),
                result.getTotal(), page, size);
    }

    /**
     * 新建租户及其首个租户管理员（同一事务；管理员首次登录须修改密码）。用户名在租户插入之后检查，
     * 冲突时抛出的异常让整个事务回滚，不会留下没有管理员的租户。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 400：编码、名称、用户名、密码或显示名称不合规；409：编码或用户名已存在
     */
    @Transactional
    public TenantView create(CreateTenantRequest request) {
        String code = TenantCodePolicy.requireValid(request.code());
        String name = requireName(request.name());
        AdminAccount account = request.admin();
        String username = UsernamePolicy.requireValid(account.username());
        PasswordPolicy.requireValid(account.password());
        String displayName = DisplayNamePolicy.normalize(account.displayName());
        if (tenants.exists(new LambdaQueryWrapper<Tenant>().eq(Tenant::getCode, code))) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("租户编码已存在: " + code);
        }
        Tenant tenant = new Tenant();
        tenant.setCode(code);
        tenant.setName(name);
        tenant.setStatus(Tenant.ACTIVE);
        tenants.insert(tenant);
        if (users.exists(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getUsername, username))) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("用户名已存在: " + username);
        }
        AppUser admin = new AppUser();
        admin.setTenantId(tenant.getId());
        admin.setUsername(username);
        admin.setDisplayName(displayName);
        admin.setPasswordHash(encoder.encode(account.password()));
        admin.setRole(Roles.TENANT_ADMIN);
        admin.setStatus(AppUser.ACTIVE);
        admin.setMustChangePassword(true);
        users.insert(admin);
        log.info("Created tenant {} with admin {}", code, username);
        return view(tenants.selectById(tenant.getId()), stats.snapshot());
    }

    /**
     * 修改租户名称。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户不存在；400：名称为空或超过 64 个字符
     */
    public TenantView rename(String code, UpdateTenantRequest request) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        String name = requireName(request.name());
        tenants.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<Tenant>()
                .eq(Tenant::getId, tenant.getId())
                .set(Tenant::getName, name)
                .set(Tenant::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        log.info("Renamed tenant {} to {}", code, name);
        return view(require(code), stats.snapshot());
    }

    /**
     * 停用或启用租户（重复操作幂等），并清除该租户全部设备的身份缓存，使 MQTT 鉴权立即按新状态判断。
     * 停用后其用户登录与续期被拒、设备连接被拒、已连接设备的上报进入 TENANT_DISABLED 死信。
     *
     * @param status ACTIVE 或 DISABLED
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户不存在；409：默认租户
     */
    public TenantView setStatus(String code, String status) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        if (Tenant.DEFAULT_CODE.equals(tenant.getCode())) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("默认租户不能停用或启用");
        }
        tenants.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<Tenant>()
                .eq(Tenant::getId, tenant.getId())
                .set(Tenant::getStatus, status)
                .set(Tenant::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        evictIdentities(tenant);
        log.info("Set status of tenant {} to {}", code, status);
        return view(require(code), stats.snapshot());
    }

    /**
     * 删除租户及其用户（同一事务）。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户不存在；409：默认租户，或租户下仍有产品或设备
     */
    @Transactional
    public void delete(String code) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        if (Tenant.DEFAULT_CODE.equals(tenant.getCode())) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("默认租户不能删除");
        }
        boolean hasData = TenantContext.callAs(tenant.getId(),
                () -> products.exists(null) || devices.exists(null));
        if (hasData) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("租户下仍有产品或设备，不能删除");
        }
        int removedUsers = users.delete(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>().eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenant.getId()));
        tenants.deleteById(tenant.getId());
        log.info("Deleted tenant {} and its {} users", code, removedUsers);
    }

    /**
     * 列出租户的全部用户（不分页，按角色、用户名排序）。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户不存在
     */
    public List<UserItem> users(String code) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        return users.selectList(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>()
                        .eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenant.getId())
                        .orderByAsc(AppUser::getRole)
                        .orderByAsc(AppUser::getUsername))
                .stream().map(UserItem::of).toList();
    }

    /**
     * 重置租户管理员的密码，并要求其下次登录修改。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户或用户不存在；409：用户不是租户管理员；400：密码不合规
     */
    @Transactional
    public void resetAdminPassword(String code, String username, PasswordRequest request) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        AppUser user = users.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getTenantId, tenant.getId())
                .eq(AppUser::getUsername, username));
        if (user == null) {
            throw ApiException.notFound("用户不存在: " + username);
        }
        if (!Roles.TENANT_ADMIN.equals(user.getRole())) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("只能重置租户管理员的密码");
        }
        PasswordPolicy.requireValid(request.password());
        users.update(null, new LambdaUpdateWrapper<AppUser>()
                .eq(AppUser::getId, user.getId())
                .set(AppUser::getPasswordHash, encoder.encode(request.password()))
                .set(AppUser::getMustChangePassword, true)
                .set(AppUser::getUpdatedAt, OffsetDateTime.now(clock)));
        log.info("Platform reset the password of tenant admin {} in tenant {}", username, code);
    }

    /**
     * 平台管理员进入租户：返回代入身份（有效角色 TENANT_ADMIN），记一行 INFO。
     *
     * @throws ApiException 404：租户不存在；409：租户已停用；401：平台管理员已不可用
     */
    public AuthPrincipal enter(AuthPrincipal actor, String code) {
        Tenant tenant = require(code);
        if (!Tenant.ACTIVE.equals(tenant.getStatus())) {
            throw ApiException.conflict("租户已停用，不能进入");
        }
        AppUser admin = sessions.requireActive(users.selectById(actor.userId()));
        log.info("Platform admin {} entered tenant {}", admin.getUsername(), code);
        return sessions.impersonation(admin, tenant);
    }

    /** 按编码取租户，不存在抛 404。 */
    public Tenant require(String code) {
        Tenant tenant = tenants.selectOne(new LambdaQueryWrapper<Tenant>().eq(Tenant::getCode, code));
        if (tenant == null) {
            throw ApiException.notFound("租户不存在: " + code);
        }
        return tenant;
    }

    /** 清除租户全部设备的身份缓存；Redis 不可用时只记 WARN（缓存 5 分钟后自然过期）。 */
    private void evictIdentities(Tenant tenant) {
        List<String> deviceKeys = statsMapper.deviceKeysOf(tenant.getId());
        try {
            identityCache.evictAll(deviceKeys);
        } catch (DataAccessException e) {
            log.warn("Failed to evict device identities of tenant {}", tenant.getCode(), e);
        }
    }

    /** 组装租户视图。 */
    private static TenantView view(Tenant t, TenantStats.Snapshot snapshot) {
        TenantStats.TenantCounts c = snapshot.of(t.getId());
        return new TenantView(t.getCode(), t.getName(), t.getStatus(), c.adminCount(), c.userCount(),
                c.productCount(), c.deviceCount(), c.onlineCount(), t.getCreatedAt());
    }

    /** 校验并规范化租户名称（去首尾空白，1～64 个字符），非法抛 400。 */
    static String requireName(String name) {
        String trimmed = name == null ? "" : name.trim();
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("租户名称不能为空");
        }
        if (trimmed.length() > NAME_MAX) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("租户名称最多 64 个字符");
        }
        return trimmed;
    }

    /** 校验租户状态（不区分大小写），返回大写值；非法抛 400。 */
    static String requireStatus(String status) {
        String upper = status.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!Tenant.ACTIVE.equals(upper) && !Tenant.DISABLED.equals(upper)) {
            throw ApiException.badRequest("状态只能是 ACTIVE 或 DISABLED");
        }
        return upper;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Add the controller**

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/platform/PlatformController.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.platform;

import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthDtos.TokenResponse;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.AuthPrincipal;
import dev.conduit.server.auth.SessionService;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.CreateTenantRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.PlatformOverview;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.TenantView;
import dev.conduit.server.platform.PlatformDtos.UpdateTenantRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.PasswordRequest;
import dev.conduit.server.user.UserDtos.UserItem;
import dev.conduit.server.web.PageView;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.DeleteMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ResponseStatus;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 平台接口（/api/v1/platform，仅未代入的平台管理员，权限在 SecurityConfig）：平台概况与租户管理、进入租户。
 * 逻辑在 {@link PlatformOverviewService} 与 {@link TenantAdminService}。
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/platform")
public class PlatformController {

    private final PlatformOverviewService overview;
    private final TenantAdminService tenants;
    private final SessionService sessions;

    public PlatformController(PlatformOverviewService overview, TenantAdminService tenants, SessionService sessions) {
        this.overview = overview;
        this.tenants = tenants;
        this.sessions = sessions;
    }

    /** 平台概况。 */
    @GetMapping("/overview")
    public PlatformOverview overview() {
        return overview.overview();
    }

    /** 分页列出租户（默认每页 10 条），可按名称/编码关键字与状态筛选。 */
    @GetMapping("/tenants")
    public PageView<TenantView> list(@RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
                                     @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
                                     @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") @Min(1) int page,
                                     @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") @Min(1) @Max(100) int size) {
        return tenants.list(keyword, status, page, size);
    }

    @PostMapping("/tenants")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public TenantView create(@Valid @RequestBody CreateTenantRequest request) {
        return tenants.create(request);
    }

    @PutMapping("/tenants/{code}")
    public TenantView rename(@PathVariable String code, @Valid @RequestBody UpdateTenantRequest request) {
        return tenants.rename(code, request);
    }

    @PostMapping("/tenants/{code}/disable")
    public TenantView disable(@PathVariable String code) {
        return tenants.setStatus(code, Tenant.DISABLED);
    }

    @PostMapping("/tenants/{code}/enable")
    public TenantView enable(@PathVariable String code) {
        return tenants.setStatus(code, Tenant.ACTIVE);
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/tenants/{code}")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void delete(@PathVariable String code) {
        tenants.delete(code);
    }

    /** 租户的全部用户（不分页，按角色、用户名排序）。 */
    @GetMapping("/tenants/{code}/users")
    public List<UserItem> users(@PathVariable String code) {
        return tenants.users(code);
    }

    /** 重置租户管理员的密码（仅 TENANT_ADMIN，否则 409）。 */
    @PostMapping("/tenants/{code}/users/{username}/reset-password")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void resetPassword(@PathVariable String code, @PathVariable String username,
                              @Valid @RequestBody PasswordRequest request) {
        tenants.resetAdminPassword(code, username, request);
    }

    /** 进入租户：签发代入令牌（access + refresh Cookie）。 */
    @PostMapping("/tenants/{code}/enter")
    public ResponseEntity<TokenResponse> enter(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthPrincipal actor,
                                               @PathVariable String code) {
        return sessions.issue(tenants.enter(actor, code));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Log impersonated writes**

Create `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/ImpersonationLogFilter.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.auth;

import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;
import dev.conduit.server.tenant.TenantMapper;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 代入操作留痕：平台管理员代入租户期间的每个写请求（POST/PUT/DELETE/PATCH，/api/v1/auth/** 除外）在完成后记一行 INFO：
 * 平台管理员用户名、租户编码、方法、路径与最终状态码。不记请求体与查询串。V1.0 并入审计表。
 */
public class ImpersonationLogFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ImpersonationLogFilter.class);
    private static final Set<String> WRITE_METHODS = Set.of("POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH");

    private final TenantMapper tenants;

    public ImpersonationLogFilter(TenantMapper tenants) {
        this.tenants = tenants;
    }

    /** 代入的写请求放行后记日志（finally 中，含被拒绝或出错的请求）；其余请求直接放行。 */
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, FilterChain chain)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        AuthPrincipal principal = currentPrincipal();
        if (principal == null || !principal.impersonating() || !WRITE_METHODS.contains(request.getMethod())
                || request.getRequestURI().startsWith("/api/v1/auth/")) {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
            return;
        }
        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            log.info("Impersonated write by platform admin {} in tenant {}: {} {} -> {}", principal.username(),
                    tenantCode(principal.tenantId()), request.getMethod(), request.getRequestURI(),
                    response.getStatus());
        }
    }

    /** 当前请求的认证身份；未认证为 null。 */
    private static AuthPrincipal currentPrincipal() {
        Authentication authentication = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        return authentication != null && authentication.getPrincipal() instanceof AuthPrincipal p ? p : null;
    }

    /** 租户编码；查询失败或租户不存在时退回租户 id，日志本身不能让请求失败。 */
    private String tenantCode(Long tenantId) {
        try {
            Tenant tenant = tenants.selectById(tenantId);
            return tenant == null ? String.valueOf(tenantId) : tenant.getCode();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return String.valueOf(tenantId);
        }
    }
}
```

In `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/auth/SecurityConfig.java`:

- in the class Javadoc, replace `过滤器顺序：JwtAuthFilter → PasswordChangeGateFilter → 权限判断。` with `过滤器顺序：JwtAuthFilter → PasswordChangeGateFilter → ImpersonationLogFilter → 权限判断。`
- replace

```java
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http, JwtService jwt, ObjectMapper objectMapper)
            throws Exception {
```

with

```java

                                            TenantMapper tenants) throws Exception {
```

- replace

```java

```

with


## Task 5: Backend disabled tenants at the device edge (`TENANT_DISABLED`)

The device identity used by MQTT auth and the pipeline now carries the tenant status: devices of a disabled tenant are denied at connect time, and data from devices that are still connected goes to the new dead reason `TENANT_DISABLED` (spec §3.6).

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceIdentity.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceMapper.java` (`findIdentity`)
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/mqttauth/MqttAuthService.java`
- Modify: `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/pipeline/DeadReason.java`, `PipelineProcessor.java`
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceIdentityTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/pipeline/PipelineProcessorTest.java` (helper + one test)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/mqttauth/MqttAuthIT.java` (two tests)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/pipeline/PipelineIT.java` (one test)
- Test: `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceApiIT.java`, `DeviceIdentityLookupIT.java`, `conduit-server/src/test/java/dev/conduit/server/tenant/TenantIsolationIT.java` (constructor calls)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 1, 4): `Tenant.ACTIVE/DISABLED`, `POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/{code}/disable|enable` (evicts `conduit:auth:*` of the tenant), `IntegrationTestBase.createTenant/createDevice/tenantCode/loginAsPlatformAdmin`.
- Produces:
  - `DeviceIdentity(String deviceKey, long tenantId, String status, String secretHash, String secretSalt, String productKey, String tenantStatus)`, `boolean tenantActive()`.
  - `DeadReason.TENANT_DISABLED` (dead-letter `reason` value `TENANT_DISABLED`; the frontend labels it 「租户已停用」 in Task 6).

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `findIdentity` joins `tenant` to read its status; the identity stays cached for 5 minutes, which is why Task 4 evicts the tenant's identities on disable and enable.
- Identities cached before this version have no `tenantStatus`; Jackson leaves it `null`, and `tenantActive()` treats `null` as active (such entries expire within 5 minutes).
- The pipeline checks the tenant **before** the device's own status: when both are off, the reason is `TENANT_DISABLED`, the broader cause.
- Already-connected MQTT sessions are not kicked (spec §1 out of scope; backlog in Task 9); their data is dead-lettered instead.

Replace `conduit-server/src/main/java/dev/conduit/server/device/DeviceIdentity.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.server.device;

import dev.conduit.server.tenant.Tenant;

/**
 * 鉴权与流水线需要的设备身份（含所属租户的状态），跨租户查询，缓存在 conduit:auth:{deviceKey}（5 分钟）。
 * tenantStatus 为 V0.2-C 新增：升级前写入缓存的条目没有该字段（反序列化为 null），按租户启用处理，随缓存过期自然消失。
 */
public record DeviceIdentity(String deviceKey, long tenantId, String status,
                             String secretHash, String secretSalt, String productKey, String tenantStatus) {

    /**
     * 设备是否处于启用状态；禁用的设备不应通过鉴权，其上报也不应被处理。
     */
    public boolean enabled() {
        return Device.ENABLED.equals(status);
    }

    /**
     * 所属租户是否启用；停用租户的设备不能接入，已连接设备的上报进 TENANT_DISABLED 死信。null（旧缓存条目）视为启用。
     */
    public boolean tenantActive() {
        return !Tenant.DISABLED.equals(tenantStatus);
    }
}
```

```java
     * 设备不存在返回 null。
     */
    @InterceptorIgnore(tenantLine = "true")
    @Select("""
            select d.device_key, d.tenant_id, d.status, d.secret_hash, d.secret_salt, p.product_key
            from device d join product p on p.id = d.product_id
```

```java
    @Test
    void deniesDevicesOfADisabledTenant() throws Exception {

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/disable", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
        auth("test-internal-token", d.deviceKey(), d.deviceKey(), d.secret())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.result").value("deny"));

        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/tenants/{c}/enable", code).header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearer(sys)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
        auth("test-internal-token", d.deviceKey(), d.deviceKey(), d.secret())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.result").value("allow"));
    }
```

  - `platformApi.{overview, listTenants, createTenant, updateTenant, disableTenant, enableTenant, removeTenant, tenantUsers, resetAdminPassword, enter}`; `userApi.{list, create, update, disable, enable, resetPassword, remove}`; `meApi.{update, changePassword}`; `authApi.exitTenant`.
  - `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED` (from `api/http.ts`); `TokenHolder.onRefreshed?(res)`, `TokenHolder.onPasswordChangeRequired?()`.
  - `useAuthStore`: state `token`, `user`, `passwordChangeRequired`; getters `isAuthenticated`, `needsPasswordChange`; actions `setSession(res)`, `login`, `restore`, `logout`, `enterTenant(code)`, `exitTenant()`, `changePassword(old, new)`, `updateProfile(displayName)`, `clear`.
  - `permissionsOf(user) → Permissions { isPlatformAdmin, isImpersonating, canWrite, canManageUsers }`, `roleLabel(role)`, `statusLabel(status, kind?)`, `displayNameOf(user)`; `usePermission() → { isPlatformAdmin, isImpersonating, canWrite, canManageUsers }` (computed refs).
  - `usernameProblem(v)`, `passwordProblem(v)`, `tenantCodeProblem(v)` → `string | null`.
  - Route meta `scope?: 'tenant' | 'platform'`, `requires?: 'manageUsers'`; `ROUTE_META.tenants`, `ROUTE_META.users`; `accessRedirect(to, user) → RouteLocationRaw | null`.
  - `menuGroupsFor(permissions) → MenuGroup[]` (`MENU_GROUPS` and `disabledNote` are removed).
  - `createTestPinia(user?: Partial<UserView>) → Pinia` (default: `admin`, `TENANT_ADMIN`, tenant `default`/「默认租户」); test routes `tenants` (`/tenants`) and `users` (`/users`).
  - AppLayout exposes `userName`, `roleText`, `onCommand(command)`; Task 8 adds the `profile` command.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `isPlatformAdmin` means "platform admin outside any tenant": an impersonation session has the effective role `TENANT_ADMIN`, so `isPlatformAdmin` is false and `canWrite`/`canManageUsers` are true while `isImpersonating` is true.
- Only login, refresh and logout are token-less public auth calls now; `exit-tenant` and `me` carry the token and take part in the 401 → refresh → retry cycle.
- A refresh can change the identity (impersonation falls back to the platform admin when the tenant was disabled), so `http.ts` hands the whole refresh response to `onRefreshed`, the store updates `user`, and AppLayout re-applies `accessRedirect` whenever `user` changes.
- The forced password dialog lives in AppLayout and is driven only by `auth.needsPasswordChange` (`user.mustChangePassword` from login/refresh/`me`, or any 403 `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED`). It cannot be closed; it offers 「退出登录」 so nobody is stuck.
- Client-side account rules mirror the server messages exactly; the server stays authoritative.
- The real router gets the `tenants` and `users` routes in Tasks 7 and 8 (their pages do not exist yet); this task adds their meta, menus and guard rules, and the test router already knows both names.

- [ ] **Step 3: Extend the HTTP layer and the API modules**

Replace `conduit-ui/src/api/http.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * Axios 实例工厂：统一附带 access token、401 时自动刷新并重试、识别「需要先修改密码」的 403。
 * 不直接依赖 store 与路由，token 的读写、刷新结果与登录失效处理由调用方通过 TokenHolder 注入。
 */

import axios, { isAxiosError, type AxiosError, type AxiosInstance } from 'axios'
import type { TokenResponse } from './types'

declare module 'axios' {
  interface AxiosRequestConfig {
    /** 已经因 401 重试过一次，不再刷新 */
    _retried?: boolean
  }
}

/** token 的存取与各类回调，由使用方（api/client.ts）实现，使 http.ts 不依赖 store 和路由。 */
export interface TokenHolder {
  getToken(): string | null
  setToken(token: string | null): void
  onAuthFailed(): void
  /** 自动刷新成功后收到完整响应（含最新的用户信息，例如代入的租户已停用时退回平台管理员身份） */
  onRefreshed?(res: TokenResponse): void
  /** 任一接口返回 403 且 code 为 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED 时调用（弹出强制修改密码对话框） */
  onPasswordChangeRequired?(): void
}

/** 刷新 token 的接口地址 */
export const REFRESH_URL = '/api/v1/auth/refresh'
/** 需要先修改密码时服务端 ProblemDetail 的 code */
export const PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED = 'PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED'
/** 公开的认证接口：不带 token，也不触发自动刷新（退出租户等其他 /api/v1/auth/ 接口需要 token） */
const PUBLIC_AUTH_URLS = new Set(['/api/v1/auth/login', REFRESH_URL, '/api/v1/auth/logout'])

/** 是否为公开的认证接口 */
function isPublicAuth(url: string | undefined): boolean {
  return url !== undefined && PUBLIC_AUTH_URLS.has(url)
}

/** 是否为服务端明确拒绝登录态（401/403）。网络错误、5xx 不算，避免服务端抖动时把用户登出。 */
function isAuthRejection(error: unknown): boolean {
  const status = isAxiosError(error) ? error.response?.status : undefined
  return status === 401 || status === 403
}

/** 是否为「需要先修改密码」的 403 */
function isPasswordChangeRequired(error: AxiosError): boolean {
  const data = error.response?.data as { code?: unknown } | undefined
  return error.response?.status === 403 && data?.code === PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED
}

/**
 * 创建带鉴权的 Axios 实例：自动附带 access token；遇到 401 时用 refresh Cookie 换新 token 并重试一次，
 * 并发的多个 401 共享同一次刷新，刷新成功后把完整响应交给 onRefreshed。刷新失败（401/403）则清空 token 并通知调用方（跳转登录）。
 * 任一 403 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED 通知 onPasswordChangeRequired，错误照常抛给调用方。
 */
export function createHttp(holder: TokenHolder): AxiosInstance {
  const http = axios.create({ withCredentials: true })
  let refreshing: Promise<string> | null = null

  function refreshToken(): Promise<string> {
    refreshing ??= http
      .post<TokenResponse>(REFRESH_URL, null, { _retried: true })
      .then((res) => {
        holder.setToken(res.data.accessToken)
        holder.onRefreshed?.(res.data)
        return res.data.accessToken
      })
      .catch((refreshError: unknown) => {
        // 只在共享刷新失败时处理一次；仅 401/403 视为登录失效，网络错误/5xx 不登出
        if (isAuthRejection(refreshError)) {
          holder.setToken(null)
          holder.onAuthFailed()
        }
        throw refreshError
      })
      .finally(() => {
        refreshing = null
      })
    return refreshing
  }

  http.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
    const token = holder.getToken()
    if (token && !isPublicAuth(config.url)) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
    }
    return config
  })

  http.interceptors.response.use(undefined, async (error: AxiosError) => {
    if (isPasswordChangeRequired(error)) {
      holder.onPasswordChangeRequired?.()
    }
    const config = error.config
    if (error.response?.status !== 401 || !config || config._retried || isPublicAuth(config.url)) {
      return Promise.reject(error)
    }
    config._retried = true
    try {
      const token = await refreshToken()
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
      return http(config)
    } catch (refreshError) {
      return Promise.reject(isAuthRejection(refreshError) ? refreshError : error)
    }
  })

  return http
}
```

Replace `conduit-ui/src/api/client.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * 应用全局共用的 Axios 实例：把 createHttp 与 Pinia 认证 store、路由接在一起。
 * 取/存 access token 走 auth store；自动刷新后用响应里的用户更新会话（身份可能变化，如代入的租户已停用）；
 * 接口要求先修改密码时置 store 标记（弹出强制改密码对话框）；刷新失败（登录失效）时清空会话并跳转登录页，用 redirect 记住当前地址。
 * 单独成文件是为了让 http.ts 不依赖 store 和路由，便于单测。
 */

import { createHttp } from './http'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import router from '@/router'

export const http = createHttp({
  getToken: () => useAuthStore().token,
  setToken: (token) => {
    useAuthStore().token = token
  },
  onRefreshed: (res) => {
    if (res.user) {
      useAuthStore().user = res.user
    }
  },
  onPasswordChangeRequired: () => {
    useAuthStore().passwordChangeRequired = true
  },
  onAuthFailed: () => {
    useAuthStore().clear()
    void router.replace({ name: 'login', query: { redirect: router.currentRoute.value.fullPath } })
  },
})
```

Replace `conduit-ui/src/api/auth.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * 认证相关 REST 调用：登录、用 HttpOnly refresh Cookie 刷新 access token、登出、平台管理员退出代入的租户。
 * 登录、刷新与退出租户返回 TokenResponse，由 stores/auth 写入内存；登录、刷新、登出不附带 Authorization，也不触发 401 自动刷新。
 */

import { http } from './client'
import type { TokenResponse } from './types'

export const authApi = {
  login: (username: string, password: string) =>
    http.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', { username, password }).then((r) => r.data),
  refresh: () => http.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/refresh').then((r) => r.data),
  logout: () => http.post('/api/v1/auth/logout').then(() => undefined),
  /** 退出代入的租户，换回平台管理员自己的令牌 */
  exitTenant: () => http.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant').then((r) => r.data),
}
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/api/platform.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 平台管理员的 REST 调用（/api/v1/platform/**）：平台概况；租户的列表、新建、改名、停用/启用、删除；某租户的用户；
 * 重置租户管理员密码；进入租户（返回代入令牌，由 stores/auth 写入会话）。租户编码与用户名经 encodeURIComponent 拼进路径。
 */

import { http } from './client'
import type {
  CreateTenantRequest,
  PageView,
  PlatformOverview,
  TenantQuery,
  TenantUserView,
  TenantView,
  TokenResponse,
  UpdateTenantRequest,
} from './types'

const tenantPath = (code: string) => `/api/v1/platform/tenants/${encodeURIComponent(code)}`

export const platformApi = {
  overview: () => http.get<PlatformOverview>('/api/v1/platform/overview').then((r) => r.data),
  listTenants: (query: TenantQuery) =>
    http.get<PageView<TenantView>>('/api/v1/platform/tenants', { params: query }).then((r) => r.data),
  createTenant: (req: CreateTenantRequest) =>
    http.post<TenantView>('/api/v1/platform/tenants', req).then((r) => r.data),
  updateTenant: (code: string, req: UpdateTenantRequest) =>
    http.put<TenantView>(tenantPath(code), req).then((r) => r.data),
  disableTenant: (code: string) => http.post<TenantView>(`${tenantPath(code)}/disable`).then((r) => r.data),
  enableTenant: (code: string) => http.post<TenantView>(`${tenantPath(code)}/enable`).then((r) => r.data),
  removeTenant: (code: string) => http.delete(tenantPath(code)).then(() => undefined),
  tenantUsers: (code: string) => http.get<TenantUserView[]>(`${tenantPath(code)}/users`).then((r) => r.data),
  resetAdminPassword: (code: string, username: string, password: string) =>
    http
      .post(`${tenantPath(code)}/users/${encodeURIComponent(username)}/reset-password`, { password })
      .then(() => undefined),
  enter: (code: string) => http.post<TokenResponse>(`${tenantPath(code)}/enter`).then((r) => r.data),
}
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/stores/auth.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** auth store 单元测试：登录写入会话、需改密码判断、修改密码与进入/退出租户换用新会话、修改显示名称、恢复失败时清空 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { createPinia, setActivePinia } from 'pinia'
import { useAuthStore } from './auth'
import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
import { meApi } from '@/api/me'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { TokenResponse, UserView } from '@/api/types'

vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({ authApi: { login: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), logout: vi.fn(), exitTenant: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/me', () => ({ meApi: { update: vi.fn(), changePassword: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { enter: vi.fn() } }))

function user(patch: Partial<UserView> = {}): UserView {
  return {
    username: 'east-admin',
    displayName: null,
    role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    tenantCode: 'east',
    tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
    mustChangePassword: false,
    impersonatedBy: null,
    ...patch,
  }
}

function session(token: string, patch: Partial<UserView> = {}): TokenResponse {
  return { accessToken: token, expiresIn: 900, user: user(patch) }
}

describe('useAuthStore', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    setActivePinia(createPinia())
    vi.clearAllMocks()
  })

  it('stores the session on login and flags a required password change', async () => {
    vi.mocked(authApi.login).mockResolvedValue(session('t1', { mustChangePassword: true }))
    const auth = useAuthStore()

    await auth.login('east-admin', 'Init-pass-2026')

    expect(auth.token).toBe('t1')
    expect(auth.needsPasswordChange).toBe(true)
  })

  it('flags a password change reported by an endpoint', () => {
    const auth = useAuthStore()
    auth.setSession(session('t1'))
    expect(auth.needsPasswordChange).toBe(false)

    auth.passwordChangeRequired = true

    expect(auth.needsPasswordChange).toBe(true)
  })

  it('changes the password and swaps in the new session', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.changePassword).mockResolvedValue(session('t2'))
    const auth = useAuthStore()
    auth.setSession(session('t1', { mustChangePassword: true }))
    auth.passwordChangeRequired = true

    await auth.changePassword('Init-pass-2026', 'Active-pass-2026')

    expect(meApi.changePassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ oldPassword: 'Init-pass-2026', newPassword: 'Active-pass-2026' })
    expect(auth.token).toBe('t2')
    expect(auth.needsPasswordChange).toBe(false)
  })

  it('enters and exits a tenant', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.enter).mockResolvedValue(session('imp', { username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' }))
    vi.mocked(authApi.exitTenant).mockResolvedValue(
      session('own', { username: 'sysadmin', role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null }),
    )
    const auth = useAuthStore()

    await auth.enterTenant('east')
    expect(platformApi.enter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east')
    expect(auth.user?.impersonatedBy).toBe('sysadmin')

    await auth.exitTenant()
    expect(auth.token).toBe('own')
    expect(auth.user?.role).toBe('PLATFORM_ADMIN')
  })

  it('updates the display name from the server response', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.update).mockResolvedValue(user({ displayName: '华东运维 张工' }))
    const auth = useAuthStore()
    auth.setSession(session('t1'))

    await auth.updateProfile('华东运维 张工')

    expect(meApi.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ displayName: '华东运维 张工' })
    expect(auth.user?.displayName).toBe('华东运维 张工')
  })

  it('clears everything when the cookie cannot restore the session', async () => {
    vi.mocked(authApi.refresh).mockRejectedValue(new Error('401'))
    const auth = useAuthStore()
    auth.setSession(session('t1'))
    auth.passwordChangeRequired = true

    expect(await auth.restore()).toBe(false)
    expect(auth.token).toBeNull()
    expect(auth.user).toBeNull()
    expect(auth.passwordChangeRequired).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

```ts
/** permissions 单元测试：各身份的权限（平台管理员、代入、租户管理员、只读用户、未登录）与角色、状态、名称的显示 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { displayNameOf, permissionsOf, roleLabel, statusLabel } from './permissions'

function user(patch: Partial<UserView>): UserView {
  return {
    username: 'u',
    displayName: null,
    role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    tenantCode: 'east',
    tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
    mustChangePassword: false,
    impersonatedBy: null,
    ...patch,
  }
}

describe('permissionsOf', () => {
  it('gives the platform admin only the platform', () => {
    expect(permissionsOf(user({ role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null }))).toEqual({
      isPlatformAdmin: true,
      isImpersonating: false,
      canWrite: false,
      canManageUsers: false,
    })
  })

  it('treats an impersonating platform admin as a tenant admin', () => {
    expect(permissionsOf(user({ impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' }))).toEqual({
      isPlatformAdmin: false,
      isImpersonating: true,
      canWrite: true,
      canManageUsers: true,
    })
  })

  it('lets tenant admins write and manage users, viewers only read', () => {
    expect(permissionsOf(user({}))).toMatchObject({ canWrite: true, canManageUsers: true, isPlatformAdmin: false })
    expect(permissionsOf(user({ role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' }))).toMatchObject({ canWrite: false, canManageUsers: false })
    expect(permissionsOf(null)).toEqual({ isPlatformAdmin: false, isImpersonating: false, canWrite: false, canManageUsers: false })
  })
})

describe('labels', () => {
  it('names roles, statuses and users in Chinese', () => {
    expect(roleLabel('PLATFORM_ADMIN')).toBe('平台管理员')
    expect(roleLabel('TENANT_ADMIN')).toBe('租户管理员')
    expect(roleLabel('TENANT_VIEWER')).toBe('只读用户')
    expect(statusLabel('ACTIVE')).toBe('启用')
    expect(statusLabel('DISABLED')).toBe('禁用')
    expect(statusLabel('DISABLED', 'tenant')).toBe('停用')
    expect(displayNameOf({ username: 'east-admin', displayName: '华东管理员' })).toBe('华东管理员')
    expect(displayNameOf({ username: 'east-admin', displayName: null })).toBe('east-admin')
    expect(displayNameOf(null)).toBe('')
  })
})
```

```ts
/** accountRules 单元测试：用户名、密码、租户编码的合法与非法取值（与服务端规则一致） */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { passwordProblem, tenantCodeProblem, usernameProblem } from './accountRules'

describe('accountRules', () => {
  it('checks usernames', () => {
    for (const ok of ['abc', 'Admin_01', 'zhang.san', 'a-b', 'a'.repeat(32)]) {
      expect(usernameProblem(ok)).toBeNull()
    }
    for (const bad of ['', 'ab', '1abc', '_abc', 'abc def', '张三丰', 'a'.repeat(33)]) {
      expect(usernameProblem(bad)).toContain('用户名须为 3～32 位')
    }
  })

  it('checks passwords', () => {
    for (const ok of ['abcdefg1', 'Passw0rd!', '1234567a', 'a'.repeat(63) + '1']) {
      expect(passwordProblem(ok)).toBeNull()
    }
    for (const bad of ['', 'abc1234', 'abcdefgh', '12345678', 'a'.repeat(64) + '1']) {
      expect(passwordProblem(bad)).toBe('密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字')
    }
  })

  it('checks tenant codes', () => {
    for (const ok of ['default', 'ab', 'east-1', 'a' + 'b'.repeat(31)]) {
      expect(tenantCodeProblem(ok)).toBeNull()
    }
    for (const bad of ['', 'a', '1abc', 'Abc', 'a_b', '-ab', 'a'.repeat(33), '华东']) {
      expect(tenantCodeProblem(bad)).toContain('租户编码须为 2～32 位')
    }
  })
})
```

```ts
/** usePermission 测试：随 auth store 中的当前用户变化（租户管理员 → 只读用户 → 平台管理员 → 代入）实时更新 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { createPinia, setActivePinia } from 'pinia'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { usePermission } from './usePermission'

const base: UserView = {
  username: 'u',
  displayName: null,
  role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
  tenantCode: 'east',
  tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
  mustChangePassword: false,
  impersonatedBy: null,
}

describe('usePermission', () => {
  it('follows the current user', () => {
    setActivePinia(createPinia())
    const auth = useAuthStore()
    auth.user = base
    const { isPlatformAdmin, isImpersonating, canWrite, canManageUsers } = usePermission()

    expect(canWrite.value).toBe(true)
    expect(canManageUsers.value).toBe(true)

    auth.user = { ...base, role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' }
    expect(canWrite.value).toBe(false)

    auth.user = { ...base, role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null }
    expect(isPlatformAdmin.value).toBe(true)
    expect(canWrite.value).toBe(false)

    auth.user = { ...base, impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' }
    expect(isImpersonating.value).toBe(true)
    expect(isPlatformAdmin.value).toBe(false)
    expect(canWrite.value).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

Replace `conduit-ui/src/stores/auth.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * 认证状态（Pinia）：access token 只放内存，页面刷新后靠 HttpOnly refresh Cookie 恢复。
 * 同时保存当前用户（有效角色、所属租户、代入者、是否需改密码）与「接口要求先改密码」标记，
 * 并提供进入/退出租户、修改密码、修改显示名称；这些操作返回的新会话都经 setSession 写入。
 */

import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
import { meApi } from '@/api/me'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { TokenResponse, UserView } from '@/api/types'

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', {
  state: () => ({
    token: null as string | null,
    user: null as UserView | null,
    /** 某个接口返回了 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED（令牌带「需改密码」标记，即使 user 里还没体现） */
    passwordChangeRequired: false,
  }),
  getters: {
    isAuthenticated: (state) => state.token !== null,
    /** 是否需要弹出强制修改密码对话框：已登录，且用户带标记或接口要求先改密码 */
    needsPasswordChange: (state) =>
      state.token !== null && (state.passwordChangeRequired || state.user?.mustChangePassword === true),
  },
  actions: {
    /** 写入一次签发的会话（token 与用户），并清除「接口要求先改密码」标记。 */
    setSession(res: TokenResponse) {
      this.token = res.accessToken
      this.user = res.user
      this.passwordChangeRequired = false
    },
    /** 用户名密码登录，成功后保存会话；失败时抛出接口错误，由调用方提示。 */
    async login(username: string, password: string) {
      this.setSession(await authApi.login(username, password))
    },
    /** 页面刷新后内存里的 token 没了，用 HttpOnly refresh Cookie 恢复会话；失败时清空并返回 false。 */
    async restore(): Promise<boolean> {
      try {
        this.setSession(await authApi.refresh())
        return true
      } catch {
        this.clear()
        return false
      }
    },
    /** 登出：通知服务端作废 refresh Cookie。无论请求成败都清空本地会话，失败异常仍会抛出。 */
    async logout() {
      try {
        await authApi.logout()
      } finally {
        this.clear()
      }
    },
    /** 平台管理员进入租户：换成代入令牌；失败时抛出接口错误，会话不变。 */
    async enterTenant(code: string) {
      this.setSession(await platformApi.enter(code))
    },
    /** 退出代入的租户，换回平台管理员自己的令牌。 */
    async exitTenant() {
      this.setSession(await authApi.exitTenant())
    },
    /** 修改自己的密码：成功后换用新令牌（不再带需改密码标记）；失败时抛出接口错误。 */
    async changePassword(oldPassword: string, newPassword: string) {
      this.setSession(await meApi.changePassword({ oldPassword, newPassword }))
    },
    /** 修改自己的显示名称（null 即清除），用服务端返回的用户更新会话。 */
    async updateProfile(displayName: string | null) {
      this.user = await meApi.update({ displayName })
    },
    /** 清空本地会话（token、用户与改密码标记），不请求服务端；登录失效或登出时使用。 */
    clear() {
      this.token = null
      this.user = null
      this.passwordChangeRequired = false
    },
  },
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/utils/permissions.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 权限与身份显示：由当前用户算出 isPlatformAdmin（未代入的平台管理员）、isImpersonating（平台管理员代入租户中）、
 * canWrite / canManageUsers（租户管理员，含代入的平台管理员），以及角色、状态与用户名称的中文显示。
 * 前端只用这些结果隐藏入口，真正的权限判断在后端。
 */
import type { AccountStatus, Role, UserView } from '@/api/types'

/** 当前用户的权限 */
export interface Permissions {
  /** 未代入的平台管理员（代入时有效角色是 TENANT_ADMIN，此项为 false） */
  isPlatformAdmin: boolean
  /** 平台管理员正在代入某个租户 */
  isImpersonating: boolean
  /** 可以写租户数据（新建、编辑、删除、导入、启用/禁用、重置密钥） */
  canWrite: boolean
  /** 可以管理本租户的用户 */
  canManageUsers: boolean
}

/** 由当前用户算出权限；未登录时全部为 false */
export function permissionsOf(user: UserView | null): Permissions {
  const role = user?.role
  return {
    isPlatformAdmin: role === 'PLATFORM_ADMIN',
    isImpersonating: Boolean(user?.impersonatedBy),
    canWrite: role === 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    canManageUsers: role === 'TENANT_ADMIN',
  }
}

const ROLE_LABELS: Record<Role, string> = {
  PLATFORM_ADMIN: '平台管理员',
  TENANT_ADMIN: '租户管理员',
  TENANT_VIEWER: '只读用户',
}

/** 角色的中文名称 */
export function roleLabel(role: Role): string {
  return ROLE_LABELS[role] ?? role
}

/**
 * 状态的中文名称：ACTIVE 为「启用」；DISABLED 对用户是「禁用」、对租户是「停用」。
 * @param kind 状态的主体，默认用户
 */
export function statusLabel(status: AccountStatus, kind: 'user' | 'tenant' = 'user'): string {
  if (status === 'ACTIVE') {
    return '启用'
  }
  return kind === 'tenant' ? '停用' : '禁用'
}

/** 显示用的名称：有显示名称用显示名称，否则用用户名；没有用户时为空串 */
export function displayNameOf(user: { username: string; displayName: string | null } | null): string {
  return user ? user.displayName || user.username : ''
}
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/utils/accountRules.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 账号输入规则（与服务端一致，用于表单即时校验；服务端仍会再次校验）：用户名、密码、租户编码。
 * 各函数返回错误文案，合法时返回 null。
 */

/** 用户名：3～32 位，字母开头，只能含字母、数字、_、-、. */
export const USERNAME_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{2,31}$/
/** 租户编码：2～32 位，小写字母开头，只能含小写字母、数字和 - */
export const TENANT_CODE_PATTERN = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$/

/** 校验用户名 */
export function usernameProblem(value: string): string | null {
  return USERNAME_PATTERN.test(value) ? null : '用户名须为 3～32 位，字母开头，只能包含字母、数字、_、-、.'
}

/** 校验密码：8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字 */
export function passwordProblem(value: string): string | null {
  const ok = value.length >= 8 && value.length <= 64 && /[A-Za-z]/.test(value) && /[0-9]/.test(value)
  return ok ? null : '密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字'
}

/** 校验租户编码 */
export function tenantCodeProblem(value: string): string | null {
  return TENANT_CODE_PATTERN.test(value) ? null : '租户编码须为 2～32 位，小写字母开头，只能包含小写字母、数字和 -'
}
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/composables/usePermission.ts`:

```ts
/** 当前用户的权限（响应式）：isPlatformAdmin、isImpersonating、canWrite、canManageUsers，规则见 utils/permissions。 */
import { computed, type ComputedRef } from 'vue'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { permissionsOf } from '@/utils/permissions'

/** usePermission 的返回值；解构后在模板中直接使用（computed 会自动解包） */
export interface PermissionRefs {
  isPlatformAdmin: ComputedRef<boolean>
  isImpersonating: ComputedRef<boolean>
  canWrite: ComputedRef<boolean>
  canManageUsers: ComputedRef<boolean>
}

/** 读取当前会话的权限；随 auth store 的用户变化实时更新 */
export function usePermission(): PermissionRefs {
  const auth = useAuthStore()
  const perms = computed(() => permissionsOf(auth.user))
  return {
    isPlatformAdmin: computed(() => perms.value.isPlatformAdmin),
    isImpersonating: computed(() => perms.value.isImpersonating),
    canWrite: computed(() => perms.value.canWrite),
    canManageUsers: computed(() => perms.value.canManageUsers),
  }
}
```

```ts
/** accessRedirect 单元测试：平台管理员访问租户页面带到租户管理；租户身份访问平台页面带到首页；只读用户不能进入用户管理；其余放行 */
```
