```ts
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { ROUTE_META } from './meta'
import { accessRedirect } from './access'

function user(patch: Partial<UserView>): UserView {
  return {
    username: 'u',
    displayName: null,
    role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    tenantCode: 'east',
    tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
    mustChangePassword: false,
    impersonatedBy: null,
    ...patch,
  }
}

const platform = user({ role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null })
const admin = user({})
const viewer = user({ role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' })
const impersonating = user({ impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' })

describe('accessRedirect', () => {
  it('sends the platform admin from tenant pages to the tenant list', () => {
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.products }, platform)).toEqual({ name: 'tenants' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META['device-detail'] }, platform)).toEqual({ name: 'tenants' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.users }, platform)).toEqual({ name: 'tenants' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.tenants }, platform)).toBeNull()
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.home }, platform)).toBeNull()
  })

  it('sends tenant identities from platform pages to home', () => {
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.tenants }, admin)).toEqual({ name: 'home' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.tenants }, viewer)).toEqual({ name: 'home' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.tenants }, impersonating)).toEqual({ name: 'home' })
  })

  it('keeps user management for those who may manage users', () => {
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.users }, admin)).toBeNull()
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.users }, impersonating)).toBeNull()
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.users }, viewer)).toEqual({ name: 'home' })
    expect(accessRedirect({ meta: ROUTE_META.products }, viewer)).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

Replace `conduit-ui/src/router/meta.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * 路由元信息：页面名称（浏览器标题）、面包屑、侧栏菜单归属，以及访问范围（租户页面 / 平台页面）与额外要求（管理用户）。
 * 真实路由表（router/index.ts）与测试用内存路由（test/viewTestUtils.ts）共用 ROUTE_META，保证两边一致。
 */
import type { RouteLocationRaw, RouteMeta } from 'vue-router'

/** 面包屑的一级；有 to 时可点击跳转 */
export interface BreadcrumbItem {
  label: string
  to?: RouteLocationRaw
}

declare module 'vue-router' {
  interface RouteMeta {
    /** 不需要登录即可访问（登录页） */
    public?: boolean
    /** 页面名称，用于浏览器标签标题「<页面名> · Conduit」 */
    title?: string
    /** 面包屑（详情页的对象名称不在这里，由 useBreadcrumb 追加为最后一级） */
    breadcrumb?: BreadcrumbItem[]
    /** 侧栏中高亮的菜单路径：列表页与详情页归同一个菜单 */
    menu?: string
    /**
     * 访问范围：tenant 为租户页面（未代入的平台管理员会被带到租户管理）；platform 为平台页面（其他身份会被带到首页）；
     * 不设表示所有登录用户（首页、404）。规则见 router/access.ts。
     */
    scope?: 'tenant' | 'platform'
    /** 额外要求：manageUsers 只有租户管理员（含代入的平台管理员）可以进入 */
    requires?: 'manageUsers'
  }
}

/** 各路由的元信息，键为路由名称 */
export const ROUTE_META = {
  home: { title: '首页', menu: '/home', breadcrumb: [{ label: '概览' }, { label: '首页' }] },
  login: { public: true, title: '登录' },
  products: {
    title: '产品',
    menu: '/products',
    scope: 'tenant',
    breadcrumb: [{ label: '设备接入' }, { label: '产品' }],
  },
  'product-detail': {
    title: '产品详情',
    menu: '/products',
    scope: 'tenant',
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/router/access.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 路由访问规则（身份与页面是否匹配）：未代入的平台管理员进入租户页面时带到租户管理；其他身份进入平台页面时带到首页；
 * 不能管理用户的身份进入用户管理时带到首页。路由守卫与 AppLayout（会话身份变化时）共用。只做体验上的引导，权限以后端为准。
 */
import type { RouteLocationRaw, RouteMeta } from 'vue-router'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { permissionsOf } from '@/utils/permissions'

/**
 * 计算应当改去的地址。
 * @param to 目标路由（只用到 meta）
 * @param user 当前用户
 * @returns 需要改去的地址；可以访问时为 null
 */
export function accessRedirect(to: { meta: RouteMeta }, user: UserView | null): RouteLocationRaw | null {
  const p = permissionsOf(user)
  if (to.meta.scope === 'platform' && !p.isPlatformAdmin) {
    return { name: 'home' }
  }
  if (to.meta.scope === 'tenant' && p.isPlatformAdmin) {
    return { name: 'tenants' }
  }
  if (to.meta.requires === 'manageUsers' && !p.canManageUsers) {
    return { name: 'home' }
  }
  return null
}
```

```ts
 * 每个路由带 meta（页面名称、面包屑、菜单归属、访问范围，见 router/meta.ts）；身份与页面不匹配时按 router/access.ts 改去合适的页面；

 */
```

```ts
/**
 * 登录守卫：公开页直接放行；未登录时先尝试用 refresh Cookie 恢复会话（页面刷新场景），
 * 恢复失败再跳转登录页并带上 redirect，登录成功后回到原页面。
 */
router.beforeEach(async (to) => {
  if (to.meta.public) {
    return true
  }
  const auth = useAuthStore()
  if (auth.isAuthenticated || (await auth.restore())) {
    return true
  }
  return { name: 'login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath } }
})
```

with

```ts
/**
 * 登录与身份守卫：公开页直接放行；未登录时先尝试用 refresh Cookie 恢复会话（页面刷新场景），
 * 恢复失败再跳转登录页并带上 redirect；已登录时把身份与页面不匹配的访问改去合适的页面（见 router/access.ts）。
 */
router.beforeEach(async (to) => {
  if (to.meta.public) {
    return true
  }
  const auth = useAuthStore()
  if (!auth.isAuthenticated && !(await auth.restore())) {
    return { name: 'login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath } }
  }
  return accessRedirect(to, auth.user) ?? true
})
```

```ts
/**
 * 侧栏菜单定义：按身份给出分组与菜单项（路径、名称、图标）。
 * 平台管理员：概览、系统管理（租户）；租户管理员（含代入的平台管理员）：概览、设备接入、系统管理（用户）；只读用户：概览、设备接入。
 */
import type { Component } from 'vue'
import { Box, Cpu, House, OfficeBuilding, User } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import type { Permissions } from '@/utils/permissions'

/** 菜单项；path 同时是 el-menu 的 index 与跳转地址 */
export interface MenuItem {
  path: string
  label: string
  icon: Component
}

/** 菜单分组 */
export interface MenuGroup {
  title: string
  items: MenuItem[]
}

const OVERVIEW: MenuGroup = { title: '概览', items: [{ path: '/home', label: '首页', icon: House }] }

const DEVICE_ACCESS: MenuGroup = {
  title: '设备接入',
  items: [
    { path: '/products', label: '产品', icon: Box },
    { path: '/devices', label: '设备', icon: Cpu },
  ],
}

/**
 * 按权限给出侧栏分组（按显示顺序）。菜单项 path 与路由 meta.menu 对应，用来在详情页高亮所属菜单。
 */
export function menuGroupsFor(p: Permissions): MenuGroup[] {
  if (p.isPlatformAdmin) {
    return [OVERVIEW, { title: '系统管理', items: [{ path: '/tenants', label: '租户', icon: OfficeBuilding }] }]
  }
  const groups = [OVERVIEW, DEVICE_ACCESS]
  if (p.canManageUsers) {
    groups.push({ title: '系统管理', items: [{ path: '/users', label: '用户', icon: User }] })
  }
  return groups
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 页面组件测试的公共工具：与真实路由同名、同 meta 的内存路由（含租户管理与用户管理），带登录用户的 Pinia，
 * 以及等待异步渲染完成。
 */

import { flushPromises } from '@vue/test-utils'
import { createMemoryHistory, createRouter, type Router } from 'vue-router'
import { defineComponent } from 'vue'
import { createPinia, setActivePinia, type Pinia } from 'pinia'
import { ROUTE_META } from '@/router/meta'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'

const Empty = defineComponent({ template: '<div />' })

/** 与真实路由同名、同 meta 的内存路由，组件全部用空壳，只用来断言跳转、query、面包屑与菜单高亮 */
export function createTestRouter(): Router {
  return createRouter({
    history: createMemoryHistory(),
    routes: [
      { path: '/login', name: 'login', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.login },
      { path: '/home', name: 'home', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.home },
      { path: '/products', name: 'products', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.products },
      {
        path: '/products/:productKey',
        name: 'product-detail',
        component: Empty,
        props: true,
        meta: ROUTE_META['product-detail'],
      },
      { path: '/devices', name: 'devices', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.devices },
      {
        path: '/devices/:deviceKey',
        name: 'device-detail',
        component: Empty,
        props: true,
        meta: ROUTE_META['device-detail'],
      },
      { path: '/tenants', name: 'tenants', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.tenants },
      { path: '/users', name: 'users', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META.users },
      { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', name: 'not-found', component: Empty, meta: ROUTE_META['not-found'] },
    ],
  })
}

/**
 * 创建并激活一个已登录的 Pinia：默认是默认租户的租户管理员 admin，可用 user 覆盖任意字段（如角色、代入者、需改密码）。
 */
export function createTestPinia(user: Partial<UserView> = {}): Pinia {
  const pinia = createPinia()
  setActivePinia(pinia)
  const auth = useAuthStore()
  auth.token = 'token'
  auth.user = {
    username: 'admin',
    displayName: null,
    role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    tenantCode: 'default',
    tenantName: '默认租户',
    mustChangePassword: false,
    impersonatedBy: null,
    ...user,
  }
  return pinia
}

/** 等待挂载后的异步加载和渲染都完成 */
export async function settle() {
  await flushPromises()
  await flushPromises()
}
```

```ts
/**

 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { nextTick } from 'vue'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus, { ElDialog } from 'element-plus'
import ForcePasswordDialog from './ForcePasswordDialog.vue'
import { authApi } from '@/api/auth'
import { meApi } from '@/api/me'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { createTestPinia, createTestRouter, settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({ authApi: { login: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), logout: vi.fn(), exitTenant: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/me', () => ({ meApi: { update: vi.fn(), changePassword: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { enter: vi.fn() } }))

type Vm = {
  form: { oldPassword: string; newPassword: string; confirm: string }
  error: string
  submit(): Promise<void>
  logout(): Promise<void>
}

async function mountDialog(user: Partial<UserView> = {}) {
  const pinia = createTestPinia(user)
  const router = createTestRouter()
  await router.push('/home')
  await router.isReady()
  const wrapper = mount(ForcePasswordDialog, { global: { plugins: [pinia, router, ElementPlus] }, attachTo: document.body })
  await settle()
  return { wrapper, router, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

describe('ForcePasswordDialog', () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks())

  it('opens for a user who must change the password and cannot be closed', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountDialog({ mustChangePassword: true })

    const dialog = wrapper.findComponent(ElDialog)
    expect(dialog.props('modelValue')).toBe(true)
    expect(dialog.props('showClose')).toBe(false)
    expect(dialog.props('closeOnClickModal')).toBe(false)
    expect(dialog.props('closeOnPressEscape')).toBe(false)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('stays closed otherwise and opens when an endpoint asks for a password change', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountDialog()
    expect(wrapper.findComponent(ElDialog).props('modelValue')).toBe(false)

    useAuthStore().passwordChangeRequired = true
    await nextTick()

    expect(wrapper.findComponent(ElDialog).props('modelValue')).toBe(true)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('checks the new password locally before calling the server', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog({ mustChangePassword: true })

    vm.form.oldPassword = 'Init-pass-2026'
    vm.form.newPassword = 'short1'
    vm.form.confirm = 'short1'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字')

    vm.form.newPassword = 'Active-pass-2026'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Active-pass-2027'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('两次输入的新密码不一致')
    expect(meApi.changePassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('changes the password and closes', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.changePassword).mockResolvedValue({
      accessToken: 'fresh',
      expiresIn: 900,
      user: {
        username: 'admin',
        displayName: null,
        role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
        tenantCode: 'default',
        tenantName: '默认租户',
        mustChangePassword: false,
        impersonatedBy: null,
      },
    })
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog({ mustChangePassword: true })

    vm.form.oldPassword = 'Init-pass-2026'
    vm.form.newPassword = 'Active-pass-2026'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Active-pass-2026'
    await vm.submit()
    await settle()

    expect(meApi.changePassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ oldPassword: 'Init-pass-2026', newPassword: 'Active-pass-2026' })
    expect(useAuthStore().token).toBe('fresh')
    expect(wrapper.findComponent(ElDialog).props('modelValue')).toBe(false)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the server error inside the dialog', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.changePassword).mockRejectedValue(new Error('原密码错误'))
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog({ mustChangePassword: true })

    vm.form.oldPassword = 'Wrong-pass-1'
    vm.form.newPassword = 'Active-pass-2026'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Active-pass-2026'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(vm.error).toBe('原密码错误')
    expect(wrapper.findComponent(ElDialog).props('modelValue')).toBe(true)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('logs out', async () => {
    vi.mocked(authApi.logout).mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper, router, vm } = await mountDialog({ mustChangePassword: true })

    await vm.logout()
    await settle()

    expect(useAuthStore().token).toBeNull()
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.name).toBe('login')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 代入提示条：平台管理员进入租户后固定显示在顶栏下方，提示「正在以平台管理员身份操作租户：XX」；
 * 「退出」换回平台管理员自己的令牌并回到租户管理。未代入时不渲染。
 */
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { WarningFilled } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { usePermission } from '@/composables/usePermission'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const router = useRouter()
const { isImpersonating } = usePermission()
const exiting = ref(false)

/** 退出租户：成功后回到租户管理；失败时提示接口错误并留在当前页面。 */
async function exit() {
  if (exiting.value) return
  exiting.value = true
  try {
    await auth.exitTenant()
    await router.replace({ name: 'tenants' })
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  } finally {
    exiting.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="isImpersonating" class="impersonation-bar" role="status">
    <el-icon class="icon"><WarningFilled /></el-icon>
    <span class="text">
      正在以平台管理员身份操作租户：<strong>{{ auth.user?.tenantName ?? auth.user?.tenantCode }}</strong>
    </span>
    <el-button size="small" type="warning" :loading="exiting" @click="exit">退出</el-button>
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.impersonation-bar {
  display: flex;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--cd-space-2);
  padding: var(--cd-space-2) var(--cd-space-4);
  color: var(--el-text-color-primary);
  background: var(--el-color-warning-light-9);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--el-color-warning-light-5);
}
.icon {
  color: var(--el-color-warning);
}
.text {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}
</style>
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/ForcePasswordDialog.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 强制修改密码对话框：用户带「需改密码」标记（首次登录、密码被重置）或任一接口返回 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED 时弹出，不能关闭。
 * 新密码先按本地规则校验（8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字，两次一致）；修改成功后换用新令牌并自动关闭。也可以退出登录。
 */
import { computed, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import { passwordProblem } from '@/utils/accountRules'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const router = useRouter()
const form = reactive({ oldPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirm: '' })
const error = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)
const visible = computed(() => auth.needsPasswordChange)

/** 校验并提交：原密码必填、新密码合规、两次一致；失败时把原因显示在对话框内。 */
async function submit() {
  if (saving.value) return
  error.value = ''
  if (!form.oldPassword) {
    error.value = '请输入原密码'
    return
  }
  const problem = passwordProblem(form.newPassword)
  if (problem) {
    error.value = problem
    return
  }
  if (form.newPassword !== form.confirm) {
    error.value = '两次输入的新密码不一致'
    return
  }
  saving.value = true
  try {
    await auth.changePassword(form.oldPassword, form.newPassword)
    form.oldPassword = ''
    form.newPassword = ''
    form.confirm = ''
    ElMessage.success('密码已修改')
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}

/** 退出登录：无论请求是否成功都回到登录页。 */
async function logout() {
  try {
    await auth.logout()
  } catch {
    // 登出请求失败也要回到登录页
  } finally {
    await router.replace({ name: 'login' })
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-dialog
    :model-value="visible"
    title="请修改密码"
    width="min(420px, 92vw)"
    :show-close="false"
    :close-on-click-modal="false"
    :close-on-press-escape="false"
    append-to-body
  >
    <p class="cd-muted hint">首次登录或密码被重置后，需要先设置新密码才能继续使用。</p>
    <el-form label-position="top" @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-alert v-if="error" class="error" type="error" :title="error" show-icon :closable="false" />
      <el-form-item label="原密码">
        <el-input v-model="form.oldPassword" type="password" show-password autocomplete="current-password" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="新密码">
        <el-input
          v-model="form.newPassword"
          type="password"
          show-password
          autocomplete="new-password"
          placeholder="8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字"
        />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="确认新密码">
        <el-input v-model="form.confirm" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" />
      </el-form-item>
    </el-form>
    <template #footer>
      <el-button @click="logout">退出登录</el-button>

}
.error {
```

```ts
  })

  })

    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices')

    expect(useUiStore().sidebarCollapsed).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.collapse-toggle').attributes('aria-label')).toBe('展开菜单')

    const space = new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: ' ', cancelable: true, bubbles: true })
    wrapper.find('.collapse-toggle').element.dispatchEvent(space)
    expect(useUiStore().sidebarCollapsed).toBe(false)
    expect(space.defaultPrevented).toBe(true)

    expect(wrapper.find('.user').attributes('aria-label')).toBe('用户菜单')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

    expect(wrapper.find('.sidebar').classes()).toContain('cd-sidebar--dark')
    useUiStore().setSidebarStyle('light')

    expect(wrapper.find('.sidebar').classes()).toContain('cd-sidebar--light')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

    expect(crumbs).toEqual(['设备接入', '设备'])
    expect(wrapper.find('.tenant').text()).toBe('默认租户')
    expect(wrapper.find('.el-avatar').text()).toBe('A')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('names the user by display name and role', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices', { displayName: '华东运维 张工', role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' })
    const vm = wrapper.vm as unknown as { userName: string; roleText: string }

    expect(vm.userName).toBe('华东运维 张工')
    expect(vm.roleText).toBe('只读用户')
    expect(wrapper.find('.el-avatar').text()).toBe('华')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('logs out from the user menu and returns to the login page', async () => {
    const { wrapper, router } = await mountAt('/devices')

    ;(wrapper.vm as unknown as { onCommand(command: string): void }).onCommand('logout')
    await settle()

    expect(authApi.logout).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
    expect(useAuthStore().user).toBeNull()
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.name).toBe('login')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the 概览 group with 首页 highlighted on the home page', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/home')

    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain('概览')
    expect(wrapper.find('.el-menu-item.is-active').text()).toBe('首页')
    expect(wrapper.findAll('.breadcrumb .el-breadcrumb__inner').map((c) => c.text())).toEqual(['概览', '首页'])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the impersonation bar only while impersonating', async () => {
    const plain = await mountAt('/devices')
    expect(plain.wrapper.find('.impersonation-bar').exists()).toBe(false)
    plain.wrapper.unmount()

    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices', { username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin', tenantName: '验收租户（华东）' })
    expect(wrapper.find('.main > .impersonation-bar').text()).toContain('验收租户（华东）')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('leaves a tenant page when the session turns into the platform admin', async () => {
    const { wrapper, router } = await mountAt('/products', { username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' })

    useAuthStore().user = {
      username: 'sysadmin',
      displayName: null,
      role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN',
      tenantCode: null,
      tenantName: null,
      mustChangePassword: false,
      impersonatedBy: null,
    }
    await settle()

    expect(router.currentRoute.value.name).toBe('tenants')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  describe('on a narrow screen', () => {
    const originalMatchMedia = window.matchMedia

    beforeEach(() => {
      window.matchMedia = ((query: string) => ({
        matches: query === '(max-width: 819px)',
        media: query,
        addEventListener: () => {},
        removeEventListener: () => {},
      })) as unknown as typeof window.matchMedia
    })

    afterEach(() => {
      window.matchMedia = originalMatchMedia
    })

    it('forces the sidebar collapsed, hides the toggle and leaves the saved preference unchanged', async () => {
      localStorage.setItem(UI_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ sidebarCollapsed: false }))
      const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices')

      expect(wrapper.find('.el-menu').classes()).toContain('el-menu--collapse')
      expect(wrapper.find('.collapse-toggle').exists()).toBe(false)
      expect(useUiStore().sidebarCollapsed).toBe(false)
      expect(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_STORAGE_KEY)!).sidebarCollapsed).toBe(false)
      wrapper.unmount()
    })
  })

  it('renders the logo in the sidebar, with the dark-bg tone on the dark sidebar and also when collapsed', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices')

    expect(wrapper.find('.brand svg.conduit-logo').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.brand svg.conduit-logo').attributes('data-tone')).toBe(useUiStore().sidebarStyle === 'dark' ? 'dark-bg' : 'auto')
    expect(wrapper.find('.brand-name').text()).toBe('Conduit')
    useUiStore().toggleSidebar()
    await nextTick()
    expect(wrapper.find('.brand svg.conduit-logo').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.brand-name').exists()).toBe(false)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('keeps the shell one screen tall: sidebar with the toggle inside it, header fixed, only el-main scrolls', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountAt('/devices')

    const layout = wrapper.find('.layout')
    expect(layout.classes()).toContain('el-container')
    expect(wrapper.find('.sidebar .collapse-toggle').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.main > .header').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.main > .el-main.content').exists()).toBe(true)
    // 页面里只有一个 el-main，它就是唯一的滚动区
    expect(wrapper.findAll('.el-main')).toHaveLength(1)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('declares the one-screen and main-scroll styles in the component source', () => {
    const source = readFileSync(resolve(__dirname, 'AppLayout.vue'), 'utf-8')
    expect(source).toMatch(/\.layout\s*\{[^}]*height:\s*100vh;[^}]*height:\s*100dvh;[^}]*overflow:\s*hidden/)
    expect(source).toMatch(/\.content\s*\{[^}]*overflow:\s*auto/)
    expect(source).toMatch(/\.menu\s*\{[^}]*min-height:\s*0/)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Rewrite the layout**

Replace `conduit-ui/src/layouts/AppLayout.vue` with:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 登录后的整体布局：外壳恰好一屏高（100vh），侧栏固定在左侧、「收起菜单」钉在其底部，顶栏固定，只有右侧内容区（el-main）滚动。
 * 左侧侧栏：Logo、按身份给出的分组菜单（带图标，见 layouts/menu.ts），按路由 meta.menu 高亮；
 * 可折叠（偏好保存，窗口宽度 < 820px 时强制折叠），浅色/深色两种风格（偏好）。
 * 顶栏：左侧全局面包屑（路由 meta + 详情页对象名称），右侧外观设置、租户名称、用户头像下拉（显示名称、角色、租户、退出登录）。
 * 顶栏下方：平台管理员代入租户时的提示条。页面渲染在 router-view；需要先改密码时弹出不可关闭的强制改密码对话框。
 * 会话身份变化（如代入的租户被停用、续期后退回平台管理员）时，按 router/access 的规则离开不匹配的页面。
 */
import { computed, watch } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ArrowDown, Brush, Expand, Fold } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { useUiStore } from '@/stores/ui'
import { useCurrentPage } from '@/composables/useBreadcrumb'
import { useMediaQuery } from '@/composables/useMediaQuery'
import { displayNameOf, permissionsOf, roleLabel } from '@/utils/permissions'
import { accessRedirect } from '@/router/access'
import ConduitLogo from '@/components/ConduitLogo.vue'
import AppearancePanel from '@/components/AppearancePanel.vue'
import ImpersonationBar from '@/components/ImpersonationBar.vue'
import ForcePasswordDialog from '@/components/ForcePasswordDialog.vue'
import { menuGroupsFor } from './menu'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const ui = useUiStore()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const { breadcrumb } = useCurrentPage()
const narrow = useMediaQuery('(max-width: 819px)')

/** 是否折叠：窄屏强制折叠，否则按偏好 */
const collapsed = computed(() => narrow.value || ui.sidebarCollapsed)
/** 高亮的菜单：列表页与详情页通过 meta.menu 归到同一项 */
const activeMenu = computed(() => route.meta.menu ?? '')
/** 当前身份可见的菜单分组 */
const menuGroups = computed(() => menuGroupsFor(permissionsOf(auth.user)))
/** 用户菜单与头像显示的名称：显示名称，未设置时用用户名 */
const userName = computed(() => displayNameOf(auth.user))
/** 当前角色的中文名称 */
const roleText = computed(() => (auth.user ? roleLabel(auth.user.role) : ''))
/** 头像文字：名称首字（大写） */
const avatarText = computed(() => (userName.value || '?').slice(0, 1).toUpperCase())

// 会话身份变化后，当前页面可能已不属于新身份（例如续期时代入被取消）
watch(
  () => auth.user,
  (user) => {
    if (!user) return
    const target = accessRedirect(route, user)
    if (target) {
      void router.replace(target)
    }
  },
)

/** 退出登录：无论登出请求是否成功，都回到登录页。 */
async function logout() {
  try {
    await auth.logout()
  } catch {
    // 登出请求失败也要回到登录页
  } finally {
    await router.replace({ name: 'login' })
  }
}

/** 用户下拉菜单的命令：logout 退出登录 */
function onCommand(command: string) {
  if (command === 'logout') {
    void logout()
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-container class="layout">
    <el-aside :width="collapsed ? 'var(--cd-sidebar-collapsed-width)' : 'var(--cd-sidebar-width)'" :class="['sidebar', `cd-sidebar--${ui.sidebarStyle}`]">
      <div class="brand">
        <ConduitLogo :size="28" :tone="ui.sidebarStyle === 'dark' ? 'dark-bg' : 'auto'" />
        <span v-if="!collapsed" class="brand-name">Conduit</span>
      </div>
      <el-menu :default-active="activeMenu" :collapse="collapsed" :collapse-transition="false" router class="menu">
        <el-menu-item-group v-for="group in menuGroups" :key="group.title" :title="group.title">
          <el-menu-item v-for="item in group.items" :key="item.path" :index="item.path">
            <el-icon><component :is="item.icon" /></el-icon>
            <template #title>
              <span>{{ item.label }}</span>
            </template>
          </el-menu-item>
        </el-menu-item-group>
      </el-menu>
      <div
        v-if="!narrow"
        class="collapse-toggle"
        role="button"
        tabindex="0"
        :title="collapsed ? '展开菜单' : '收起菜单'"
        :aria-label="collapsed ? '展开菜单' : '收起菜单'"
        @click="ui.toggleSidebar()"
        @keydown.enter="ui.toggleSidebar()"
        @keydown.space.prevent="ui.toggleSidebar()"
      >
        <el-icon><Expand v-if="collapsed" /><Fold v-else /></el-icon>
        <span v-if="!collapsed">收起菜单</span>
      </div>
    </el-aside>

    <el-container class="main">
      <el-header class="header">
        <el-breadcrumb separator="/" class="breadcrumb">
          <el-breadcrumb-item v-for="(item, i) in breadcrumb" :key="i" :to="item.to">{{ item.label }}</el-breadcrumb-item>
        </el-breadcrumb>
        <div class="header-right">
          <el-popover trigger="click" placement="bottom-end" :width="300">
            <template #reference>
              <el-button circle :icon="Brush" title="外观设置" aria-label="外观设置" />
            </template>
            <AppearancePanel />
          </el-popover>
          <el-tag v-if="auth.user?.tenantName" type="info" class="tenant">{{ auth.user.tenantName }}</el-tag>
          <el-dropdown trigger="click" @command="onCommand">
            <span class="user" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="用户菜单">
              <el-avatar :size="28">{{ avatarText }}</el-avatar>
              <el-icon><ArrowDown /></el-icon>
            </span>
            <template #dropdown>
              <el-dropdown-menu>
                <el-dropdown-item disabled>{{ userName }}</el-dropdown-item>
                <el-dropdown-item disabled>角色：{{ roleText }}</el-dropdown-item>
                <el-dropdown-item v-if="auth.user?.tenantName" disabled>租户：{{ auth.user.tenantName }}</el-dropdown-item>
                <el-dropdown-item divided command="logout">退出登录</el-dropdown-item>
              </el-dropdown-menu>
            </template>
          </el-dropdown>
        </div>
      </el-header>
      <ImpersonationBar />
      <el-main class="content">
        <router-view />
      </el-main>
    </el-container>
    <ForcePasswordDialog />
  </el-container>
</template>

<style scoped>
.layout {
  height: 100vh;
  height: 100dvh;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.sidebar {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--cd-sidebar-bg);
  border-right: 1px solid var(--cd-sidebar-border);
  transition: width 0.2s;
}
.cd-sidebar--dark {
  --cd-sidebar-bg: var(--cd-sidebar-dark-bg);
  --cd-sidebar-text: var(--cd-sidebar-dark-text);
  --cd-sidebar-muted: var(--cd-sidebar-dark-muted);
  --cd-sidebar-hover: var(--cd-sidebar-dark-hover);

}
.cd-sidebar--light {
  --cd-sidebar-bg: var(--cd-sidebar-light-bg);
  --cd-sidebar-text: var(--cd-sidebar-light-text);
  --cd-sidebar-muted: var(--cd-sidebar-light-muted);
  --cd-sidebar-hover: var(--cd-sidebar-light-hover);
  --cd-sidebar-active-bg: var(--cd-sidebar-light-active-bg);
  --cd-sidebar-active-text: var(--cd-sidebar-light-active-text);

}
.brand {
  height: var(--cd-header-height);
  flex-shrink: 0;

}
```

---


## Task 7: Frontend platform home and tenant management

Adds the platform home (spec §5.2: four tiles and 「设备最多的租户」), the `/home` entry that picks the platform or tenant home, and the tenant management page with the create dialog, the edit dialog, the users drawer with password reset, disable/enable/delete and the enter-tenant confirmation.

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/views/HomeEntry.vue`, `HomeEntry.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/views/PlatformHomeView.vue`, `PlatformHomeView.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/views/TenantsView.vue`, `TenantsView.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/components/TenantCreateDialog.vue`, `TenantCreateDialog.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/components/TenantUsersDrawer.vue`, `TenantUsersDrawer.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/components/ResetPasswordDialog.vue`, `ResetPasswordDialog.spec.ts`
- Modify: `conduit-ui/src/router/index.ts` (home entry, `tenants` route)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 6): `platformApi.*`, types `PlatformOverview`, `TenantView`, `TenantUserView`, `AccountStatus`; `useAuthStore().enterTenant(code)`; `usePermission().isPlatformAdmin`; `statusLabel`, `roleLabel`; `tenantCodeProblem`, `usernameProblem`, `passwordProblem`; `ROUTE_META.tenants`; `createTestPinia`, `createTestRouter`, `settle`. Existing: `useTable`, `FilterBar`, `PageHeader`, `createVisiblePoller`, `createRequestSeq`, `formatCount`, `formatDateTime`, `onlineRate`, `PAGE_SIZES`, `problemMessage`.
- Produces:
  - `ResetPasswordDialog` props `modelValue: boolean`, `username: string`, `save: (password: string) => Promise<void>` — Task 8 reuses it for tenant users.
  - Route `tenants` → `/tenants` (`TenantsView`, reads `?keyword=` once on mount); route `home` → `HomeEntry`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `/home` stays one address: `HomeEntry` renders `PlatformHomeView` for an un-impersonated platform admin and the existing `HomeView` otherwise, so breadcrumbs, menus and the login fallback need no special case.
- The platform home refreshes every 30 s like the tenant home and reuses its layout classes; its tiles are 租户 (启用/停用), 用户, 设备 (产品数), 在线率 (progress bar, 「—」 when unknown); clicking 租户 opens the tenant list, clicking a top tenant opens it filtered by that code.
- The tenant list's 「进入」 is disabled for disabled tenants; the default tenant shows no 停用/启用/删除 (the server also refuses with 409). Disabling and deleting ask for confirmation; enabling does not.
- After entering, the page goes to `/home` (now the tenant home of the entered tenant); exiting is handled by the impersonation bar (Task 6).
- The users drawer lists all users of the tenant (not paged, spec §4.1) and offers 「重置密码」 only on `TENANT_ADMIN` rows.

```ts
/** HomeEntry 组件测试：未代入的平台管理员看到平台首页；租户用户与代入的平台管理员看到租户首页 */
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import HomeEntry from './HomeEntry.vue'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { createTestPinia } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('./HomeView.vue', async () => {
  const { defineComponent, h } = await import('vue')
  return { default: defineComponent({ render: () => h('div', { class: 'tenant-home' }) }) }
})
vi.mock('./PlatformHomeView.vue', async () => {
  const { defineComponent, h } = await import('vue')
  return { default: defineComponent({ render: () => h('div', { class: 'platform-home' }) }) }
})

function mountAs(user: Partial<UserView>) {
  return mount(HomeEntry, { global: { plugins: [createTestPinia(user)] } })
}

describe('HomeEntry', () => {
  it('shows the platform home to a platform admin', () => {
    const wrapper = mountAs({ username: 'sysadmin', role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null })

    expect(wrapper.find('.platform-home').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(wrapper.find('.tenant-home').exists()).toBe(false)
  })

  it('shows the tenant home to tenant users and to an impersonating platform admin', () => {
    expect(mountAs({ role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' }).find('.tenant-home').exists()).toBe(true)
    expect(mountAs({ username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' }).find('.tenant-home').exists()).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/PlatformHomeView.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** PlatformHomeView 组件测试：4 个指标卡的数值与副文、在线相关为 null 时显示「—」、点击租户卡与租户行进入租户管理（带筛选）、每 30 秒刷新 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import PlatformHomeView from './PlatformHomeView.vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { PlatformOverview } from '@/api/types'
import { createTestPinia, createTestRouter, settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { overview: vi.fn() } }))

function overview(patch: Partial<PlatformOverview> = {}): PlatformOverview {
  return {
    tenantCount: 3,
    activeTenantCount: 2,
    disabledTenantCount: 1,
    userCount: 12,
    productCount: 4,
    deviceCount: 200,
    onlineCount: 100,
    topTenants: [
      { code: 'east', name: '验收租户（华东）', deviceCount: 150, onlineCount: 80 },
      { code: 'default', name: 'Default', deviceCount: 50, onlineCount: 20 },
    ],
    ...patch,
  }
}

async function mountView() {
  const pinia = createTestPinia({ username: 'sysadmin', role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null })
  const router = createTestRouter()
  await router.push('/home')
  await router.isReady()
  const wrapper = mount(PlatformHomeView, { global: { plugins: [pinia, router, ElementPlus] }, attachTo: document.body })
  await settle()
  return { wrapper, router }
}

const tile = (wrapper: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof mountView>>['wrapper'], name: string) =>
  wrapper.find(`[data-test="tile-${name}"]`).text()

describe('PlatformHomeView', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout', 'setInterval', 'clearInterval'] })
    Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', { configurable: true, get: () => false })
    vi.clearAllMocks()
    vi.mocked(platformApi.overview).mockResolvedValue(overview())
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers()
  })

  it('renders the four tiles', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    expect(tile(wrapper, 'tenants')).toContain('3')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'tenants')).toContain('启用 2 · 停用 1')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'users')).toContain('12')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'devices')).toContain('200')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'devices')).toContain('共 4 个产品')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'online')).toContain('50%')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'online')).toContain('在线 100 / 设备 200')
    expect(wrapper.find('.top-tenants').text()).toContain('80 / 150')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows — when the online state is unknown', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.overview).mockResolvedValue(
      overview({ onlineCount: null, topTenants: [{ code: 'east', name: '验收租户（华东）', deviceCount: 150, onlineCount: null }] }),
    )
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    expect(tile(wrapper, 'online')).toContain('—')
    expect(tile(wrapper, 'online')).toContain('在线状态暂不可用')
    expect(wrapper.find('.top-tenants').text()).toContain('— / 150')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('opens the tenant list, filtered when a top tenant is clicked', async () => {
    const { wrapper, router } = await mountView()

    await wrapper.find('.top-tenants .row').trigger('click')
    await settle()
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.name).toBe('tenants')
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.query).toEqual({ keyword: 'east' })

    await router.push('/home')
    await wrapper.find('[data-test="tile-tenants"]').trigger('click')
    await settle()
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.fullPath).toBe('/tenants')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('refreshes every 30 seconds and stops on unmount', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()
    expect(platformApi.overview).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)

    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000)
    expect(platformApi.overview).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)

    wrapper.unmount()
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(90_000)
    expect(platformApi.overview).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/ResetPasswordDialog.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** ResetPasswordDialog 组件测试：密码不合规或两次不一致时不调用 save；成功后关闭；save 失败时把原因显示在对话框内 */
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import ResetPasswordDialog from './ResetPasswordDialog.vue'

type Vm = { form: { password: string; confirm: string }; error: string; submit(): Promise<void> }

function mountDialog(save: (password: string) => Promise<void>) {
  const wrapper = mount(ResetPasswordDialog, {
    props: { modelValue: true, username: 'east-admin', save },
    global: { plugins: [ElementPlus] },
    attachTo: document.body,
  })
  return { wrapper, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

describe('ResetPasswordDialog', () => {
  it('checks the password before saving', async () => {
    const save = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(save)

    vm.form.password = 'weak'
    vm.form.confirm = 'weak'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字')

    vm.form.password = 'Reset-pass-9'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Reset-pass-8'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('两次输入的密码不一致')
    expect(save).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('saves and closes', async () => {
    const save = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(save)

    vm.form.password = 'Reset-pass-9'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Reset-pass-9'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(save).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Reset-pass-9')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toEqual([[false]])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the server error', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('只能重置租户管理员的密码')))

    vm.form.password = 'Reset-pass-9'
    vm.form.confirm = 'Reset-pass-9'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(vm.error).toBe('只能重置租户管理员的密码')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toBeUndefined()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/TenantCreateDialog.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** TenantCreateDialog 组件测试：编码、名称、用户名、密码按规则校验后才请求；成功后发出 created 并关闭；接口错误显示在对话框内 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import TenantCreateDialog from './TenantCreateDialog.vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { TenantView } from '@/api/types'

vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { createTenant: vi.fn() } }))

type Vm = {
  form: { code: string; name: string; username: string; displayName: string; password: string }
  error: string
  submit(): Promise<void>
}

const created: TenantView = {
  code: 'east',
  name: '验收租户（华东）',
  status: 'ACTIVE',
  adminCount: 1,
  userCount: 1,
  productCount: 0,
  deviceCount: 0,
  onlineCount: 0,
  createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z',
}

function mountDialog() {
  const wrapper = mount(TenantCreateDialog, {
    props: { modelValue: true },
    global: { plugins: [ElementPlus] },
    attachTo: document.body,
  })
  return { wrapper, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

function fill(vm: Vm) {
  vm.form.code = 'east'
  vm.form.name = ' 验收租户（华东） '
  vm.form.username = 'east-admin'
  vm.form.displayName = ''
  vm.form.password = 'Init-pass-2026'
}

describe('TenantCreateDialog', () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks())

  it('validates the fields before calling the server', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog()

    fill(vm)
    vm.form.code = 'East_1'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toContain('租户编码须为 2～32 位')

    fill(vm)
    vm.form.name = '  '
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('请输入租户名称')

    fill(vm)
    vm.form.username = '1abc'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toContain('用户名须为 3～32 位')

    fill(vm)
    vm.form.password = 'weak'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字')
    expect(platformApi.createTenant).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('creates the tenant with its first admin and closes', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.createTenant).mockResolvedValue(created)
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog()

    fill(vm)
    await vm.submit()

    expect(platformApi.createTenant).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      code: 'east',
      name: '验收租户（华东）',
      admin: { username: 'east-admin', displayName: null, password: 'Init-pass-2026' },
    })
    expect(wrapper.emitted('created')).toEqual([[created]])
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toEqual([[false]])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the server error', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.createTenant).mockRejectedValue(new Error('租户编码已存在: east'))
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog()

    fill(vm)
    await vm.submit()

    expect(vm.error).toBe('租户编码已存在: east')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('created')).toBeUndefined()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

```ts
/** TenantUsersDrawer 组件测试：打开时加载该租户的用户；角色、状态与待改密码标签；只有租户管理员行有「重置密码」；重置后重新加载 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import TenantUsersDrawer from './TenantUsersDrawer.vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { TenantUserView, TenantView } from '@/api/types'
import { settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { tenantUsers: vi.fn(), resetAdminPassword: vi.fn() } }))

const tenant: TenantView = {
  code: 'east',
  name: '验收租户（华东）',
  status: 'ACTIVE',
  adminCount: 1,
  userCount: 2,
  productCount: 0,
  deviceCount: 0,
  onlineCount: 0,
  createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z',
}

const users: TenantUserView[] = [
  { username: 'east-admin', displayName: '华东管理员', role: 'TENANT_ADMIN', status: 'ACTIVE', mustChangePassword: true, createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' },
  { username: 'east-viewer', displayName: null, role: 'TENANT_VIEWER', status: 'DISABLED', mustChangePassword: false, createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' },
]

async function mountDrawer() {
  const wrapper = mount(TenantUsersDrawer, {
    props: { modelValue: true, tenant },
    global: { plugins: [ElementPlus] },
    attachTo: document.body,
  })
  await settle()
  return wrapper
}

describe('TenantUsersDrawer', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks()
    vi.mocked(platformApi.tenantUsers).mockResolvedValue(users)
    vi.mocked(platformApi.resetAdminPassword).mockResolvedValue(undefined)
  })

  it('lists the tenant users with role and status tags', async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountDrawer()

    expect(platformApi.tenantUsers).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east')
    const text = wrapper.find('.el-table').text()
    expect(text).toContain('华东管理员')
    expect(text).toContain('租户管理员')
    expect(text).toContain('只读用户')
    expect(text).toContain('禁用')
    expect(text).toContain('待改密码')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('offers a password reset only for tenant admins and reloads after it', async () => {
    const wrapper = await mountDrawer()

    const resetButtons = wrapper.findAll('.el-table button').filter((b) => b.text() === '重置密码')
    expect(resetButtons).toHaveLength(1)

    const vm = wrapper.vm as unknown as { openReset(u: TenantUserView): void; savePassword(p: string): Promise<void> }
    vm.openReset(users[0])
    await vm.savePassword('Reset-pass-9')

    expect(platformApi.resetAdminPassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east', 'east-admin', 'Reset-pass-9')
    expect(platformApi.tenantUsers).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/TenantsView.spec.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * TenantsView 组件测试：首屏第 1 页 10 条并带上 query 中的关键字、状态标签与计数、默认租户不显示停用与删除、停用租户不能进入、
 * 确认后进入租户并回到首页、停用后刷新、筛选与重置、改名、删除、空列表引导新建。
 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus, { ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import TenantsView from './TenantsView.vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import type { TenantView } from '@/api/types'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { createTestPinia, createTestRouter, settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({
  platformApi: {
    overview: vi.fn(),
    listTenants: vi.fn(),
    createTenant: vi.fn(),
    updateTenant: vi.fn(),
    disableTenant: vi.fn(),
    enableTenant: vi.fn(),
    removeTenant: vi.fn(),
    tenantUsers: vi.fn(),
    resetAdminPassword: vi.fn(),
    enter: vi.fn(),
  },
}))
vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({ authApi: { login: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), logout: vi.fn(), exitTenant: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/me', () => ({ meApi: { update: vi.fn(), changePassword: vi.fn() } }))
// 确认框直接确认
vi.mock('element-plus', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('element-plus')>()
  return { ...actual, ElMessageBox: { ...actual.ElMessageBox, confirm: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('confirm') } }
})

function tenant(patch: Partial<TenantView> = {}): TenantView {
  return {
    code: 'east',
    name: '验收租户（华东）',
    status: 'ACTIVE',
    adminCount: 1,
    userCount: 2,
    productCount: 1,
    deviceCount: 5,
    onlineCount: null,
    createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z',
    ...patch,
  }
}

type Vm = {
  filters: { keyword: string; status: string }
  editName: string
  createVisible: boolean
  search(): void
  reset(): void
  openEdit(row: TenantView): void
  saveEdit(): Promise<void>
}

async function mountView(url = '/tenants') {
  const pinia = createTestPinia({ username: 'sysadmin', role: 'PLATFORM_ADMIN', tenantCode: null, tenantName: null })
  const router = createTestRouter()
  await router.push(url)
  await router.isReady()
  const wrapper = mount(TenantsView, { global: { plugins: [pinia, router, ElementPlus] }, attachTo: document.body })
  await settle()
  return { wrapper, router, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

function rowButtons(wrapper: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof mountView>>['wrapper'], index: number) {
  return wrapper.findAll('.el-table__body tr')[index].findAll('button')
}

describe('TenantsView', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks()
    vi.mocked(platformApi.listTenants).mockResolvedValue({
      items: [
        tenant(),
        tenant({ code: 'south', name: '验收租户（华南）', status: 'DISABLED', onlineCount: 0 }),
        tenant({ code: 'default', name: 'Default', userCount: 1 }),
      ],
      total: 3,
      page: 1,
      size: 10,
    })
  })

  it('requests the first page with 10 rows and the keyword from the query', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView('/tenants?keyword=east')

    expect(platformApi.listTenants).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ keyword: 'east', status: undefined, page: 1, size: 10 })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows status tags and counts', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    const text = wrapper.find('.el-table').text()
    expect(text).toContain('启用')
    expect(text).toContain('停用')
    expect(text).toContain('2（1）')
    expect(wrapper.findAll('.device-count').map((c) => c.text())).toEqual(['— / 5', '0 / 5', '— / 5'])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('hides disable and delete for the default tenant and blocks entering a disabled tenant', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    expect(rowButtons(wrapper, 2).map((b) => b.text())).toEqual(['进入', '编辑', '用户'])
    const enterSouth = rowButtons(wrapper, 1).find((b) => b.text() === '进入')!
    expect(enterSouth.attributes('disabled')).toBeDefined()
    expect(rowButtons(wrapper, 1).map((b) => b.text())).toContain('启用')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('enters a tenant after confirming and goes home', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.enter).mockResolvedValue({
      accessToken: 'imp',
      expiresIn: 900,
      user: {
        username: 'sysadmin',
        displayName: null,
        role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
        tenantCode: 'east',
        tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
        mustChangePassword: false,
        impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin',
      },
    })
    const { wrapper, router } = await mountView()

    await rowButtons(wrapper, 0).find((b) => b.text() === '进入')!.trigger('click')
    await settle()

    expect(vi.mocked(ElMessageBox.confirm).mock.calls[0][0]).toBe(
      '将以租户管理员权限操作租户「验收租户（华东）」的数据，所有修改都会被记录',
    )
    expect(platformApi.enter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east')
    expect(useAuthStore().user?.impersonatedBy).toBe('sysadmin')
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.name).toBe('home')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('disables a tenant after confirming and reloads', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.disableTenant).mockResolvedValue(tenant({ status: 'DISABLED' }))
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    await rowButtons(wrapper, 0).find((b) => b.text() === '停用')!.trigger('click')
    await settle()

    expect(platformApi.disableTenant).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east')
    expect(platformApi.listTenants).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('filters by status and resets', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    vm.filters.status = 'DISABLED'
    vm.search()
    await settle()
    expect(platformApi.listTenants).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ keyword: undefined, status: 'DISABLED', page: 1, size: 10 })

    vm.reset()
    await settle()
    expect(platformApi.listTenants).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ keyword: undefined, status: undefined, page: 1, size: 10 })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('renames a tenant', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.updateTenant).mockResolvedValue(tenant({ name: '验收租户（华东二区）' }))
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    vm.openEdit(tenant())
    vm.editName = ' 验收租户（华东二区） '
    await vm.saveEdit()

    expect(platformApi.updateTenant).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east', { name: '验收租户（华东二区）' })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('deletes a tenant after confirming', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.removeTenant).mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    await rowButtons(wrapper, 0).find((b) => b.text() === '删除')!.trigger('click')
    await settle()

    expect(platformApi.removeTenant).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('guides to create the first tenant when the list is empty', async () => {
    vi.mocked(platformApi.listTenants).mockResolvedValue({ items: [], total: 0, page: 1, size: 10 })
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    const empty = wrapper.find('.el-empty')
    expect(empty.text()).toContain('还没有租户')
    await empty.findAll('button').find((b) => b.text() === '新建租户')!.trigger('click')

    expect(vm.createVisible).toBe(true)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the reset-password dialog**

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/ResetPasswordDialog.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 重置密码对话框：输入两次新的初始密码，按密码规则校验后调用 save（由父组件决定调用哪个接口：平台重置租户管理员、租户管理员重置本租户用户）；
 * 成功后提示「对方下次登录需修改密码」并关闭，失败时把原因显示在对话框内。
 */
import { reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import { passwordProblem } from '@/utils/accountRules'

const visible = defineModel<boolean>({ required: true })
const props = defineProps<{ username: string; save: (password: string) => Promise<void> }>()

const form = reactive({ password: '', confirm: '' })
const error = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)

watch(visible, (open) => {
  if (open) {
    form.password = ''
    form.confirm = ''
    error.value = ''
  }
})

/** 校验并保存：密码合规且两次一致才调用 save */
async function submit() {
  if (saving.value) return
  error.value = passwordProblem(form.password) ?? (form.password !== form.confirm ? '两次输入的密码不一致' : '')
  if (error.value) return
  saving.value = true
  try {
    await props.save(form.password)
    visible.value = false
    ElMessage.success(`已重置 ${props.username} 的密码，对方下次登录需修改密码`)
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-dialog v-model="visible" :title="`重置密码 · ${username}`" width="min(420px, 92vw)" :close-on-click-modal="false" append-to-body>
    <el-form label-width="96px" @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-alert v-if="error" class="error" type="error" :title="error" show-icon :closable="false" />
      <el-form-item label="新密码" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.password" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="确认密码" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.confirm" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" />
      </el-form-item>
    </el-form>
    <template #footer>
      <el-button @click="visible = false">取消</el-button>
      <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="submit">重置</el-button>
    </template>
  </el-dialog>
</template>

<style scoped>
.error {
  margin-bottom: var(--cd-space-3);
}
</style>
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the tenant dialog and drawer**

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/TenantCreateDialog.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 新建租户对话框：分「租户信息」（编码、名称）与「首个管理员」（用户名、显示名称、初始密码）两块，
 * 提交前按账号规则校验，错误显示在对话框内。成功后发出 created（新租户）并关闭；首个管理员首次登录须修改密码。
 */
import { reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { TenantView } from '@/api/types'
import { passwordProblem, tenantCodeProblem, usernameProblem } from '@/utils/accountRules'

const visible = defineModel<boolean>({ required: true })
const emit = defineEmits<{ created: [tenant: TenantView] }>()

const form = reactive({ code: '', name: '', username: '', displayName: '', password: '' })
const error = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)

watch(visible, (open) => {
  if (open) {
    Object.assign(form, { code: '', name: '', username: '', displayName: '', password: '' })
    error.value = ''
  }
})

/** 租户名称的错误：为空或超过 64 个字符 */
function nameProblem(name: string): string | null {
  if (!name) return '请输入租户名称'
  return name.length > 64 ? '租户名称最多 64 个字符' : null
}

/** 校验表单，返回第一条错误；全部合法时为 null */
function validate(): string | null {
  return (
    tenantCodeProblem(form.code.trim()) ??
    nameProblem(form.name.trim()) ??
    usernameProblem(form.username.trim()) ??
    passwordProblem(form.password)
  )
}

/** 校验并创建租户与首个管理员；保存中忽略重复点击 */
async function submit() {
  if (saving.value) return
  error.value = validate() ?? ''
  if (error.value) return
  saving.value = true
  try {
    const tenant = await platformApi.createTenant({
      code: form.code.trim(),
      name: form.name.trim(),
      admin: { username: form.username.trim(), displayName: form.displayName.trim() || null, password: form.password },
    })
    visible.value = false
    emit('created', tenant)
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-dialog v-model="visible" title="新建租户" width="min(520px, 92vw)" :close-on-click-modal="false">
    <el-form label-width="96px" @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-alert v-if="error" class="error" type="error" :title="error" show-icon :closable="false" />
      <el-divider content-position="left">租户信息</el-divider>
      <el-form-item label="编码" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.code" maxlength="32" placeholder="如 east-1，小写字母开头，创建后不可修改" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="名称" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.name" maxlength="64" placeholder="如 验收租户（华东）" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-divider content-position="left">首个管理员</el-divider>
      <el-form-item label="用户名" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.username" maxlength="32" autocomplete="off" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="显示名称">
        <el-input v-model="form.displayName" maxlength="64" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="初始密码" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.password" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字" />
        <div class="cd-muted hint">管理员首次登录时需要修改密码。</div>
      </el-form-item>
    </el-form>
    <template #footer>
      <el-button @click="visible = false">取消</el-button>
      <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="submit">创建</el-button>
    </template>
  </el-dialog>
</template>

<style scoped>
.error {
  margin-bottom: var(--cd-space-3);
}
.hint {
  margin-top: var(--cd-space-1);
  font-size: 12px;
}
</style>
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/TenantUsersDrawer.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 租户用户抽屉（平台管理员）：列出某租户的全部用户（不分页，按角色、用户名排序），角色、状态与「待改密码」用标签显示；
 * 租户管理员行有「重置密码」，经 ResetPasswordDialog 设置新的初始密码（对方下次登录须修改）。打开或切换租户时重新加载。
 */
import { ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { TenantUserView, TenantView } from '@/api/types'
import { formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format'
import { roleLabel, statusLabel } from '@/utils/permissions'
import ResetPasswordDialog from './ResetPasswordDialog.vue'

const visible = defineModel<boolean>({ required: true })
const props = defineProps<{ tenant: TenantView | null }>()

const users = ref<TenantUserView[]>([])
const loading = ref(false)
const failed = ref(false)
const resetVisible = ref(false)
const resetting = ref<TenantUserView | null>(null)

/** 加载当前租户的用户；失败时清空并提示 */
async function load() {
  if (!props.tenant) return
  loading.value = true
  failed.value = false
  try {
    users.value = await platformApi.tenantUsers(props.tenant.code)
  } catch (e) {
    users.value = []
    failed.value = true
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

watch(
  () => [visible.value, props.tenant?.code] as const,
  ([open]) => {
    if (open) void load()
  },
  { immediate: true },
)

/** 打开重置密码对话框 */
function openReset(user: TenantUserView) {
  resetting.value = user
  resetVisible.value = true
}

/** 重置所选租户管理员的密码（由 ResetPasswordDialog 调用；失败时异常交给对话框显示），成功后刷新列表 */
async function savePassword(password: string) {
  if (!props.tenant || !resetting.value) return
  await platformApi.resetAdminPassword(props.tenant.code, resetting.value.username, password)
  await load()
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-drawer v-model="visible" :title="tenant ? `用户 · ${tenant.name}` : '用户'" size="min(680px, 100vw)">
    <el-table v-loading="loading" :data="users">
      <el-table-column label="用户名" min-width="160">
        <template #default="{ row }">
          {{ row.username }}
          <div v-if="row.displayName" class="cd-muted">{{ row.displayName }}</div>
        </template>
      </el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="角色" width="110">
        <template #default="{ row }">
          <el-tag :type="row.role === 'TENANT_ADMIN' ? 'primary' : 'info'" size="small">{{ roleLabel(row.role) }}</el-tag>
        </template>
      </el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="状态" width="150">
        <template #default="{ row }">
          <el-tag :type="row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'success' : 'info'" size="small">{{ statusLabel(row.status) }}</el-tag>
          <el-tag v-if="row.mustChangePassword" type="warning" size="small" class="flag">待改密码</el-tag>
        </template>
      </el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="创建时间" width="170">
        <template #default="{ row }">{{ formatDateTime(row.createdAt) }}</template>
      </el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="操作" width="100">
        <template #default="{ row }">
          <el-button v-if="row.role === 'TENANT_ADMIN'" link type="primary" @click="openReset(row)">重置密码</el-button>
        </template>
      </el-table-column>
      <template #empty>
        <el-empty :description="failed ? '加载失败，点击重试' : '该租户还没有用户'">
          <el-button v-if="failed" type="primary" @click="load">重试</el-button>
        </el-empty>
      </template>
    </el-table>
    <ResetPasswordDialog v-model="resetVisible" :username="resetting?.username ?? ''" :save="savePassword" />
  </el-drawer>
</template>

<style scoped>
.flag {
  margin-left: var(--cd-space-1);
}
</style>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the platform home and the home entry**

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/PlatformHomeView.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 平台首页（未代入的平台管理员）：4 个指标卡（租户、用户、设备、在线率）与「设备最多的租户」列表，
 * 点击租户卡进入租户管理，点击某个租户进入租户管理并按该租户筛选。数据来自 GET /api/v1/platform/overview，
 * 每 30 秒自动刷新（页面隐藏时暂停）；服务端 Redis 不可用时在线相关数值为 null，显示「—」。
 * 刷新失败时保留上次数据，同一段连续失败只提示一次。
 */
import { computed, onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { Connection, Cpu, OfficeBuilding, User } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { PlatformOverview } from '@/api/types'
import { createVisiblePoller } from '@/composables/usePolling'
import { createRequestSeq } from '@/utils/requestSeq'
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/format'
import { onlineRate } from '@/utils/overview'
import PageHeader from '@/components/PageHeader.vue'

const REFRESH_MS = 30_000

const router = useRouter()
const data = ref<PlatformOverview | null>(null)
const seq = createRequestSeq()
let errorShown = false

/** 在线率；在线数未知时为 null */
const rate = computed(() => (data.value ? onlineRate(data.value.onlineCount, data.value.deviceCount) : null))

/** 加载平台概况：只有最后发出的请求能写入结果；失败时保留上次数据，连续失败只提示一次 */
async function load() {
  const id = seq.next()
  try {
    const res = await platformApi.overview()
    if (seq.isLatest(id)) {
      data.value = res
      errorShown = false
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (seq.isLatest(id) && !errorShown) {
      errorShown = true
      ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
    }
  }
}

const poller = createVisiblePoller(load, REFRESH_MS)

/** 进入租户管理；给出 code 时按该租户筛选 */
function openTenants(code?: string) {
  void router.push(code ? { name: 'tenants', query: { keyword: code } } : { name: 'tenants' })
}

onMounted(() => poller.start())
onBeforeUnmount(() => {
  poller.dispose()
  seq.next()
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="home">
    <PageHeader title="平台首页" subtitle="全平台运行概况，每 30 秒自动刷新。" />

    <el-skeleton v-if="!data" :rows="6" animated />
    <template v-else>
      <div class="tiles">
        <div class="tile cd-panel clickable" data-test="tile-tenants" role="link" tabindex="0" @click="openTenants()" @keydown.enter="openTenants()">
          <div class="tile-head"><el-icon><OfficeBuilding /></el-icon>租户</div>
          <div class="tile-value">{{ data.tenantCount }}</div>
          <div class="tile-sub">启用 {{ data.activeTenantCount }} · 停用 {{ data.disabledTenantCount }}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="tile cd-panel" data-test="tile-users">
          <div class="tile-head"><el-icon><User /></el-icon>用户</div>
          <div class="tile-value">{{ data.userCount }}</div>
          <div class="tile-sub">各租户的管理员与只读用户</div>
        </div>
        <div class="tile cd-panel" data-test="tile-devices">
          <div class="tile-head"><el-icon><Cpu /></el-icon>设备</div>
          <div class="tile-value">{{ data.deviceCount }}</div>
          <div class="tile-sub">共 {{ data.productCount }} 个产品</div>
        </div>
        <div class="tile cd-panel" data-test="tile-online">
          <div class="tile-head"><el-icon><Connection /></el-icon>在线率</div>
          <div class="tile-value">{{ rate === null ? '—' : `${rate}%` }}</div>
          <el-progress v-if="rate !== null" :percentage="rate" :show-text="false" :stroke-width="6" status="success" />
          <div class="tile-sub">
            {{ rate === null ? '在线状态暂不可用' : `在线 ${formatCount(data.onlineCount)} / 设备 ${data.deviceCount}` }}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="list cd-panel top-tenants">
        <div class="list-head">设备最多的租户</div>
        <el-empty v-if="data.topTenants.length === 0" description="还没有租户" :image-size="64">
          <el-button type="primary" @click="openTenants()">去新建租户</el-button>
        </el-empty>
        <ul v-else>
          <li
            v-for="t in data.topTenants"
            :key="t.code"
            class="row"
            role="button"
            tabindex="0"
            @click="openTenants(t.code)"
            @keydown.enter="openTenants(t.code)"
          >
            <div class="row-main">
              <span class="row-title">{{ t.name }}</span>
              <span class="cd-muted">{{ t.code }} · 在线 / 设备</span>
            </div>
            <span class="row-count">{{ formatCount(t.onlineCount) }} / {{ t.deviceCount }}</span>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </template>
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>

  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(220px, 1fr));
  gap: var(--cd-space-4);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the tenant management page**

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/TenantsView.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 租户管理（平台管理员）：筛选栏（名称/编码关键字、状态）+ 服务端分页（useTable，默认每页 10 条）；状态用标签。
 * 操作列：进入（确认后以租户管理员身份进入，停用的租户不能进入）、编辑（改名）、用户（抽屉查看用户、重置租户管理员密码）、
 * 停用/启用、删除（默认租户不显示停用/启用与删除）。「新建租户」对话框同时创建首个管理员。
 * 进入页面时从 query keyword 读取初始关键字（平台首页点击租户跳来）。首次加载显示骨架屏，没有租户时引导新建，加载失败时可重试。
 */
import { onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { Plus } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { platformApi } from '@/api/platform'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { AccountStatus, TenantView } from '@/api/types'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { useTable } from '@/composables/useTable'
import { formatCount, formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format'
import { PAGE_SIZES } from '@/utils/pagination'
import { statusLabel } from '@/utils/permissions'
import PageHeader from '@/components/PageHeader.vue'
import FilterBar from '@/components/FilterBar.vue'
import TenantCreateDialog from '@/components/TenantCreateDialog.vue'
import TenantUsersDrawer from '@/components/TenantUsersDrawer.vue'

/** 默认租户编码：不能停用、启用或删除 */
const DEFAULT_TENANT = 'default'

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const auth = useAuthStore()

const filters = reactive({
  keyword: typeof route.query.keyword === 'string' ? route.query.keyword : '',
  status: '' as '' | AccountStatus,
})
/** 已应用的筛选（点查询/回车时才从输入框复制），翻页与刷新都以它为准 */
const applied = ref({ ...filters })

const { items, total, page, size, loading, loaded, failed, load, search: searchTable, onSizeChange, onPageChange } =
  useTable<TenantView>((p, s) =>
    platformApi.listTenants({
      keyword: applied.value.keyword.trim() || undefined,
      status: applied.value.status || undefined,
      page: p,
      size: s,
    }),
  )

const createVisible = ref(false)
const editVisible = ref(false)
const editing = ref<TenantView | null>(null)
const editName = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)
const usersVisible = ref(false)
const usersTenant = ref<TenantView | null>(null)
const busy = ref(new Set<string>())

/** 应用筛选并回到第 1 页 */
function search() {
  applied.value = { ...filters }
  void searchTable()
}

/** 清空筛选并回到第 1 页 */
function reset() {
  filters.keyword = ''
  filters.status = ''
  search()
}

/** 新建成功：提示并回到第 1 页（新租户在最前） */
function onCreated(tenant: TenantView) {
  ElMessage.success(`已创建租户「${tenant.name}」，首个管理员登录后需修改密码`)
  void searchTable()
}

/** 打开改名对话框 */
function openEdit(row: TenantView) {
  editing.value = row
  editName.value = row.name
  editVisible.value = true
}

/** 保存租户名称：去首尾空白后 1～64 个字符；成功后关闭并刷新当前页 */
async function saveEdit() {
  if (saving.value || !editing.value) return
  const name = editName.value.trim()
  if (!name || name.length > 64) {
    ElMessage.warning('租户名称为 1～64 个字符')
    return
  }
  saving.value = true
  try {
    await platformApi.updateTenant(editing.value.code, { name })
    editVisible.value = false
    ElMessage.success('已保存')
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}

/** 打开用户抽屉 */
function openUsers(row: TenantView) {
  usersTenant.value = row
  usersVisible.value = true
}

/** 进入租户：确认后换成代入令牌并去首页（此时首页显示该租户的数据）；取消则什么也不做 */
async function enter(row: TenantView) {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`将以租户管理员权限操作租户「${row.name}」的数据，所有修改都会被记录`, '进入租户', {
      type: 'warning',
      confirmButtonText: '进入',
      cancelButtonText: '取消',
    })
  } catch {
    return
  }
  try {
    await auth.enterTenant(row.code)
    await router.push({ name: 'home' })
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  }
}

/** 停用（先确认）或启用租户，成功后刷新当前页；操作中的行按钮显示加载 */
async function toggleStatus(row: TenantView) {
  const disabling = row.status === 'ACTIVE'
  if (disabling) {
    try {
      await ElMessageBox.confirm(`停用后，租户「${row.name}」的用户无法登录、设备无法接入。确定停用？`, '停用租户', {
        type: 'warning',
      })
    } catch {
      return
    }
  }
  busy.value.add(row.code)
  try {
    await (disabling ? platformApi.disableTenant(row.code) : platformApi.enableTenant(row.code))
    ElMessage.success(disabling ? '已停用' : '已启用')
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  } finally {
    busy.value.delete(row.code)
  }
}

/** 删除租户（先确认）：其用户一并删除；租户下仍有产品或设备时服务端返回 409 并提示 */
async function remove(row: TenantView) {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(
      `确定删除租户「${row.name}」？其用户将一并删除；租户下仍有产品或设备时不能删除。`,
      '删除租户',
      { type: 'warning' },
    )
  } catch {
    return
  }
  try {
    await platformApi.removeTenant(row.code)
    ElMessage.success('已删除')
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  void load()
})
</script>

<template>
  <div>
    <PageHeader title="租户" subtitle="平台上的租户：各租户的产品、设备与用户相互隔离。">
      <template #actions>
        <el-button type="primary" :icon="Plus" @click="createVisible = true">新建租户</el-button>
      </template>
    </PageHeader>

    <div class="cd-panel">
      <FilterBar @search="search" @reset="reset">
        <el-form-item label="关键字">
          <el-input v-model="filters.keyword" placeholder="名称或编码" clearable />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="状态">
          <el-select v-model="filters.status" placeholder="全部" clearable class="status-select">
            <el-option label="启用" value="ACTIVE" />
            <el-option label="停用" value="DISABLED" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
      </FilterBar>

      <el-skeleton v-if="!loaded" :rows="5" animated />
      <template v-else>
        <el-table v-loading="loading" :data="items">
          <el-table-column label="名称" min-width="180">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              {{ row.name }}
              <div class="cd-muted">{{ row.code }}</div>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="状态" width="90">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <el-tag :type="row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'success' : 'info'" size="small">
                {{ statusLabel(row.status, 'tenant') }}
              </el-tag>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="用户（管理员）" width="130">
            <template #default="{ row }">{{ row.userCount }}（{{ row.adminCount }}）</template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column prop="productCount" label="产品" width="80" />
          <el-table-column label="设备（在线/总数）" width="150">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <span class="device-count">{{ formatCount(row.onlineCount) }} / {{ row.deviceCount }}</span>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="创建时间" width="180">
            <template #default="{ row }">{{ formatDateTime(row.createdAt) }}</template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="操作" width="300">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <el-button link type="primary" :disabled="row.status !== 'ACTIVE'" @click="enter(row)">进入</el-button>
              <el-button link type="primary" @click="openEdit(row)">编辑</el-button>
              <el-button link type="primary" @click="openUsers(row)">用户</el-button>
              <template v-if="row.code !== DEFAULT_TENANT">
                <el-button
                  link
                  :type="row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'warning' : 'success'"
                  :loading="busy.has(row.code)"
                  @click="toggleStatus(row)"
                >
                  {{ row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? '停用' : '启用' }}
                </el-button>
                <el-button link type="danger" @click="remove(row)">删除</el-button>
              </template>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <template #empty>
            <el-empty v-if="failed" description="加载失败，点击重试">
              <el-button type="primary" @click="load">重试</el-button>
            </el-empty>
            <el-empty v-else-if="applied.keyword || applied.status" description="没有符合条件的租户">
              <el-button @click="reset">重置筛选</el-button>
            </el-empty>
            <el-empty v-else description="还没有租户，先新建一个租户">
              <el-button type="primary" @click="createVisible = true">新建租户</el-button>
            </el-empty>
          </template>
        </el-table>

        <el-pagination
          v-model:current-page="page"
          v-model:page-size="size"
          class="cd-pager"
          layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next"
          :page-sizes="PAGE_SIZES"
          :total="total"
          @current-change="onPageChange"
          @size-change="onSizeChange"
        />
      </template>
    </div>

    <TenantCreateDialog v-model="createVisible" @created="onCreated" />

    <el-dialog v-model="editVisible" title="编辑租户" width="min(420px, 92vw)" :close-on-click-modal="false">
      <el-form label-width="80px" @submit.prevent="saveEdit">
        <el-form-item label="编码">
          <el-input :model-value="editing?.code" disabled />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="名称" required>
          <el-input v-model="editName" maxlength="64" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="editVisible = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="saveEdit">保存</el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>

    <TenantUsersDrawer v-model="usersVisible" :tenant="usersTenant" />
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.status-select {
  width: 120px;
}
</style>
```

## Task 8: Frontend user management, personal settings and read-only gating

Adds the user management page with its form dialog (spec §5.2), the personal settings dialog from the user menu, and hides every write action on the existing product, thing-model and device pages from read-only users (spec §5.3).

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/views/UsersView.vue`, `UsersView.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/components/UserFormDialog.vue`, `UserFormDialog.spec.ts`
- Create: `conduit-ui/src/components/PersonalSettingsDialog.vue`, `PersonalSettingsDialog.spec.ts`
- Modify: `conduit-ui/src/layouts/AppLayout.vue`, `AppLayout.spec.ts` (personal settings)
- Modify: `conduit-ui/src/router/index.ts` (`users` route)
- Modify: `conduit-ui/src/views/ProductsView.vue`, `ProductDetailView.vue`, `DevicesView.vue`, `DeviceDetailView.vue`, `conduit-ui/src/components/DeviceInfoCard.vue` (`canWrite`)
- Modify: `conduit-ui/src/views/ProductsView.spec.ts`, `ProductDetailView.spec.ts`, `DevicesView.spec.ts`, `DeviceDetailView.spec.ts` (Pinia + viewer tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 6–7): `userApi.*`, `TenantUserView`, `TenantRole`, `AccountStatus`, `Role`, `UserView`; `useAuthStore().updateProfile/changePassword`; `usePermission().canWrite/isImpersonating`; `roleLabel`, `statusLabel`; `usernameProblem`, `passwordProblem`; `ResetPasswordDialog` (`modelValue`, `username`, `save`); `ROUTE_META.users`; `createTestPinia`, `createTestRouter`, `settle`; AppLayout `onCommand`.
- Produces: route `users` → `/users` (`UsersView`); `UserFormDialog` props `modelValue`, `user: TenantUserView | null`, `editingSelf?: boolean`, event `saved`; `PersonalSettingsDialog` prop `modelValue`; AppLayout `profileVisible` and the `profile` command; `DeviceInfoCard` prop `canEdit?: boolean` (default `true`).

**Decisions recorded here:**
- "Own row" means `row.username === auth.user.username` and not impersonating; it hides 禁用/启用 and 删除, and the edit dialog locks the role (`editingSelf`). The server enforces the same rules (409), including the last-admin rule, whose message is shown as is.
- Read-only gating hides, never disables, write buttons: products (新建、编辑、删除), product detail (基本信息 form becomes `disabled` and loses 保存; 物模型 loses 导入、新增属性 and the 操作 column; 导出 stays), devices (新建设备、启用/禁用、重置密钥、删除), device detail (编辑). Empty-state guide buttons that lead to writes are hidden too.
- Personal settings: the 「基本信息」 tab saves the display name (blank clears it); the 「修改密码」 tab reuses the forced-password rules; while impersonating the password tab shows a notice instead of the form (the server answers 409 anyway).
- Disabling a user shows that existing sessions end within 15 minutes (the access-token lifetime, spec §3.2).
