Create `conduit-ui/src/components/UserFormDialog.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** UserFormDialog 组件测试：新建前按规则校验并提交、编辑只改显示名称与角色、编辑自己时角色锁定、接口错误显示在对话框内 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus, { ElRadioGroup } from 'element-plus'
import UserFormDialog from './UserFormDialog.vue'
import { userApi } from '@/api/users'
import type { TenantUserView } from '@/api/types'

vi.mock('@/api/users', () => ({ userApi: { create: vi.fn(), update: vi.fn() } }))

type Vm = {
  form: { username: string; displayName: string; role: string; password: string }
  error: string
  submit(): Promise<void>
}

const viewer: TenantUserView = {
  username: 'east-viewer',
  displayName: '华东运维 张工',
  role: 'TENANT_VIEWER',
  status: 'ACTIVE',
  mustChangePassword: false,
  createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z',
}

function mountDialog(user: TenantUserView | null, editingSelf = false) {
  const wrapper = mount(UserFormDialog, {
    props: { modelValue: true, user, editingSelf },
    global: { plugins: [ElementPlus] },
    attachTo: document.body,
  })
  return { wrapper, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

describe('UserFormDialog', () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks())

  it('validates and creates a user', async () => {
    vi.mocked(userApi.create).mockResolvedValue(viewer)
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(null)

    vm.form.username = '1abc'
    vm.form.password = 'Viewer-pass-1'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toContain('用户名须为 3～32 位')

    vm.form.username = 'east-viewer'
    vm.form.password = 'weak'
    await vm.submit()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('密码须为 8～64 位，且同时包含字母和数字')
    expect(userApi.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled()

    vm.form.password = 'Viewer-pass-1'
    vm.form.displayName = ' 华东运维 张工 '
    vm.form.role = 'TENANT_VIEWER'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(userApi.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      username: 'east-viewer',
      displayName: '华东运维 张工',
      role: 'TENANT_VIEWER',
      password: 'Viewer-pass-1',
    })
    expect(wrapper.emitted('saved')).toEqual([[viewer]])
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toEqual([[false]])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('edits the display name and the role', async () => {
    vi.mocked(userApi.update).mockResolvedValue({ ...viewer, displayName: null, role: 'TENANT_ADMIN' })
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(viewer)

    expect(vm.form.displayName).toBe('华东运维 张工')
    vm.form.displayName = '  '
    vm.form.role = 'TENANT_ADMIN'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(userApi.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east-viewer', { displayName: null, role: 'TENANT_ADMIN' })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('locks the role when editing oneself', () => {
    const { wrapper } = mountDialog({ ...viewer, role: 'TENANT_ADMIN' }, true)

    expect(wrapper.findComponent(ElRadioGroup).props('disabled')).toBe(true)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows the server error', async () => {
    vi.mocked(userApi.create).mockRejectedValue(new Error('用户名已存在: east-viewer'))
    const { wrapper, vm } = mountDialog(null)

    vm.form.username = 'east-viewer'
    vm.form.password = 'Viewer-pass-1'
    await vm.submit()

    expect(vm.error).toBe('用户名已存在: east-viewer')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('saved')).toBeUndefined()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/PersonalSettingsDialog.spec.ts`:

```ts
/** PersonalSettingsDialog 组件测试：修改与清除显示名称、本地校验后修改密码、代入期间提示不能修改密码 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import PersonalSettingsDialog from './PersonalSettingsDialog.vue'
import { meApi } from '@/api/me'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import type { UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { createTestPinia, settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({ authApi: { login: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), logout: vi.fn(), exitTenant: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/me', () => ({ meApi: { update: vi.fn(), changePassword: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { enter: vi.fn() } }))

type Vm = {
  displayName: string
  pw: { oldPassword: string; newPassword: string; confirm: string }
  error: string
  saveProfile(): Promise<void>
  savePassword(): Promise<void>
}

const me: UserView = {
  username: 'east-admin',
  displayName: '华东运维',
  role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
  tenantCode: 'east',
  tenantName: '验收租户（华东）',
  mustChangePassword: false,
  impersonatedBy: null,
}

async function mountDialog(user: Partial<UserView> = {}) {
  const pinia = createTestPinia({ ...me, ...user })
  const wrapper = mount(PersonalSettingsDialog, {
    props: { modelValue: true },
    global: { plugins: [pinia, ElementPlus] },
    attachTo: document.body,
  })
  await settle()
  return { wrapper, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

describe('PersonalSettingsDialog', () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks())

  it('starts with the current display name and saves a new one', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.update).mockResolvedValue({ ...me, displayName: '华东运维 张工' })
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog()

    expect(vm.displayName).toBe('华东运维')
    vm.displayName = '  华东运维 张工 '
    await vm.saveProfile()

    expect(meApi.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ displayName: '华东运维 张工' })
    expect(useAuthStore().user?.displayName).toBe('华东运维 张工')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toEqual([[false]])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('clears the display name with a blank value', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.update).mockResolvedValue({ ...me, displayName: null })
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog()

    vm.displayName = '   '
    await vm.saveProfile()

    expect(meApi.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ displayName: null })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('changes the password after local checks', async () => {
    vi.mocked(meApi.changePassword).mockResolvedValue({ accessToken: 'fresh', expiresIn: 900, user: me })
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountDialog()

    vm.pw.oldPassword = 'Active-pass-2026'
    vm.pw.newPassword = 'Newer-pass-2026'
    vm.pw.confirm = 'Other-pass-2026'
    await vm.savePassword()
    expect(vm.error).toBe('两次输入的新密码不一致')
    expect(meApi.changePassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled()

    vm.pw.confirm = 'Newer-pass-2026'
    await vm.savePassword()

    expect(meApi.changePassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ oldPassword: 'Active-pass-2026', newPassword: 'Newer-pass-2026' })
    expect(useAuthStore().token).toBe('fresh')
    expect(wrapper.emitted('update:modelValue')).toEqual([[false]])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('tells an impersonating platform admin to exit before changing the password', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountDialog({ username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' })

    expect(document.body.textContent).toContain('代入租户期间不能修改密码')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/UsersView.spec.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * UsersView 组件测试：首屏第 1 页 10 条、角色/状态/待改密码标签、自己那一行没有禁用与删除（代入时没有「自己」）、
 * 筛选（关键字、角色、状态）、启用与删除、经对话框重置密码后刷新、打开新建对话框。
 */
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import ElementPlus from 'element-plus'
import UsersView from './UsersView.vue'
import { userApi } from '@/api/users'
import type { TenantUserView, UserView } from '@/api/types'
import { createTestPinia, createTestRouter, settle } from '@/test/viewTestUtils'

vi.mock('@/api/users', () => ({
  userApi: { list: vi.fn(), create: vi.fn(), update: vi.fn(), disable: vi.fn(), enable: vi.fn(), resetPassword: vi.fn(), remove: vi.fn() },
}))
vi.mock('@/api/auth', () => ({ authApi: { login: vi.fn(), refresh: vi.fn(), logout: vi.fn(), exitTenant: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/me', () => ({ meApi: { update: vi.fn(), changePassword: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('@/api/platform', () => ({ platformApi: { enter: vi.fn() } }))
// 确认框直接确认
vi.mock('element-plus', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('element-plus')>()
  return { ...actual, ElMessageBox: { ...actual.ElMessageBox, confirm: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('confirm') } }
})

function user(patch: Partial<TenantUserView> = {}): TenantUserView {
  return {
    username: 'east-admin',
    displayName: '华东管理员',
    role: 'TENANT_ADMIN',
    status: 'ACTIVE',
    mustChangePassword: false,
    createdAt: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z',
    ...patch,
  }
}

type Vm = {
  filters: { keyword: string; role: string; status: string }
  formVisible: boolean
  editing: TenantUserView | null
  search(): void
  openReset(row: TenantUserView): void
  savePassword(password: string): Promise<void>
}

async function mountView(session: Partial<UserView> = {}) {
  const pinia = createTestPinia({ username: 'east-admin', tenantCode: 'east', tenantName: '验收租户（华东）', ...session })
  const router = createTestRouter()
  await router.push('/users')
  await router.isReady()
  const wrapper = mount(UsersView, { global: { plugins: [pinia, router, ElementPlus] }, attachTo: document.body })
  await settle()
  return { wrapper, vm: wrapper.vm as unknown as Vm }
}

function rowButtons(wrapper: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof mountView>>['wrapper'], index: number) {
  return wrapper.findAll('.el-table__body tr')[index].findAll('button').map((b) => b.text())
}

describe('UsersView', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks()
    vi.mocked(userApi.list).mockResolvedValue({
      items: [
        user(),
        user({ username: 'east-viewer', displayName: null, role: 'TENANT_VIEWER', status: 'DISABLED', mustChangePassword: true }),
      ],
      total: 2,
      page: 1,
      size: 10,
    })
  })

  it('requests the first page with 10 rows', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    expect(userApi.list).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ keyword: undefined, role: undefined, status: undefined, page: 1, size: 10 })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('shows role, status and password tags', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    const text = wrapper.find('.el-table').text()
    expect(text).toContain('租户管理员')
    expect(text).toContain('只读用户')
    expect(text).toContain('禁用')
    expect(text).toContain('待改密码')
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('hides disable and delete on the own row', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()

    expect(rowButtons(wrapper, 0)).toEqual(['编辑', '重置密码'])
    expect(rowButtons(wrapper, 1)).toEqual(['编辑', '重置密码', '启用', '删除'])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('has no own row while impersonating', async () => {
    const { wrapper } = await mountView({ username: 'sysadmin', impersonatedBy: 'sysadmin' })

    expect(rowButtons(wrapper, 0)).toEqual(['编辑', '重置密码', '禁用', '删除'])
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('filters by keyword, role and status', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    vm.filters.keyword = ' 张工 '
    vm.filters.role = 'TENANT_VIEWER'
    vm.filters.status = 'DISABLED'
    vm.search()
    await settle()

    expect(userApi.list).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ keyword: '张工', role: 'TENANT_VIEWER', status: 'DISABLED', page: 1, size: 10 })
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('enables and deletes users', async () => {
    vi.mocked(userApi.enable).mockResolvedValue(user({ username: 'east-viewer', role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' }))
    vi.mocked(userApi.remove).mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper } = await mountView()
    const row = () => wrapper.findAll('.el-table__body tr')[1]

    await row().findAll('button').find((b) => b.text() === '启用')!.trigger('click')
    await settle()
    expect(userApi.enable).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east-viewer')

    await row().findAll('button').find((b) => b.text() === '删除')!.trigger('click')
    await settle()
    expect(userApi.remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east-viewer')
    expect(userApi.list).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('resets a password and reloads', async () => {
    vi.mocked(userApi.resetPassword).mockResolvedValue(undefined)
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    vm.openReset(user({ username: 'east-viewer' }))
    await vm.savePassword('Reset-pass-9')

    expect(userApi.resetPassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith('east-viewer', 'Reset-pass-9')
    expect(userApi.list).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
    wrapper.unmount()
  })

  it('opens the create dialog from the page header', async () => {
    const { wrapper, vm } = await mountView()

    await wrapper.findAll('button').find((b) => b.text() === '新建用户')!.trigger('click')

    expect(vm.formVisible).toBe(true)
    expect(vm.editing).toBeNull()
    wrapper.unmount()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the user form and personal settings dialogs**

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/UserFormDialog.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 新建/编辑用户对话框：新建时填写用户名、显示名称、角色与初始密码（对方首次登录须修改）；编辑时只改显示名称与角色，
 * 编辑自己时角色锁定（editingSelf）。提交前按账号规则校验，错误显示在对话框内；成功后发出 saved（保存后的用户）并关闭。
 */
import { computed, reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { userApi } from '@/api/users'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { TenantRole, TenantUserView } from '@/api/types'
import { passwordProblem, usernameProblem } from '@/utils/accountRules'

const visible = defineModel<boolean>({ required: true })
const props = withDefaults(defineProps<{ user: TenantUserView | null; editingSelf?: boolean }>(), { editingSelf: false })
const emit = defineEmits<{ saved: [user: TenantUserView] }>()

const form = reactive({ username: '', displayName: '', role: 'TENANT_VIEWER' as TenantRole, password: '' })
const error = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)
/** 是否在编辑已有用户（否则为新建） */
const editing = computed(() => props.user !== null)

// 每次打开都按当前要编辑的用户重置表单
watch(
  visible,
  (open) => {
    if (!open) return
    form.username = props.user?.username ?? ''
    form.displayName = props.user?.displayName ?? ''
    form.role = props.user?.role ?? 'TENANT_VIEWER'
    form.password = ''
    error.value = ''
  },
  { immediate: true },
)

/** 校验表单，返回第一条错误；编辑时只校验显示名称长度 */
function validate(): string | null {
  if (form.displayName.trim().length > 64) return '显示名称最多 64 个字符'
  if (editing.value) return null
  return usernameProblem(form.username.trim()) ?? passwordProblem(form.password)
}

/** 校验并保存（新建或编辑）；保存中忽略重复点击 */
async function submit() {
  if (saving.value) return
  error.value = validate() ?? ''
  if (error.value) return
  saving.value = true
  const displayName = form.displayName.trim() || null
  try {
    const saved = props.user
      ? await userApi.update(props.user.username, { displayName, role: form.role })
      : await userApi.create({ username: form.username.trim(), displayName, role: form.role, password: form.password })
    visible.value = false
    emit('saved', saved)
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-dialog v-model="visible" :title="editing ? '编辑用户' : '新建用户'" width="min(480px, 92vw)" :close-on-click-modal="false">
    <el-form label-width="96px" @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-alert v-if="error" class="error" type="error" :title="error" show-icon :closable="false" />
      <el-form-item label="用户名" :required="!editing">
        <el-input v-model="form.username" :disabled="editing" maxlength="32" autocomplete="off" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="显示名称">
        <el-input v-model="form.displayName" maxlength="64" placeholder="不填则显示用户名" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="角色" required>
        <el-radio-group v-model="form.role" :disabled="editingSelf">
          <el-radio value="TENANT_ADMIN">租户管理员</el-radio>
          <el-radio value="TENANT_VIEWER">只读用户</el-radio>
        </el-radio-group>
        <div v-if="editingSelf" class="cd-muted hint">不能修改自己的角色。</div>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item v-if="!editing" label="初始密码" required>
        <el-input v-model="form.password" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字" />
        <div class="cd-muted hint">用户首次登录时需要修改密码。</div>
      </el-form-item>
    </el-form>
    <template #footer>
      <el-button @click="visible = false">取消</el-button>
      <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="submit">保存</el-button>
    </template>
  </el-dialog>
</template>

<style scoped>
.error {
  margin-bottom: var(--cd-space-3);
}
.hint {
  width: 100%;
  margin-top: var(--cd-space-1);
  font-size: 12px;
}
</style>
```

Create `conduit-ui/src/components/PersonalSettingsDialog.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 个人设置对话框（用户菜单「个人设置」）：「基本信息」显示用户名、角色与租户，可修改显示名称（留空即显示用户名）；
 * 「修改密码」输入原密码与两次新密码（规则同强制改密码）；平台管理员代入租户期间不能修改密码，提示先退出租户。
 * 保存成功后更新会话、提示并关闭；错误显示在对话框内。
 */
import { reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { usePermission } from '@/composables/usePermission'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import { passwordProblem } from '@/utils/accountRules'
import { roleLabel } from '@/utils/permissions'

const visible = defineModel<boolean>({ required: true })
const auth = useAuthStore()
const { isImpersonating } = usePermission()

const tab = ref<'profile' | 'password'>('profile')
const displayName = ref('')
const pw = reactive({ oldPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirm: '' })
const error = ref('')
const saving = ref(false)

// 每次打开都从当前会话重新填充，并回到「基本信息」
watch(
  visible,
  (open) => {
    if (!open) return
    tab.value = 'profile'
    displayName.value = auth.user?.displayName ?? ''
    pw.oldPassword = ''
    pw.newPassword = ''
    pw.confirm = ''
    error.value = ''
  },
  { immediate: true },
)

watch(tab, () => {
  error.value = ''
})

/** 保存显示名称（去首尾空白，空白即清除），成功后关闭 */
async function saveProfile() {
  if (saving.value) return
  const name = displayName.value.trim()
  if (name.length > 64) {
    error.value = '显示名称最多 64 个字符'
    return
  }
  saving.value = true
  error.value = ''
  try {
    await auth.updateProfile(name || null)
    ElMessage.success('已保存')
    visible.value = false
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}

/** 修改密码：原密码必填、新密码合规、两次一致；成功后换用新令牌并关闭 */
async function savePassword() {
  if (saving.value) return
  error.value = ''
  if (!pw.oldPassword) {
    error.value = '请输入原密码'
    return
  }
  const problem = passwordProblem(pw.newPassword)
  if (problem) {
    error.value = problem
    return
  }
  if (pw.newPassword !== pw.confirm) {
    error.value = '两次输入的新密码不一致'
    return
  }
  saving.value = true
  try {
    await auth.changePassword(pw.oldPassword, pw.newPassword)
    ElMessage.success('密码已修改')
    visible.value = false
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = problemMessage(e)
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <el-dialog v-model="visible" title="个人设置" width="min(480px, 92vw)" :close-on-click-modal="false" append-to-body>
    <el-alert v-if="error" class="error" type="error" :title="error" show-icon :closable="false" />
    <el-tabs v-model="tab">
      <el-tab-pane label="基本信息" name="profile">
        <el-form label-width="80px" @submit.prevent="saveProfile">
          <el-form-item label="用户名">{{ auth.user?.username }}</el-form-item>
          <el-form-item label="角色">{{ auth.user ? roleLabel(auth.user.role) : '' }}</el-form-item>
          <el-form-item v-if="auth.user?.tenantName" label="租户">{{ auth.user.tenantName }}</el-form-item>
          <el-form-item label="显示名称">
            <el-input v-model="displayName" maxlength="64" placeholder="不填则显示用户名" />
          </el-form-item>
          <el-form-item>
            <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="saveProfile">保存</el-button>
          </el-form-item>
        </el-form>
      </el-tab-pane>
      <el-tab-pane label="修改密码" name="password">
        <el-alert
          v-if="isImpersonating"
          type="info"
          show-icon
          :closable="false"
          title="代入租户期间不能修改密码，请先退出租户后修改自己的密码。"
        />
        <el-form v-else label-width="96px" @submit.prevent="savePassword">
          <el-form-item label="原密码">
            <el-input v-model="pw.oldPassword" type="password" show-password autocomplete="current-password" />
          </el-form-item>
          <el-form-item label="新密码">
            <el-input v-model="pw.newPassword" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="8～64 位，同时包含字母和数字" />
          </el-form-item>
          <el-form-item label="确认新密码">
            <el-input v-model="pw.confirm" type="password" show-password autocomplete="new-password" />
          </el-form-item>
          <el-form-item>
            <el-button type="primary" :loading="saving" @click="savePassword">修改密码</el-button>
          </el-form-item>
        </el-form>
      </el-tab-pane>
    </el-tabs>
  </el-dialog>
</template>

<style scoped>
.error {
  margin-bottom: var(--cd-space-3);
}
</style>
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the user management page**

Create `conduit-ui/src/views/UsersView.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
/**
 * 用户管理（租户管理员与代入的平台管理员）：筛选栏（关键字、角色、状态）+ 服务端分页（useTable，默认每页 10 条）；
 * 角色、状态与「待改密码」用标签。操作列：编辑（显示名称与角色）、重置密码、禁用/启用、删除；自己那一行不显示禁用与删除
 * （代入的平台管理员不是本租户用户，没有「自己」那一行）。新建用户须设置初始密码，对方首次登录时修改。
 * 首次加载显示骨架屏，加载失败时可重试；「最后一个管理员」等保护规则由服务端返回 409 并提示。
 */
import { onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { Plus } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { userApi } from '@/api/users'
import { problemMessage } from '@/api/errors'
import type { AccountStatus, TenantRole, TenantUserView } from '@/api/types'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { usePermission } from '@/composables/usePermission'
import { useTable } from '@/composables/useTable'
import { formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format'
import { PAGE_SIZES } from '@/utils/pagination'
import { roleLabel, statusLabel } from '@/utils/permissions'
import PageHeader from '@/components/PageHeader.vue'
import FilterBar from '@/components/FilterBar.vue'
import UserFormDialog from '@/components/UserFormDialog.vue'
import ResetPasswordDialog from '@/components/ResetPasswordDialog.vue'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const { isImpersonating } = usePermission()

const filters = reactive({ keyword: '', role: '' as '' | TenantRole, status: '' as '' | AccountStatus })
/** 已应用的筛选（点查询/回车时才从输入框复制），翻页与刷新都以它为准 */
const applied = ref({ ...filters })

const { items, total, page, size, loading, loaded, failed, load, search: searchTable, onSizeChange, onPageChange } =
  useTable<TenantUserView>((p, s) =>
    userApi.list({
      keyword: applied.value.keyword.trim() || undefined,
      role: applied.value.role || undefined,
      status: applied.value.status || undefined,
      page: p,
      size: s,
    }),
  )

const formVisible = ref(false)
const editing = ref<TenantUserView | null>(null)
const resetVisible = ref(false)
const resetting = ref<TenantUserView | null>(null)
const busy = ref(new Set<string>())

/** 应用筛选并回到第 1 页 */
function search() {
  applied.value = { ...filters }
  void searchTable()
}

/** 清空筛选并回到第 1 页 */
function reset() {
  filters.keyword = ''
  filters.role = ''
  filters.status = ''
  search()
}

/** 是否是当前登录用户自己（代入的平台管理员没有「自己」） */
function isSelf(row: TenantUserView): boolean {
  return !isImpersonating.value && row.username === auth.user?.username
}

/** 打开新建对话框 */
function openCreate() {
  editing.value = null
  formVisible.value = true
}

/** 打开编辑对话框 */
function openEdit(row: TenantUserView) {
  editing.value = row
  formVisible.value = true
}

/** 新建或编辑成功：提示并刷新当前页 */
function onSaved() {
  ElMessage.success('已保存')
  void load()
}

/** 打开重置密码对话框 */
function openReset(row: TenantUserView) {
  resetting.value = row
  resetVisible.value = true
}

/** 重置所选用户的密码（由 ResetPasswordDialog 调用；失败时异常交给对话框显示），成功后刷新以显示「待改密码」 */
async function savePassword(password: string) {
  if (!resetting.value) return
  await userApi.resetPassword(resetting.value.username, password)
  await load()
}

/** 禁用（先确认）或启用用户，成功后刷新当前页 */
async function toggleStatus(row: TenantUserView) {
  const disabling = row.status === 'ACTIVE'
  if (disabling) {
    try {
      await ElMessageBox.confirm(
        `禁用后，用户「${row.username}」将无法登录，已登录的会话最多 15 分钟后失效。确定禁用？`,
        '禁用用户',
        { type: 'warning' },
      )
    } catch {
      return
    }
  }
  busy.value.add(row.username)
  try {
    await (disabling ? userApi.disable(row.username) : userApi.enable(row.username))
    ElMessage.success(disabling ? '已禁用' : '已启用')
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  } finally {
    busy.value.delete(row.username)
  }
}

/** 删除用户（先确认），成功后刷新（删掉当前页最后一条时 useTable 自动退到上一页） */
async function remove(row: TenantUserView) {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`确定删除用户「${row.username}」？`, '删除用户', { type: 'warning' })
  } catch {
    return
  }
  try {
    await userApi.remove(row.username)
    ElMessage.success('已删除')
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error(problemMessage(e))
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  void load()
})
</script>

<template>
  <div>
    <PageHeader title="用户" subtitle="本租户的账号：租户管理员可读写并管理用户，只读用户只能查看。">
      <template #actions>
        <el-button type="primary" :icon="Plus" @click="openCreate">新建用户</el-button>
      </template>
    </PageHeader>

    <div class="cd-panel">
      <FilterBar @search="search" @reset="reset">
        <el-form-item label="关键字">
          <el-input v-model="filters.keyword" placeholder="用户名或显示名称" clearable />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="角色">
          <el-select v-model="filters.role" placeholder="全部" clearable class="filter-select">
            <el-option label="租户管理员" value="TENANT_ADMIN" />
            <el-option label="只读用户" value="TENANT_VIEWER" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="状态">
          <el-select v-model="filters.status" placeholder="全部" clearable class="filter-select">
            <el-option label="启用" value="ACTIVE" />
            <el-option label="禁用" value="DISABLED" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
      </FilterBar>

      <el-skeleton v-if="!loaded" :rows="5" animated />
      <template v-else>
        <el-table v-loading="loading" :data="items">
          <el-table-column label="用户名" min-width="180">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              {{ row.username }}
              <div v-if="row.displayName" class="cd-muted">{{ row.displayName }}</div>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="角色" width="120">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <el-tag :type="row.role === 'TENANT_ADMIN' ? 'primary' : 'info'" size="small">{{ roleLabel(row.role) }}</el-tag>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="状态" width="160">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <el-tag :type="row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'success' : 'info'" size="small">{{ statusLabel(row.status) }}</el-tag>
              <el-tag v-if="row.mustChangePassword" type="warning" size="small" class="flag">待改密码</el-tag>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="创建时间" width="180">
            <template #default="{ row }">{{ formatDateTime(row.createdAt) }}</template>
          </el-table-column>
          <el-table-column label="操作" width="260">
            <template #default="{ row }">
              <el-button link type="primary" @click="openEdit(row)">编辑</el-button>
              <el-button link type="primary" @click="openReset(row)">重置密码</el-button>
              <template v-if="!isSelf(row)">
                <el-button
                  link
                  :type="row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'warning' : 'success'"
                  :loading="busy.has(row.username)"
                  @click="toggleStatus(row)"
                >
                  {{ row.status === 'ACTIVE' ? '禁用' : '启用' }}
                </el-button>
                <el-button link type="danger" @click="remove(row)">删除</el-button>
              </template>
            </template>
          </el-table-column>
          <template #empty>
            <el-empty v-if="failed" description="加载失败，点击重试">
              <el-button type="primary" @click="load">重试</el-button>
            </el-empty>
            <el-empty v-else-if="applied.keyword || applied.role || applied.status" description="没有符合条件的用户">
              <el-button @click="reset">重置筛选</el-button>
            </el-empty>
            <el-empty v-else description="还没有其他用户，新建一个只读用户试试">
              <el-button type="primary" @click="openCreate">新建用户</el-button>
            </el-empty>
          </template>
        </el-table>

        <el-pagination
          v-model:current-page="page"
          v-model:page-size="size"
          class="cd-pager"
          layout="total, sizes, prev, pager, next"
          :page-sizes="PAGE_SIZES"
          :total="total"
          @current-change="onPageChange"
          @size-change="onSizeChange"
        />
      </template>
    </div>

    <UserFormDialog
      v-model="formVisible"
      :user="editing"
      :editing-self="editing !== null && isSelf(editing)"
      @saved="onSaved"
    />
    <ResetPasswordDialog v-model="resetVisible" :username="resetting?.username ?? ''" :save="savePassword" />
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.filter-select {
  width: 130px;
}
.flag {
  margin-left: var(--cd-space-1);
}
</style>
```

## Task 9: Docs, configuration, CLAUDE.md sync, full verification and acceptance

Runs on `main` after `v0.2c-backend` (Tasks 1–5) and `v0.2c-frontend` (Tasks 6–8) are merged.

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`
- Modify: `deploy/.env.example`, `deploy/docker-compose.yml`
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md` (§1.1, §1.2, §2.2, §3, §4)
- Modify: `docs/backlog-v0.2.md`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2c-tenants-users-design.md` (status line, acceptance record)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; the owner's IDE-run stack (`Conduit (local all)`: server on `http://localhost:8080`, UI on `http://localhost:5173`, local logs in `logs/`).
- Produces: synced docs and configuration, the full test run, and the spec §7 acceptance record.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- No deployment (CLAUDE.md §3). The owner restarts the IDE-run server once after the merge so Flyway `V3` runs and `sysadmin` is created; that requires `conduit.bootstrap.platform-admin-password` in the untracked `application-local.yml` (or `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD` in the run configuration).
- Acceptance uses `curl` from Git Bash. Request bodies with Chinese text go through `--data-binary @-` heredocs so they stay UTF-8. MQTT acceptance (spec §7 item 5) calls the EMQX auth callback `/internal/mqtt/auth` directly with the internal token: it is exactly the decision EMQX asks for when a device connects.
- Acceptance creates tenant `acceptance-east` (「验收租户（华东）」) with users `east-admin` and `east-viewer`, one product and one device; the last step deletes all of them through the API. Temporary passwords (`Init-pass-2026`, `East-pass-2026`, `View-pass-2026`) exist only for this local, deleted tenant.
- Browser-only behavior (pages, dialogs, bar, menus, dark mode) is listed as owner visual checks.
- Deferred items go to `docs/backlog-v0.2.md`, as spec §8 requires.

- [ ] **Step 1: Update the README**

3. In the code-structure tree, replace

```
│   ├── auth/                        登录、JWT、Spring Security 配置

```

```
│   ├── tenant/                      租户上下文与租户隔离、租户编码规则
│   ├── user/                        租户用户管理（/api/v1/users）
│   ├── platform/                    平台管理员接口：平台概况、租户管理、进入租户
```

and replace `密钥生成与哈希；启动时创建默认租户和管理员` with `密钥生成与哈希；启动时创建默认租户、租户管理员与平台管理员`.
4. Replace `用 `.env` 中的 `CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME` / `CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD` 登录。` with `用 `.env` 中的 `CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME` / `CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD` 登录（默认租户的租户管理员）；管理租户用 `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_USERNAME` / `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD` 登录（平台管理员，见「账号与角色」）。`
5. Insert this section directly before `## 设备接入`:

```markdown
## 账号与角色

| 角色 | 所属 | 能力 |
|---|---|---|
| 平台管理员 `PLATFORM_ADMIN` | 不属于任何租户 | 平台首页、租户管理（新建含首个管理员、改名、停用/启用、删除）、查看租户用户、重置租户管理员密码、进入租户 |
| 租户管理员 `TENANT_ADMIN` | 一个租户 | 本租户产品、物模型、设备的全部读写，用户管理 |
| 只读用户 `TENANT_VIEWER` | 一个租户 | 本租户只读 |

- **初始账号**：启动时若没有默认租户 `default` 与 `CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME`（默认 `admin`）就创建它们（`admin` 是默认租户的租户管理员）；若库中没有任何平台管理员，用 `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_USERNAME`（默认 `sysadmin`）与 `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD` 创建一个，密码为空则不创建（server 打一条 WARN，照常启动）。已有账号不会被改动。
- **规则**：用户名 3～32 位，字母开头，只能含字母、数字、`_`、`-`、`.`，全平台唯一（登录不选租户）；密码 8～64 位，同时含字母和数字；租户编码 `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$`，创建后不可改。
- **首次登录改密码**：新建的用户、新租户的首个管理员、被重置密码的用户登录后必须先修改密码，界面弹出不可关闭的对话框；此前其余接口返回 403 `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED`。
- **进入租户**：平台管理员在「租户」页点「进入」，确认后以租户管理员身份操作该租户，顶栏下方显示「正在以平台管理员身份操作租户：XX」与「退出」；代入期间每个写请求在 server 日志记一行 INFO（`Impersonated write by platform admin …`）。代入期间不能修改密码。
- **停用租户**：其用户登录与续期被拒，其设备 MQTT 连接被拒，已连接设备的上报进入死信 `TENANT_DISABLED`；默认租户不能停用或删除；删除租户前须先删掉其产品与设备，用户随之删除。
- **生效时间**：禁用用户、停用租户、修改角色在对方下次续期时生效，最长 15 分钟（access token 有效期）。
```


CLAUDE.md's maintenance rule requires every upgrade to sync its decision-type conventions and append to §4. In `CLAUDE.md`:

1. §1.1 table: replace `REST API、多租户 |` with `REST API、多租户与用户（平台管理员 / 租户管理员 / 只读用户、进入租户） |`; in the `conduit-ui` row replace `、首页总览、暗色模式 |` with `、首页总览、暗色模式、按角色的菜单与写操作、平台首页与租户/用户管理 |`.
2. §1.2: replace the bullet that starts with `- **多租户**：` with these two bullets:

```markdown
- **多租户与用户**（V0.2-C）：所有业务表带 `tenant_id`，MyBatis-Plus 租户拦截器按令牌中的租户自动过滤；`tenant`、`app_user` 不过滤，按租户查用户必须显式带 `tenant_id`；跨租户查询（按 productKey 找产品、设备身份、平台统计 `PlatformStatsMapper`）必须显式标注 `@InterceptorIgnore` 并只在内部或平台接口使用，按租户检查数据用 `TenantContext.callAs`。角色 `PLATFORM_ADMIN`（不属于任何租户，`tenant_id` 为空，数据库约束保证）/ `TENANT_ADMIN` / `TENANT_VIEWER`；用户名 3～32 位字母开头（字母、数字、`_-.`）全平台唯一，密码 8～64 位含字母和数字（BCrypt），租户编码 `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$` 不可改；用户与租户状态 `ACTIVE`/`DISABLED`。启动时没有平台管理员则按 `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_USERNAME`（默认 `sysadmin`）/ `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD` 创建，密码为空只 WARN。默认租户 `default` 不能停用或删除；删除租户需无产品与设备，用户随之删除。租户用户保护：不能删除、禁用自己或改自己的角色，本租户最后一个启用的 `TENANT_ADMIN` 不能被降级、禁用或删除（租户行锁内检查）。停用租户时清除该租户设备的身份缓存 `conduit:auth:*`；设备身份带租户状态，MQTT 鉴权拒绝停用租户的设备，已连接设备的上报进死信 `TENANT_DISABLED`。
- **权限与会话**（V0.2-C）：JWT（access 15 分钟、refresh 7 天 HttpOnly Cookie）声明 `sub` 用户 id、`tid`（平台管理员没有）、`role`（有效角色）、`typ`，代入时加 `imp`（平台管理员 id），需改密码时加 `mcp`。登录拒绝禁用用户与停用租户；续期重读数据库（禁用、停用、角色变化最长 15 分钟生效），代入令牌续期时目标租户停用或删除则退回平台管理员自己的令牌。`SecurityConfig` 按路径与方法统一判断：`/platform/**` 仅平台管理员（代入令牌的有效角色是 `TENANT_ADMIN`，被拒），`/users/**` 租户管理员，其余 `/api/v1/**` GET 为租户管理员与只读用户、写为租户管理员，`/auth/**`、`/me/**` 所有登录用户；前端只隐藏入口。需改密码时（新用户、首个管理员、被重置密码）除 `GET /auth/me`、`PUT /me/password`、登录/刷新/登出外一律 403 `code=PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED`，`PUT /me/password` 返回新令牌。进入租户 `POST /platform/tenants/{code}/enter`（停用租户 409）与退出 `POST /auth/exit-tenant` 各记 INFO，代入期间每个写请求由 `ImpersonationLogFilter` 记一行 INFO（不记请求体），代入期间不能改密码。
```

3. §1.2, in the `**首页总览与计数**` bullet, replace ``，`UserView` 带 `tenantName`。`` with ``，`UserView` 带显示名称、租户编码与名称、是否需改密码与代入者（`impersonatedBy`）。``
4. §1.2, in the `**前端框架与外观**` bullet, replace `分组侧栏（概览 / 设备接入 / 系统管理，V0.2-C 的用户、租户为禁用项）可折叠` with `分组侧栏按身份给出（平台管理员：概览 / 系统管理→租户；租户管理员与代入者：概览 / 设备接入 / 系统管理→用户；只读用户：概览 / 设备接入）、可折叠`, replace `租户名称与用户菜单；` with `租户名称与用户菜单（显示名称、角色、个人设置、退出登录），代入时顶栏下方固定显示代入提示条与「退出」，需改密码时弹出不可关闭的改密码对话框；路由 `meta.scope`（tenant / platform）与 `meta.requires`（manageUsers）由守卫与 AppLayout 统一处理，身份不匹配时改去租户管理或首页；`/home` 对未代入的平台管理员显示平台首页；`, and leave the rest of the bullet unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 4: Record the deferred items in the backlog**

Append to `docs/backlog-v0.2.md`:

```markdown

## V0.2-C 多租户与用户管理遗留（2026-10-16）

- 登录失败锁定（暴力破解防护）。
- 停用租户或设备时调用 EMQX 接口踢下线已连接的设备；目前连接保留，其上报进入 `TENANT_DISABLED` / `DEVICE_DISABLED` 死信。
- 代入操作留痕并入 V1.0 审计表；目前只有 server INFO 日志（`Impersonated write by platform admin …`）。
- 没有令牌吊销：禁用用户、停用租户、修改角色、重置密码对已签发的 access token 最长 15 分钟后才生效。
- 平台统计（平台首页、租户列表）每次读出全平台用户、设备与 Redis 在线状态，开销 O(设备数)；规模变大时与首页统计一起改为增量计数，租户列表只统计本页租户。
- 停用/启用租户时清除设备身份缓存是尽力而为；Redis 不可用导致清除失败时，最长 5 分钟（缓存 TTL）后鉴权才按新状态判断。
- 平台管理员只能在「库中没有平台管理员」时由环境变量创建；没有管理多个平台管理员的界面。
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Acceptance against the local stack (spec §7)**

Precondition (owner): `application-local.yml` of conduit-server contains `conduit.bootstrap.platform-admin-password`; restart `Conduit (local all)` from the merged `main`; the server log shows `Created platform admin 'sysadmin'` (or a platform admin already exists). In Git Bash, from the repository root, with `ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `SYSADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN` exported by the operator:

```bash
export API=http://localhost:8080
json() { sed -E "s/.*\"$1\":\"([^\"]*)\".*/\1/"; }
login() { curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"username\":\"$1\",\"password\":\"$2\"}"; }
code() { curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' "$@"; }
SYS=$(login sysadmin "$SYSADMIN_PASSWORD" | json accessToken)
```

Access tokens live 15 minutes; if a later call returns 401, repeat the `login` line that produced the token.

Item 1 — create the tenant and its admin; the admin must change the password first:

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- <<'EOF'
{"code":"acceptance-east","name":"验收租户（华东）","admin":{"username":"east-admin","displayName":"华东管理员","password":"Init-pass-2026"}}
EOF
EAST_INIT=$(login east-admin Init-pass-2026 | json accessToken)
code "$API/api/v1/products" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST_INIT"
curl -s "$API/api/v1/products" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST_INIT" | grep -o PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED
EAST=$(curl -s -X PUT "$API/api/v1/me/password" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST_INIT" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"oldPassword":"Init-pass-2026","newPassword":"East-pass-2026"}' | json accessToken)
```

Expected: the create response contains `"code":"acceptance-east"`, `"status":"ACTIVE"`, `"adminCount":1`; then `403`; then `PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED`; `EAST` is a non-empty token.

Item 2 — the new tenant's data is invisible to `admin`, and the demo data is invisible to the new tenant:

```bash
PK=$(curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/products" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- <<'EOF' | json productKey
{"name":"验收电表（华东）"}
EOF
)
DEV=$(curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/devices" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- <<EOF
{"productKey":"$PK","name":"华东1号楼 电表01"}
EOF
)
DK=$(echo "$DEV" | json deviceKey); SECRET=$(echo "$DEV" | json deviceSecret)
ADMIN=$(login admin "$ADMIN_PASSWORD" | json accessToken)
curl -s "$API/api/v1/products?size=100" -H "Authorization: Bearer $ADMIN" | grep -c "$PK"
code "$API/api/v1/devices/$DK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $ADMIN"
curl -s "$API/api/v1/products" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST" | grep -o '"total":[0-9]*'
curl -s "$API/api/v1/devices" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST" | grep -o '"total":[0-9]*'
```

Expected: `0`; `404`; `"total":1`; `"total":1` (only the acceptance product and device, no demo data).

Item 3 — a read-only user can read but not write:

```bash
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/users" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- <<'EOF'
{"username":"east-viewer","displayName":"华东运维 张工","role":"TENANT_VIEWER","password":"Init-pass-2026"}
EOF
VIEW_INIT=$(login east-viewer Init-pass-2026 | json accessToken)
VIEW=$(curl -s -X PUT "$API/api/v1/me/password" -H "Authorization: Bearer $VIEW_INIT" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"oldPassword":"Init-pass-2026","newPassword":"View-pass-2026"}' | json accessToken)
code "$API/api/v1/devices/$DK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $VIEW"
code -X POST "$API/api/v1/products" -H "Authorization: Bearer $VIEW" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"name":"x"}'
code "$API/api/v1/users" -H "Authorization: Bearer $VIEW"
```

Expected: the create response has `"role":"TENANT_VIEWER"` and `"mustChangePassword":true`; then `200`; `403`; `403`.

Item 4 — `sysadmin` enters the tenant, reads and writes, the write is logged, and exiting restores the platform identity:

```bash
IMP=$(curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants/acceptance-east/enter" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS" | json accessToken)
curl -s "$API/api/v1/auth/me" -H "Authorization: Bearer $IMP"
code "$API/api/v1/devices/$DK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $IMP"
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -X PUT "$API/api/v1/products/$PK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $IMP" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- <<'EOF'
{"name":"验收电表（华东，代入改名）","description":null}
EOF
grep "Platform admin sysadmin entered tenant acceptance-east" logs/conduit-server.log | tail -1
grep "Impersonated write by platform admin sysadmin in tenant acceptance-east: PUT /api/v1/products/$PK -> 200" logs/conduit-server.log | tail -1
SYS=$(curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant" -H "Authorization: Bearer $IMP" | json accessToken)
code "$API/api/v1/platform/overview" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS"
code "$API/api/v1/platform/overview" -H "Authorization: Bearer $IMP"
```

Expected: `/auth/me` shows `"role":"TENANT_ADMIN"`, `"tenantCode":"acceptance-east"`, `"impersonatedBy":"sysadmin"`; `200`; `200`; both `grep` lines print one log line; `200` with the new platform token; `403` with the impersonation token.

Item 5 — disabling the tenant blocks its users and devices; enabling restores them:

```bash
mqtt() { curl -s -X POST "$API/internal/mqtt/auth" -H "X-Conduit-Internal-Token: $CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"clientid\":\"$DK\",\"username\":\"$DK\",\"password\":\"$SECRET\"}"; echo; }
mqtt
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants/acceptance-east/disable" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS" | grep -o '"status":"[A-Z]*"'
code -X POST "$API/api/v1/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"username":"east-admin","password":"East-pass-2026"}'
mqtt
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants/acceptance-east/enable" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS" | grep -o '"status":"[A-Z]*"'
code -X POST "$API/api/v1/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"username":"east-admin","password":"East-pass-2026"}'
mqtt
```

Expected: `{"result":"allow","is_superuser":false}`; `"status":"DISABLED"`; `401`; `{"result":"deny"}`; `"status":"ACTIVE"`; `200`; `{"result":"allow","is_superuser":false}`.

Item 6 — delete the device and the product, then the tenant (this is also the cleanup):

```bash
EAST=$(login east-admin East-pass-2026 | json accessToken)
code -X DELETE "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants/acceptance-east" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS"
code -X DELETE "$API/api/v1/devices/$DK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST"
code -X DELETE "$API/api/v1/products/$PK" -H "Authorization: Bearer $EAST"
code -X DELETE "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants/acceptance-east" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS"
curl -s "$API/api/v1/platform/tenants?keyword=acceptance" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SYS" | grep -o '"total":[0-9]*'
code -X POST "$API/api/v1/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"username":"east-viewer","password":"View-pass-2026"}'
```

Expected: `409` (tenant still has data); `204`; `204`; `204`; `"total":0`; `401` (the tenant's users are gone).

- [ ] **Step 10: Owner visual checks (browser, `http://localhost:5173`)**

Ask the owner to check, in light and dark themes:

1. Log in as `sysadmin`: the sidebar shows only 概览 and 系统管理 → 租户; `/home` is the platform home with the four tiles (租户、用户、设备、在线率) and 「设备最多的租户」; clicking a tenant row opens 租户 filtered by that code; typing `/products` in the address bar lands on 租户.
2. 租户 page: 「新建租户」 dialog shows the 「租户信息」 and 「首个管理员」 sections and rejects an invalid code in the dialog; the default tenant row has no 停用/删除; 「用户」 opens the drawer and 「重置密码」 works on a `TENANT_ADMIN` row.
3. 「进入」 shows 「将以租户管理员权限操作租户 XX 的数据，所有修改都会被记录」; after confirming, the warning bar 「正在以平台管理员身份操作租户：XX」 stays under the top bar on every page, the menu shows 设备接入 and 用户, and 「退出」 returns to 租户.
4. Log in as a newly created tenant admin: the 「请修改密码」 dialog cannot be closed (no ×, Esc and outside clicks do nothing) and disappears after a successful change.
5. As a tenant admin, 用户 page: the own row has no 禁用/删除; editing oneself locks the role; the user menu shows the display name and the role and opens 个人设置 (display name and password tabs).
6. As a read-only user: no 用户 menu; no 新建/编辑/删除/导入/启用/禁用/重置密钥 buttons on 产品, 产品详情 (基本信息 form disabled, 导出 still available), 设备, 设备详情; `/users` and `/tenants` in the address bar land on 首页.
7. Narrow window (< 820 px): the impersonation bar and the dialogs stay usable without horizontal scrolling.

- §2.1 roles → Task 1 (`Roles`, DB constraint), Task 2 (effective role in the JWT, matrix).
- §2.2 migration V3 (nullable `tenant_id` with the role/tenant check, role and status constraints, `ADMIN` → `TENANT_ADMIN`, `display_name`, `must_change_password`, `updated_at`, tenant `updated_at`, code rule, users removed with the tenant) → Task 1 (`SchemaV3IT`, incl. migrating a V2 schema) and Task 4 (explicit user delete).
- §2.3 initialization (default tenant + `admin` as `TENANT_ADMIN`; platform admin from env, empty password only WARNs) → Task 1 (`AdminBootstrapTest`, `AdminBootstrapIT`).

The only execution-time content is the acceptance record in Task 9 Step 11, written for the passing case with an explicit instruction for a failing item.

  - `AuthPrincipal(long, Long, String, String, Long, boolean)` and its 4-argument constructor are used identically by `JwtService`, `SessionService`, `AuthController`, `MeService`, `TenantAdminService.enter`, `ImpersonationLogFilter`, `UserService.isSelf` and the tests (`JwtServiceTest`, `PermissionMatrixIT`, `SessionIT`, `MeApiIT`, `UserApiIT`, `OverviewApiIT`).
  - `SessionService.requireActive/principalOf/impersonation/issue/clearedRefreshCookie/userView` and `REFRESH_COOKIE` match their callers in `AuthController`, `MeService`, `MeController`, `PlatformController`, `TenantAdminService`, `SessionIT`, `UserApiIT`.
  - `AuthDtos.UserView(username, displayName, role, tenantCode, tenantName, mustChangePassword, impersonatedBy)` matches the TS `UserView`; `TokenResponse` is unchanged.
  - `UserDtos.UserItem` fields match TS `TenantUserView`; `PlatformDtos.TenantView`, `PlatformOverview`, `TenantStat`, `CreateTenantRequest`/`AdminAccount`, `UpdateTenantRequest` match TS `TenantView`, `PlatformOverview`, `TenantStat`, `CreateTenantRequest`/`TenantAdminAccount`, `UpdateTenantRequest`; `PasswordRequest {password}` matches `resetPassword`/`resetAdminPassword` bodies.

  - `DeviceIdentity(…, productKey, tenantStatus)` (7 arguments) is used by `findIdentity` (column `tenant_status`), `DeviceIdentityTest`, `PipelineProcessorTest`, `DeviceApiIT`, `DeviceIdentityLookupIT`, `TenantIsolationIT`; `PlatformTenantApiIT` writes a raw cache value instead, so Task 4 does not depend on Task 5's constructor.

- Frontend:
  - `platformApi`, `userApi`, `meApi`, `authApi.exitTenant` method names and arguments match the store, the pages and every `vi.mock` factory.
  - Store actions `setSession`, `enterTenant`, `exitTenant`, `changePassword`, `updateProfile` and state `passwordChangeRequired` / getter `needsPasswordChange` match `client.ts`, `ImpersonationBar`, `ForcePasswordDialog`, `PersonalSettingsDialog`, `TenantsView` and the specs.
  - `permissionsOf`/`Permissions` match `usePermission`, `menuGroupsFor`, `accessRedirect`; `roleLabel`, `statusLabel`, `displayNameOf` match AppLayout, drawers and pages.
  - Route names `tenants`, `users` and meta keys match `ROUTE_META`, `createTestRouter`, `accessRedirect`, menus and router `index.ts`.
  - `ResetPasswordDialog` props (`modelValue`, `username`, `save`) match `TenantUsersDrawer` and `UsersView`; `UserFormDialog` (`user`, `editingSelf`, `saved`) matches `UsersView`; `DeviceInfoCard.canEdit` matches `DeviceDetailView`; AppLayout `userName`, `roleText`, `profileVisible`, `onCommand('profile' | 'logout')` match `AppLayout.spec.ts`.

**Resolved ambiguities:**
- `PUT /me/password` returns a fresh `TokenResponse` (and refresh cookie) so the forced-change gate lifts at once; the must-change flag rides in the JWT (`mcp`), so the gate needs no per-request database read.
- In an impersonation token, `sub`/`usr` are the platform admin's own, `tid` is the target, `role` is `TENANT_ADMIN`, `imp` repeats the platform admin id; `isPlatformAdmin` in the UI means "not impersonating".
- Login reports 「用户名或密码错误」 for unknown users and wrong passwords; distinct 401 messages for disabled accounts and disabled tenants appear only after the password matched.
- A new password equal to the old one is rejected (400).
- Password length cap 64; display name and tenant name cap 64; names are trimmed and blank display names become `null`.
- `userCount` on the platform overview excludes platform admins; `adminCount` includes disabled tenant admins; `topTenants` may include tenants with zero devices; ties sort by code.
- Lists: users sorted by role then username (both the paged list and the platform's per-tenant list); tenants newest first; enum filters are case-insensitive and validated (400).
- Disable/enable of tenants and users return the updated item and are idempotent; enabling a tenant also evicts identity caches.
- The default tenant also cannot be *enabled* (spec §4.1 lists both endpoints as 409 for it).
- The pipeline checks the tenant before the device, so a device that is disabled in a disabled tenant dead-letters as `TENANT_DISABLED`.
- Identities cached before the upgrade (no `tenantStatus`) count as active until they expire.
- The users list (`GET /users`) keyword matches username or display name; the tenant list keyword matches name or code.
- Self rules apply only to non-impersonating actors; resetting one's own password through `/users` is allowed.
- `/home` serves both homes through `HomeEntry`; after entering a tenant the UI goes to `/home`; after exiting it goes to 租户.
